# OUTLINE_appMNOPQ — Appendices M (OPE), N (precise Collins subtraction), O (LCOPT and the Glauber cancellation), P (formal blueprint), Q (verified errata)

**Status:** binding spec-v2 outline of the five new appendices of 《QCD 因子化定理：严格证明、全阶论证与适用边界》, drafted 2026-10-10 by the spec-v2 new-appendices drafter. It is merged into `FINAL_OUTLINE_v2.md` (appendix part and registry). The global assumption register is the companion file `spec_v2/ASSUMPTIONS.md`; every `asm:` label used below is defined there (owner, exact content, tag, status, users, upgrade path).

**Inputs read in full for this file.**
- `CONTEXT.md`, `REQUIREMENTS.md`, `PROGRESS.md`.
- `refdoc/REFDOC_ANALYSIS.md`: §1.6–§1.8 (App C, D, E of the reference), §2 (errors ERR-S/M/m, OUR-1…8), §3.b–§3.d (adoption tables C1–C20, D1–D26, E1–E29), §5.2–§5.3 (S5–S8, register), §5.5 (S13–S45, chapter changes that the appendices must serve), §5.6 (S46–S49), §5.7–§5.11 (S50–S63).
- Reader notes `refdoc/notes/R5.md` (App C), `R6.md` (App D), `R7.md` (App E part 1), `R8.md` (App E part 2), all in full; R3 §3 E1 (exact eikonal box).
- Scripts and logs: `refdoc/work_SYN/triangle_remainder.py` (+log), `refdoc/work_R6/check_*.py` (+logs), `refdoc/work_R3/scripts/eikonal_box*.py` (+logs), the lists of checks in `work_R5`, `work_R7`, `work_R8`.
- `spec_v2/WRITER_SPEC_v2.md` (§1.1 structure, §3 notation rows 1–48, §4 statements/tags/qualifiers/assumptions/`\uses`/grade propagation/error types/grade dictionary, §5 labels, §6.3 promised proofs, §6.5 new results, §6.12 PENDING-VERIFY protocol, §6.13 App P/Q, §8–§10), `FIGURES_INDEX_v2.md` (§3 S12–S17, §4.5–§4.7 N18–N22, §4.9 App P template), `VERIFIED_FORMULAS_v2.md` (VF-REF-1…19, VF-DIS-13, VF-GLA-2/3/5/9, VF-WL-1/12, VF-TMD-1/8/9, pending register), `checks/check_unit_v2.py` (registry format), `checks/refs_additions_table.md`.
- `spec/FINAL_OUTLINE.md` (v1 format, labels, §0.3–§0.8, App J/K/L entries, registry), `research/c_dis/DERIVATION.md` §11 (OPE check), `research/c_glauber/DERIVATION.md` §1.5–§1.7, `research/critic/REVIEW.md` §3.1/§12 (MA0–MA4).
- Frontier reports `frontier/lamet/LAMET.md` §2.5, §3.2, §4.1–§4.5, §7.1–§7.2, §10.1–§10.2; `frontier/yzliu/YZLIU.md` §3.A3–§3.A6, §3.B1, §3.E1; `frontier/others/OTHERS.md` §1.1–§1.5, §2.1–§2.7, §8.
- `verify/CLAIMS.md` (index; E01–E36, N01–N29; the R-claims are not literature errata).

**What this file contains.**
- §00 global rules for App M–Q (binding in addition to WRITER_SPEC_v2);
- App M, App N, App O: purpose, depends-on, box 「本附录证明了什么／没有证明什么」, sections with page budgets, every canonical statement (type, label, Chinese title, tag + qualifier, premise sentence, non-perturbative/partonic line, precise statement, `\uses`, proof plan with full/sketch, literature locations, checks, VF IDs, error type), the proof-delivery tables for chapter-owned statements, the surpass ledger (what the reference had, what we fix or extend), [PENDING-VERIFY] slots for the running proof extensions X1 (Glauber), X2 (subtraction), X3 (OPE) with decision rules, figures and tables;
- App P: the complete generator specification (parsing, data model, the longtable of all statements, register and used-by tables, per-part TikZ dependency graphs, consistency checks, outputs, budget control) and the written introduction;
- App Q: structure, inclusion rules, table schema, the pending candidate list with planned row IDs;
- §08 registry of the labels owned by App M–Q (format of `check_unit_v2.py`).

## 00. Global rules for the appendices M–Q

### 00.1 Units, files, budgets (lead-author decision)

| unit | key | file | 中文标题 (binding) | pp | writer |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| M | `appM` | `appM_ope.tex` | OPE：微扰证明、无质量 QCD、非微扰条件定理 | 24 | one writer (recommended: the Ch7 `dis` writer, after Ch7 statements are frozen) |
| N | `appN` | `appN_subtraction.tex` | Collins 减除法的精确形式 | 24 | one writer (recommended: also writes Ch9 `sec:method-subtraction`) — written **first** among M/N/O |
| O | `appO` | `appO_glauber.tex` | 光锥排序微扰论与 Drell–Yan 的 Glauber 相消 | 32 | one writer after the App N labels of §N.3–§N.5 are frozen (recommended: also writes Ch12 `sec:dy-glauber`, `sec:dy-model`, `sec:dy-theorem`) |
| P | `appP` | `appP_blueprint.tex` | 形式化蓝图：假设登记表、依赖图与状态表——由 \uses 数据自动生成 + 导言 | 10 | introduction by a writer (≈2 pp, last); tables and figures generated by the integrator |
| Q | `appQ` | `appQ_errata.tex` | 经核实的文献勘误 | 4 | written after `verify/` completes |

Labels: appendix `\label{app:appM}` … `\label{app:appQ}`; sections `sec:appM-…`; statements `def|asm|lem|prop|thm|cor|ex|rem:appM-…`; tables `tab:appM-…`; figures as in FIGURES_INDEX_v2. Chapter-owned statements whose proofs are delivered here keep their chapter labels (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §5.3, §6.3); they are listed in the **proof-delivery table** of each appendix and are **not** in the registry of the appendix.

### 00.2 How each entry is written (in addition to WRITER_SPEC_v2 §4, §6)

1. **Entry format.** `**类型 `label` — 中文标题** [tag, qualifier]` · premise sentence (R_cond) · partonic/non-perturbative line · *Statement* (formulas in the binding conventions) · `\uses{…}` · *Proof plan* (full / sketch; numbered steps; literature with location; checks with script paths) · *VF* IDs · *Error type* of the conclusion (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §4.7) · *Surpass/correction* note (ERR IDs refer to `refdoc/REFDOC_ANALYSIS.md` §2; never printed).
2. **Partonic line.** Every App N and App O statement concerns partonic integrands order by order; its hypotheses end with 「（本命题只涉及部分子态。）」. App M §M.2–§M.7 likewise (massive models, partonic Green functions). App M §M.8 is hadronic: its R_cond statements list `asm:intro-*` in the premise sentence and end with 「非微扰地位：见前提（MA0、MA3、MA4）。」.
3. **Qualifier sentences** are the verbatim strings of WRITER_SPEC_v2 §4.3; the R_cond premise sentence 「前提：\cref{asm:…}、…」 lists every assumption and every non-[R] statement used, and the same labels are in `\uses`.
4. **`\uses` order inside one appendix file.** A label may be used only after it is defined in the same file (check C4). The order of the entries below respects this; writers keep it. Appendix statements may use statements of any chapter and of other appendices (no cycles; App P checks).
5. **Chapter statements proved here.** The appendix section starts with 「\cref{…} 的证明」; the statement is not repeated as an environment. The restatement given in this outline (marked *Target*) is for the appendix writer only; if it differs from the chapter's wording in `FINAL_OUTLINE_v2`, the chapter wording governs and the writer reports the difference. The chapter statement's `\uses` must contain the appendix lemmas its proof uses; the column "appendix lemmas for the chapter `\uses`" of the proof-delivery tables gives them (for the merge into the core outline).
6. **The reference document** is never cited, quoted or named (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §6.10). Internal identifiers such as "Thm D.10", "Ass. E.33" appear in this outline only for traceability. Every adopted construction is re-derived and attributed to the primary literature (Zimmermann; Lowenstein–Zimmermann; Joglekar–Lee; Weinberg; Collins 2011; Collins–Soper–Sterman; Bodwin; Aybat–Sterman; Diehl–Gaunt–Ostermeier–Plößl–Schäfer; Kogut–Soper; Veltman; Rothstein–Stewart; …) or presented as our derivation.
7. **Error types.** Each status table (`tab:appM-status`, `tab:appN-status`, `tab:appO-checklist`) has the column 「误差类型」 with E-S / E-P / E-F / E-N / E-W, and each `proofsketch` step that rests on power counting names its type.
8. **Box** 「本附录证明了什么／没有证明什么」 right after the first paragraph of App M, N, O (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §6.6 frame; labels only). Drafts are given below.
9. **Conventions.** Light-cone vectors $n_A=(1^+,0^-,\mathbf 0_T)$, $n_B=(0^+,1^-,\mathbf 0_T)$ (row 2), never $n,\bar n$ (the reference's $n,\bar n$ are our $n_A,n_B$). Projections $\pi_+(k)=(n_B\cdot k)\,n_A=(k^+,0,\mathbf 0_T)$, $\pi_-(k)=(n_A\cdot k)\,n_B=(0,k^-,\mathbf 0_T)$. Glauber direction $e_G=n_B-n_A$ (row 38, VF-REF-7). Eikonal rules VF-WL-1 (row 28): past-pointing $W[0,-\infty n]$: $\prod_j\frac{i}{n\cdot(k_1+\dots+k_j)+i0}$; future-pointing $W[\infty n,0]$: $\prod_j\frac{-i}{n\cdot(k_j+\dots+k_m)-i0}$. Coupling $D_\mu=\partial_\mu-igA_\mu^at^a$, quark–gluon vertex $+ig\gamma^\mu t^a$ (the reference's "$-igJ^{+a}$" is a sign slip, ERR-m26).

### 00.3 Tag policy for the technical assumptions (consistency with the grade-propagation rule)

The grade-propagation rule (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §4.6) makes every process theorem carry at most the weakest tag of the technical assumptions it uses. The binding master-table tags of the collinear and TMD process theorems are [A]. Therefore (details and justification per entry in `ASSUMPTIONS.md` §00.3):
- a technical assumption that encodes a step of an **accepted all-order argument** of the literature (CSS, Bodwin, Collins 2011, Sterman) which has not been written in the precise form required here is tagged `\tagA`, and its 「地位」 field says "open in the precise form stated; covered at the level of the standard all-order argument by …" — this applies to all `asm:appM-*`, all `asm:appN-*` except `asm:appN-values` (convention, no tag) and `asm:appN-ir-regulator` (`\tagC`), and all `asm:appO-*`;
- an assumption that is **not** covered by any accepted argument is tagged `\tagC` (or `\tagP` if partly proven) and is never a premise of an [A] statement: `asm:intro-*` (global non-perturbative, excluded from propagation for ordinary statements), `asm:appN-ir-regulator`, the hadronic LaMET hypotheses, the non-perturbative analyticity hypotheses of the Liu programme;
- the register of REFDOC §5.3 is refined accordingly: `(Z2)` is split off as `asm:appN-ir-regulator`; the non-local BRST classification assumed by Collins 2011 §11.3.2 becomes the explicit entry `asm:appN-brst-nonlocal` (`\tagA`, decision flagged for the lead author because frontier/others §8.3 assesses the general multilocal case as [C]).

### 00.4 [PENDING-VERIFY] slots for the proof extensions X1, X2, X3

The verification workflow (`verify/`) runs three proof extensions whose outcome may strengthen statements of App M/N/O: **X1 Glauber** (App O), **X2 subtraction** (App N), **X3 OPE** (App M); results are expected in `verify/extend/X1-glauber/`, `verify/extend/X2-subtraction/`, `verify/extend/X3-ope/`. Each slot below has a reserved label, a planned statement and tag, the evidence required, and a decision rule:
- **PASS** = two independent confirmations recorded in `verify/` (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §6.5 item 2) and, for a novelty claim, a literature check. Then the patch inserts the statement with its planned tag, updates the dependent status lines and the assumption register (status 「已解除」/「部分解除」), and adds the rows to `tab:appX-status` and to App P.
- **PARTIAL** = the extension holds in a restricted setting: the statement is inserted with the restriction in its first sentence and tag [P] (or [R, R_PT] for an explicit fixed-order statement whose first sentence states the order).
- **FAIL / not delivered** = the slot label is deleted; the fallback text (given per slot) stays; no claim is printed.
Until the patch, writers follow WRITER_SPEC_v2 §6.12 (comment `% PENDING-VERIFY: <label> <X-item>`, no marker printed, tag not stronger than planned).

A slip in our own analysis is corrected here as well: REFDOC S48 states that in `thm:appO-main` "every soft attachment to $J_A$ ($J_B$) is replaced by a past-pointing Wilson line along $n_B$ ($n_A$)". Correct (eikonal factor $n_A^\mu/(n_A\cdot k)$ for a soft gluon on an $A$-collinear line): soft attachments to $J_A$ ($J_B$) give past-pointing lines along $n_A$ ($n_B$) **in the soft factor**, and $A$-collinear longitudinal gluons attached to $H$ or $J_B$ give the past-pointing line along $n_B$ **in the collinear factor of $A$** (c_glauber §1.5 Steps 1–2). The statements below use the correct form.

# 附录 (new appendices M–Q)

## Appendix M — OPE：微扰证明、无质量 QCD、非微扰条件定理 / The OPE: perturbative proof, massless QCD, non-perturbative conditional theorems

`\label{app:appM}` · `appM_ope.tex` · **24 pp** · appendix (proofs)

**Purpose.** Split "the OPE has been proven" into checkable statements and prove what can be proven: (I) the perturbative-algebraic OPE in massive Euclidean models (BPHZ, normal products, one chain-expansion identity generating both the Zimmermann identities and the OPE identity) — unconditional; (II) the perturbative-analytic part (remainder, uniqueness, scaling of reduced coefficients, coordinate-space remainder) — conditional on one precisely stated remainder estimate that is probably dischargeable from the literature (X3a); (III) massless QCD — what is cited, why forward on-shell kinematics are exceptional, gauge-invariant operators via BRST, twist and chirality; (IV) the fixed-order all-spin sum for partons; (V) the non-perturbative layer: from Wightman-type axioms a $Q$-uniform finite-subtraction dispersion relation, from the local OPE on a shrinking Euclidean segment plus positivity the fixed-$n$ Taylor coefficients, hence the non-perturbative moment theorem (all even $n\ge n_0$; $n<n_0$ iff the growth condition). App M delivers the proofs announced in Ch7 `dis`.

**Depends on.** Ch2 `qcd`: `def:prelim-qcd`, `def:prelim-brst`, `lem:prelim-brst-nilpotent`, `thm:prelim-slavnov-taylor`, `thm:prelim-composite-renorm`, `def:prelim-msbar`, `prop:prelim-rg`. Ch6 `ee`: `thm:ee-euclid-ir` (IR power counting at non-exceptional Euclidean momenta), `thm:ee-ope`, `conj:ee-np-ope`. Ch7 `dis`: `def:dis-kinematics`, `def:dis-compton`, `def:dis-twist`, `prop:dis-compton-analytic`, `prop:dis-dispersion`, `lem:dis-euclid-segment`, `lem:dis-tree-ope`, `prop:dis-wilson-partons` and the statements proved here. Ch8 `pdf`: `def:pdf-lightray`, `thm:pdf-moments-local`. App J: `prop:appJ-local-operators`, `rem:appJ-nonlocal`. App K: `lem:appK-hausdorff`, `thm:appK-even-moments`, `lem:appK-entire`, `lem:appK-markov`, `rem:appK-counterexamples`. App A: `sec:appA-plus` (weak convergence). Pointers (no dependency): Ch21 `prop:power-rearrangement`, `rem:power-practical-ope`, `conj:power-renormalon-ht`.

**Box 「本附录证明了什么／没有证明什么」 (draft; labels only).**

### M.1 `sec:appM-overview` 概览 (1.5 pp)

- What is proved and what is assumed (the box); the three layers: algebraic (§M.2–M.3, unconditional), analytic-perturbative (§M.4–M.7, one remainder input in the massive model; cited theorems for massless QCD; one forward input), non-perturbative (§M.8, the `asm:intro-*` register entries).
- `fig:appM-dependency` (FIGURES_INDEX_v2 S12): nodes `asm:intro-wightman`, `asm:intro-composite`, `asm:intro-np-ope`, `asm:intro-af-coeff`, `asm:intro-growth`, `asm:appM-remainder`, `asm:appM-brst-ope`, `asm:appM-forward` (orange dashed, top row); statements `prop:dis-dispersion-np`, `lem:dis-euclid-segment`, `lem:dis-ope-segment`, `thm:dis-fixed-n`, `prop:dis-low-moments`, `thm:dis-moments-np`, `cor:dis-weak-xspace`, `prop:dis-ope-euclid`, `prop:dis-ope-gi`, `prop:dis-ope-forward`, `thm:dis-moments`; edges from the `\uses` lists; R_cond edges dashed. (Generated from the same data as App P; the App M writer may draw it by hand from the `\uses` lists of this outline.)
- Reading guide: which Ch7 statement is proved where (the proof-delivery table as a printed table `tab:appM-delivery` is optional; the box suffices).
- The precise meaning of "the OPE holds" used in Ch7 (formulate once here, referenced by §M.4 and §M.8): **(O1)** Euclidean, direction-uniform asymptotic expansion with remainder $O(|q|^{1-N}\ln^k|q|)$ for $T^{\mu\nu}$; **(O2)** only gauge-invariant operators in physical matrix elements, twist 2 = $O_q^{(n)},O_g^{(n)}$; **(O3)** fixed-$n$ Taylor coefficients $t_n=4\sum_iC^{(n)}_{2,i}A^{(n)}_i+O(Q^{-2}\ln^kQ)$; **(O4)** RG; **(O5)** $t_n=4\int_{[0,1]}x^{n-2}F_2\,dx$ for even $n\ge n_0$, $F_2dx\ge0$. One moment needs one Taylor coefficient, not the all-spin sum; for hadrons (O1) informs only on $|\omega|\le\sqrt3M/Q$ (`lem:dis-euclid-segment`). Partonic structure functions are not positive measures, so the positivity route of §M.8 is non-perturbative only; its perturbative analogue is `prop:dis-ope-forward`.

### M.2 `sec:appM-bphz` BPHZ 与正规乘积 (2.5 pp)

Model of §M.2–§M.4: four-dimensional Euclidean theories of massive scalars (dimension 1) and Dirac fermions (dimension 3/2) with dimension-4 monomial interactions; composite vertices of dimension $d_O$ with $\delta_v$ derivatives; everything IR finite. Massless and gauge fields: §M.5–§M.6.

- **定义 `def:appM-bphz` — 图、表观发散度、森林与 $R$ 运算** (no tag). Graph $\Gamma$ (vertices $V$, internal lines $L$, external legs with incoming $p_j$); subgraph $\gamma$ = set of lines with their endpoints; $E(\gamma)$ = lines of $\Gamma$ attached to vertices of $\gamma$ but not in $\gamma$ (counted twice if both ends lie on $\gamma$) plus external legs at vertices of $\gamma$; renormalization part = 1PI subgraph; non-overlapping = disjoint or nested; $\Gamma/\gamma$ = contraction of each component of $\gamma$ to a point. Propagator $\Delta_\ell(k)=P_\ell(k)/(k^2+m_\ell^2)$, $\deg P_\ell=2d_\ell-2$. Superficial degree $\omega(\gamma)=4L(\gamma)+\sum_{\ell\in\gamma}(2d_\ell-4)+\sum_{v\in V(\gamma)}\delta_v$ (additive extension to disconnected $\gamma$: sum over components). Taylor operator $t^\delta_\gamma I_\gamma(k_\gamma,p_\gamma)=\sum_{|\alpha|\le\delta}\frac{p_\gamma^\alpha}{\alpha!}\partial^\alpha_{p_\gamma}I_\gamma(k_\gamma,0)$, $t^\delta\equiv0$ for $\delta<0$; Zimmermann's canonical (Kirchhoff) routing of external momenta; forest formula $R_\Gamma=\sum_F\prod^{\curvearrowleft}_{\gamma\in F}(-t^{\delta(\gamma)}_\gamma)I_\Gamma$ (smaller subgraphs act first) with subtraction degrees $\delta(\gamma)\ge\omega(\gamma)$; Bogoliubov recursion $R_\Gamma=(1-t_\Gamma)\bar R_\Gamma$. `% uses: none`.
- **引理 `lem:appM-degree` — 表观发散度的外线公式** (数学). $\omega(\gamma)=4-\sum_{e\in E(\gamma)}d_e+\sum_{v\in V(\gamma)}(d_v-4)$; with one composite vertex $\omega=d_O-\sum_{E}d_e$, with two composite vertices $A,B$: $\omega=d_A+d_B-4-\sum_Ed_e$. `\uses{def:appM-bphz}`. Proof: full ($L=I-V+1$, $\sum_v(d_v-4)=2\sum_\ell d_\ell+\sum_Ed_e+\sum_v\delta_v-4V$).
- **定理 `thm:appM-zimmermann` — Zimmermann 收敛定理（顶点可加的超减除）** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$ …）（本定理只涉及部分子态。）Positive masses, real Euclidean external momenta, general line factors $P_\ell(k)/(k^2+m_\ell^2)^{n_\ell}$ and polynomial vertices ($\omega$ by actual degrees). If the subtraction degrees are **vertex-additive**, $\delta(\gamma)=\omega(\gamma)+\sum_{v\in V(\gamma)}c_v$ with $c_v\in\mathbb Z_{\ge0}$ fixed per vertex, then $\int\prod d^4k\,R_\Gamma$ converges absolutely, is smooth in the external momenta, and the subtractions are equivalent to local counterterm insertions. $c_v\equiv0$ is standard BPHZ. `\uses{def:appM-bphz}`. Proof: cited — `Zimmermann1969` (convergence of the forest formula in momentum space; canonical momenta), `Zimmermann1970` (oversubtractions, normal products), `Hepp1966`, `BogoliubovParasiuk1957` (locations: 「位置待核实」 for theorem numbers). Error type: exact (absolute convergence).
- **例 `ex:appM-nonadditive` — 非顶点可加的超减除会失败**. $\varphi^4$, two-loop four-point $\Gamma$ with a one-loop bubble $\gamma$ at a vertex, $\omega(\gamma)=\omega(\Gamma)=0$; choose $\delta(\gamma)=2$, $\delta(\Gamma)=0$ (so $\delta\ge\omega$ but not vertex-additive): $-t^2_\gamma$ produces $c_2P_\gamma^2$, which inserted into $\Gamma/\gamma$ has degree 2, not cured by $t^0_\Gamma$; both two-loop topologies (s-channel chain: logarithmic; "eye": quadratic divergence). Moral: the condition of `thm:appM-zimmermann` cannot be weakened to $\delta\ge\omega$. `\uses{thm:appM-zimmermann, lem:appM-degree}`. (Re-derived; reader check R5.)
- **定理 `thm:appM-weinberg` — Weinberg 定理（Hahn–Zimmermann 形式）** (数学，经典，引用). For $f(P,K)=\prod_jF_j(\ell_j(P,K))$, $F_j=P_j(\ell)/(\ell^2+m_j^2)^{n_j}$, $m_j>0$, with $\alpha_{S'}(F_j)=r_j-2n_j$ if $\ell_j|_{S'}\not\equiv0$ else 0, $D_{S'}(f)=\dim(S'\cap\ker\pi)+\sum_j\alpha_{S'}(F_j)$: (a) if $D_S<0$ for all non-zero $S\subset\ker\pi$, $I(P)=\int dK\,f$ converges absolutely; (b) with $\alpha(L)=\max\{D_{S'}:\pi(S')=L\}$, $|I(\eta\hat\ell+c)|\le K\eta^{\alpha(L)}(\ln\eta)^\beta$ for $\eta\ge2$, uniformly for $\hat\ell$ in compacta of the unit sphere of $L$ and $c$ in compacta. `% uses: none`. Proof: cited (`Weinberg1960`, `Hahn:1968HZ`).
- **推论 `cor:appM-weinberg-degree` — 渐近指数与子图发散度** (数学). $\alpha(L)\le\max\{\omega(\gamma):\gamma\text{ carries }L\}$, where $\gamma$ may be disconnected and $\omega$ is the additive extension (corrects the gap ERR-m24/R5 E7). `\uses{thm:appM-weinberg, def:appM-bphz}`. Proof: full ($S'_\gamma=\{P\in L,\ \ell_j=0\ \forall j\notin\gamma\}$, $\dim(S'_\gamma\cap\ker\pi)=4L(\gamma)$).
- **注 `rem:appM-forest-asymptotics` — 森林公式不能逐项用 Weinberg 定理** (no tag). Single forest terms are not integrable and grow faster than the sum; they recombine sector by sector (Zimmermann's sector method). Asymptotic expansions of zero-momentum BPHZ integrals: Bergère–de Calan–Malbouisson（文献待补：Commun. Math. Phys. 62 (1978) 137）; large-momentum and small-mass expansions `Smirnov:1990rz`, `Smirnov2002`; flow-equation bounds `Hollands:2011gf`. The frozen-$q$ form needed in §M.4 is not covered verbatim (hence `asm:appM-remainder`, slot X3a).
- Figure: `fig:appM-forest` (FIGURES_INDEX_v2 N18; owner App M).

### M.3 `sec:appM-chain` 链展开与 OPE 恒等式 (4 pp)

- **定义 `def:appM-normal-product` — 正规乘积与冻结 $q$ 的双局域正规乘积** (no tag). (i) For a monomial $O$ ($d_O$) and $a\ge d_O$, $a-d_O\in\mathbb Z$: $N_a[O]$ is the forest formula with $\delta_a(\gamma)=a-\sum_{E(\gamma)}d_e$ for $\gamma\ni V_O$ and $\delta=\omega$ otherwise ($\delta_a-\omega=a-d_O$, i.e. $c_{V_O}=a-d_O$ in `thm:appM-zimmermann`); $N[O]\equiv N_{d_O}[O]$. (ii) For monomials $A,B$, $D\equiv d_A+d_B-4$; auxiliary line $\ell_x$ ($V_A$–$V_B$, "propagator" $e^{-iq\cdot x}$), $\Gamma^\circ=\Gamma\cup\{\ell_x\}$; $\gamma$ is an **AB part** iff $V_A,V_B\in\gamma$ and $\gamma\cup\{\ell_x\}$ is 1PI in $\Gamma^\circ$; $\omega(\gamma)=D-\sum_{E(\gamma)}d_e$. (iii) Two subtractions, $a\in D+\mathbb Z_{\ge0}$: the **T-product** $\tilde G^T_{AB}(q;p)$ = BPHZ with $q$ external (1PI AB parts $t^{\omega}_{(p,q)}$, non-1PI AB parts unsubtracted); the **frozen-$q$ bilocal normal product** $\tilde N_a(q;p)$ of $N_a[A(x)B(0)]$: every AB part (including non-1PI) with $t^{(a)}_\gamma\equiv t^{\delta_a(\gamma)}_p$, $\delta_a=a-\sum_Ed_e$ (Taylor in $p$ only, $q$ frozen), other renormalization parts with $t^\omega$. `\uses{def:appM-bphz}`.
- **引理 `lem:appM-contraction` — 收缩与桥** (数学). For $\gamma^*\subset\sigma$, $\gamma^*$ connected: $\sigma$ connected ⇔ $\sigma/\gamma^*$ connected; a line of $\sigma\setminus\gamma^*$ is a bridge of $\sigma$ ⇔ it is a bridge of $\sigma/\gamma^*$; a bridge of $\sigma$ lying in $\gamma^*$ is a bridge of $\gamma^*$. Hence for 1PI $\gamma^*$: $\sigma$ 1PI ⇔ $\sigma/\gamma^*$ 1PI. `\uses{def:appM-bphz}`. Proof: full.
- **引理 `lem:appM-chain-expansion` — 链展开恒等式** (数学). Marked vertices $M\ne\emptyset$; family $\mathcal S$ of connected subgraphs containing $M$; *admissible* = in $\mathcal S$, or a renormalization part not containing all of $M$; forests = sets of pairwise non-overlapping admissible subgraphs. (i) The $\mathcal S$-elements of a forest form a chain. (ii) For operators $\tau_\gamma,\tau'_\gamma$ on $\mathcal S$ and $t^{\delta(\gamma)}$ elsewhere,
  $R^\tau_\Gamma=R^{\tau'}_\Gamma+\sum_{\gamma^*\in\mathcal S}\sum_{F\ni\gamma^*}\Big[\prod^{\curvearrowleft}_{\gamma\in F,\gamma\not\subseteq\gamma^*}(-\tau_\gamma)\Big](\tau'_{\gamma^*}-\tau_{\gamma^*})\Big[\prod^{\curvearrowleft}_{\gamma\in F,\gamma\subsetneq\gamma^*}(-\tau'_\gamma)\Big]I_\Gamma .$
  (iii) Under the contraction condition **(Ctr)** — for $\sigma\supsetneq\gamma^*$: $\sigma$ admissible ⇔ $\sigma/\gamma^*$ is a renormalization part of $\Gamma/\gamma^*$ containing the image vertex $v_*$ — each forest $F\ni\gamma^*$ splits uniquely as $F_{\rm in}\sqcup\{\gamma^*\}\sqcup F_{\rm out}$ with $F_{\rm out}$ in bijection with the forests of $\Gamma/\gamma^*$, and the $\gamma^*$-term of (ii) is the forest sum of $\Gamma/\gamma^*$ (with $\tau$ on subgraphs containing $v_*$) with vertex factor $v_{\gamma^*}=(\tau'_{\gamma^*}-\tau_{\gamma^*})\bar R^{\tau'}_{\gamma^*}$, $\bar R^{\tau'}_{\gamma^*}=\sum_{F_{\rm in}}\prod_{\gamma\in F_{\rm in}}(-\tau'_\gamma)I_{\gamma^*}$. For $M=\{V_O\}$, (Ctr) follows from `lem:appM-contraction`. `\uses{def:appM-bphz, lem:appM-contraction}`. Proof: full (write $-\tau=-\tau'+(\tau'-\tau)$ along the chain and group by the innermost difference; routing compatibility under contraction, `Zimmermann1969`). Renamed hypothesis: the reference's "(H)" is (Ctr) here (no clash with (H1)–(H5) of `thm:method-template`).
- **定理 `thm:appM-zimmermann-identity` — Zimmermann 恒等式** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$ …）（本定理只涉及部分子态。）For $d_O\le a<a'$ in $d_O+\mathbb Z$: $N_a[O]=N_{a'}[O]+\sum_{O':\,a<d_{O'}\le a'}u_{O'}N_{a'}[O']$, $O'$ local monomials, $u_{O'}=m^{d_O-d_{O'}}\hat u_{O'}$ finite (zero-momentum Taylor coefficients of 1PI vertex functions of $N_a[O]$). `\uses{def:appM-normal-product, lem:appM-chain-expansion, thm:appM-zimmermann}`. Proof: full — `lem:appM-chain-expansion` with $\tau=t^{\delta_{a'}}$, $\tau'=t^{\delta_a}$; $v_{\gamma^*}=-\sum_{\delta_a<|\alpha|\le\delta_{a'}}\frac{p^\alpha}{\alpha!}\partial^\alpha_p\bar R^{(a)}_{\gamma^*}|_{p=0}$ converges (no overall subtraction since $\delta_a(\gamma^*)-|\alpha|<0$; vertex-additivity survives differentiation, $\partial_pt^\delta=t^{\delta-1}\partial_p$); $p^\alpha$ times the legs $E(\gamma^*)$ is the vertex of $O'$ with $d_{O'}=\sum_Ed_e+|\alpha|\in(a,a']$. Literature: `Zimmermann1970`, `Zimmermann:1972te`.
- **推论 `cor:appM-reduction` — 化到最小正规乘积** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$ …）$N_a[O]=\sum_{d_O\le d_{O'}\le a}z_{OO'}N[O']$, $z_{OO}=1$. `\uses{thm:appM-zimmermann-identity}`. Proof: full (induction on $a-d_O$).
- **例 `ex:appM-callan-symanzik` — $\varphi^4$ 中的 Zimmermann 恒等式**. $O=\varphi^2$, $a=2\to a'=4$: $m^2N_2[\varphi^2]=m^2N_4[\varphi^2]+\hat u_{\varphi^4}N_4[\varphi^4]+\hat u_{\varphi\partial^2\varphi}N_4[\varphi\partial^2\varphi]+\hat u_{(\partial\varphi)^2}N_4[\partial_\mu\varphi\partial^\mu\varphi]$ ($\varphi^3$ excluded by $\mathbb Z_2$, $\varphi\partial\varphi$ by Lorentz invariance); the coefficient of $N_{a'}[O]$ is exactly 1 because $|\alpha|>\delta_a(\gamma^*)=a-d_O\ge0$. The identity behind the Callan–Symanzik equation (`Callan:1970yg`, `Symanzik:1970rt`). `\uses{thm:appM-zimmermann-identity}`.
- **引理 `lem:appM-routing` — 冻结 $q$ 时外动量分配的相容性** (数学). (New auxiliary lemma; repairs ERR-m24/R5 E8.) In $\Gamma^\circ$, treat $q$ as an external momentum injected at $V_A$ and extracted at $V_B$ that is never Taylor-expanded. The canonical (minimal-norm Kirchhoff) routing of $\Gamma^\circ$ with respect to all external momenta $(p_j,q)$ is linear in $(p,q)$; for every AB part or renormalization part $\gamma$, the restriction of the canonical flow of $\Gamma^\circ$ to $\gamma$ differs from the canonical flow of $\gamma$ (with its induced external momenta) by a loop flow of $\gamma$; hence the operators $t^{(a)}_\gamma=t^{\delta_a(\gamma)}_p$ act on the $p$-components only, commute with the contraction of AB subparts, and (Ctr) holds for $M=\{V_A,V_B\}$, $\mathcal S$ = AB parts; after contracting an AB part $\gamma^*$, $q$ no longer flows in $\Gamma/\gamma^*$ ($\ell_x$ becomes a self-loop). `\uses{def:appM-normal-product, lem:appM-contraction}`. Proof: full (adapt Zimmermann's canonical-momenta lemma, `Zimmermann1969` §2–3 (位置待核实), with $q$ in the list of external momenta; the restriction property holds for any external vector because the minimal-norm flow is an orthogonal projection).
- **引理 `lem:appM-integrability` — 冻结 $q$ 正规乘积的良定义与链分解** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$ …）With $\tau=t^{(a)}$ on AB parts, $\tau'=t^\omega_{(p,q)}$ on 1PI AB parts and $\tau'=0$ on non-1PI AB parts, $\vartheta$ = indicator of 1PI: (i) $R^T_\Gamma=R^{(a)}_\Gamma+\sum_{\gamma^*\in\mathcal S}(\text{forest sum of }\Gamma/\gamma^*\text{ with }t^{(a)}\text{ on subgraphs}\ni v_*)[v_{\gamma^*}]$, $v_{\gamma^*}=(t^{(a)}_{\gamma^*}-\tau'_{\gamma^*})\bar R^T_{\gamma^*}$; (ii) $v_{\gamma^*}=\sum_{|\alpha|\le\delta_a(\gamma^*)}\frac{p_{\gamma^*}^\alpha}{\alpha!}\hat c^{\gamma^*}_\alpha(k_{\gamma^*};q)$ with $\hat c^{\gamma^*}_\alpha=\big(1-\vartheta(\gamma^*)t_q^{\omega(\gamma^*)-|\alpha|}\big)\partial^\alpha_p\bar R^T_{\gamma^*}(k;q,p)|_{p=0}$ absolutely integrable and $c^{\gamma^*}_\alpha(q)=\int\hat c^{\gamma^*}_\alpha$ smooth (1PI $\gamma^*$: full BPHZ integrand with external momentum $q$ only, degree $\omega-|\alpha|$; non-1PI: product of fully subtracted 1PI blobs and bridge propagators); (iii) every term of (i) is absolutely integrable (factorization $\sum_\alpha\hat c_\alpha(k_{\gamma^*};q)\,g_\alpha(k';p)$ with $g_\alpha$ an oversubtracted BPHZ integrand of $\Gamma/\gamma^*$, $c_{v_*}=a-\sum_{E(\gamma^*)}d_e-|\alpha|$; Tonelli); hence $R^{(a)}_\Gamma$ is integrable and $\tilde N_a$ smooth. `\uses{def:appM-normal-product, lem:appM-chain-expansion, lem:appM-routing, thm:appM-zimmermann}`. Proof: full.
- **引理 `lem:appM-contact` — 接触项** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$ …）Replacing all AB-part subtractions in the T-product by $t^{\omega+c}_{(p,q)}$ ($c\ge0$) changes $\tilde G^T_{AB}$ only by (polynomials in $q$) × (Green functions of local insertions); hence $G_{AB}(x;p)$ at $x\neq0$ is independent of the choice and equals the Green function of $T\,A(x)B(0)$. `\uses{def:appM-normal-product, thm:appM-zimmermann}`. Proof: full.
- **定理 `thm:appM-ope-identity` — 微扰论中的 OPE 恒等式（有质量模型）** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$ …）（本定理只涉及部分子态。）At each order and for $a\in D+\mathbb Z_{\ge0}$, exactly
  $\tilde G^T_{AB}(q;p)=\sum_{d_O\le a}\tilde C_O(q)\,\tilde G_{N[O]}(p)+\tilde N_a(q;p)$,
  with coefficients $\tilde C_O$ smooth in $q$, independent of $p$ and of the external fields, given by absolutely convergent "hard" integrals; $\tilde C_O=\sum_{d_{O'}\le d_O}\tilde C^{(a)}_{O'}z_{O'O}$ (reduction of the unreduced coefficients $\tilde C^{(a)}_O=\sum_{\gamma^*}c^{\gamma^*}_\alpha$); all $d_O\in D+\mathbb Z$. `\uses{lem:appM-integrability, lem:appM-contact, cor:appM-reduction, lem:appM-routing}`. Proof: full — integrate (i) of `lem:appM-integrability`, expand by (ii); $p^\alpha E(\gamma^*)$ is the vertex of $O_{E,\alpha}$ with $d\le a$, and the subtraction degrees in $\Gamma/\gamma^*$ are those of $N_a[O_{E,\alpha}]$; reduce by `cor:appM-reduction`. Literature: the coordinate-space short-distance expansion `Zimmermann1973`, `Wilson1969`, `Wilson:1972ee`; momentum space `Anikin:1978tj`. Error type: exact identity.
- **例 `ex:appM-tree` — 树图 OPE 就是 Taylor 展开**. $A=B=\varphi^2/2$ ($D=0$), single line $V_A$–$V_B$ (non-1PI AB part, $\omega=-2$, $\delta_a=a-2$): $\tilde N_a=\Delta(q+p_1)-\sum_{|\alpha|\le a-2}\frac{p_1^\alpha}{\alpha!}\partial^\alpha\Delta(q)=O(|p_1|^{a-1}|q|^{-a-1})$, matching $D-1-a$; in $x$ space the remainder is $\Delta(x)[e^{ip_1\cdot x}-\sum_{j\le a-2}(ip_1\cdot x)^j/j!]$ — the Taylor expansion of $\varphi(x)$ with operators $\varphi\partial^\alpha\varphi$ (Fourier identities $\int e^{-iqx}\Delta(q+p)=e^{ipx}\Delta(x)$, $\int e^{-iqx}\partial^\alpha\Delta=(ix)^\alpha\Delta(x)$). `\uses{thm:appM-ope-identity}`.
- **注 `rem:appM-mechanism` — 指数 $D-1-a$ 的启发式来源** (no tag). In a sector where the lines of $\gamma_h$ carry momenta $\sim\eta$, the component $\gamma_0\ni V_A$ contains $V_B$ and has no bridge in $\gamma_0\cup\ell_x$, hence is an AB part (rigorous graph theory); its Taylor remainder scales as $\eta^{\omega(\gamma_0)-\delta_a(\gamma_0)-1}=\eta^{D-1-a}$ independently of $\gamma_0$; frozen $q$ does not worsen high-energy behaviour. Not a proof (straddling subgraphs, nested scales) — hence `asm:appM-remainder`.
- Figure: `fig:appM-handbag-ab` (FIGURES_INDEX_v2 S13; owner App M): handbag as an AB part (hard, $C^{(n)}$) vs as a reduced graph (collinear, $A^{(n)}$); the overlap is separated by the oversubtraction (massive case); in massless QCD IR counterterms are needed (§M.5).

### M.4 `sec:appM-remainder` 余项、唯一性与标度 (3.5 pp)

- **假设 `asm:appM-remainder` — 冻结 $q$ 双局域正规乘积的动量空间余项估计** [\tagA]. Full text in `ASSUMPTIONS.md`. Content: let $\mathcal H_{a,\Gamma}$ be the span of the functions obtained from $\tilde N_{a,\Gamma}$ by finitely many $\partial/\partial p_j^\nu$, multiplications by $p_{j\mu}$ and $m\partial_m$; there is an integer $k$ such that every $h\in\mathcal H_{a,\Gamma}$ obeys $|h(q;p)|\le K_h|q|^{D-1-a}(\ln|q|)^k$ for $|q|\ge2$, uniformly in $q/|q|$ and for $p$ in compact sets (massive Euclidean model, each order). `\uses{def:appM-normal-product}`.
- **引理 `lem:appM-derivative-bounds` — 由齐次性、O(4) 与 Lagrange 恒等式得到的导数界** (数学). If every $h\in\mathcal H$ obeys $|h|\le K_h|q|^\varrho\ln^k|q|$, then $|\partial^\beta_qh|\le K'_\beta|q|^{\varrho-|\beta|}\ln^k|q|$. `\uses{def:appM-normal-product}`. Proof: full — (1) homogeneity of integrands and of zero-momentum Taylor operators under $(q,p,m,k)\to\lambda(\cdot)$ and the Euler relation $q\cdot\partial_qh=D_hh-\sum p\partial_ph-\sum m\partial_mh\in\mathcal H$; (2) $O(4)$ covariance $L^{(q)}_{\mu\nu}h=-\sum_jL^{(p_j)}_{\mu\nu}h+\Sigma_{\mu\nu}h\in\mathcal H$; (3) Lagrange identity $|q|^2|v|^2=|q\cdot v|^2+\sum_{\mu<\nu}|q_\mu v_\nu-q_\nu v_\mu|^2$ (real $q$, complex $v$) ⇒ $|q||\partial_qh|\le|q\cdot\partial h|+\sum|L^{(q)}h|$; (4) induction on $|\beta|$.
- **引理 `lem:appM-uniqueness` — OPE 系数的唯一性** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$ …）(a) *Order by order (massive):* if $[\sum_{d_O\le a}\Delta_O(q)\tilde G_{N[O]}(p)]^{(r)}=O(|q|^\varrho\ln^k|q|)$ for all external field types, $p$ in compact sets and all orders $r$, then each $\Delta^{(r)}_O=O(|q|^\varrho\ln^k|q|)$. (b) *Exact (massless, log-polynomial structure):* if two coefficient families $\tilde C,\tilde C'$ both give a remainder $O(|q|^{D-1-a}\ln^k|q|)$ at Euclidean non-exceptional momenta and both are $|q|^{D-d_O}$ times polynomials in $\ln(q^2/\mu^2)$ (with $\hat q$-dependent coefficients), they coincide at each order. `\uses{def:appM-normal-product}`. Proof: full — (a) induction on $r$ using injectivity of the tree vertices of a basis of local monomials; (b) $\Delta_O=e^{(D-d_O)L}P(L)$ with $|P(L)|\le Ke^{-(a+1-d_O)L}L^k\to0$ for a polynomial $P$ ⇒ $P\equiv0$.
- **定理 `thm:appM-scaling` — 约化系数的标度与余项（有质量模型）** [R, R_cond]. （$R_{\rm cond}$ …）前提：\cref{asm:appM-remainder}。（本定理只涉及部分子态。）(ii) $|\partial^\beta_q\tilde N_a|\le K_\beta|q|^{D-1-a-|\beta|}\ln^k|q|$. (iii) Other $p$-independent coefficients with remainder $O(|q|^{D-1-a}\ln^k)$ differ from $\tilde C_O$ by $O(|q|^{D-1-a}\ln^k)$; coefficients at levels $a<a'$ agree up to that order, and level-$a'$ coefficients with $a<d_O\le a'$ are themselves $O(|q|^{D-1-a}\ln^k)$. (iv) The **reduced** coefficients scale: $|\tilde C_O(q)|\le K|q|^{D-d_O}\ln^k|q|$ for $d_O\ge D+1$ (false for the unreduced $\tilde C^{(a)}_O$: `ex:appM-unreduced`). `\uses{asm:appM-remainder, thm:appM-ope-identity, lem:appM-derivative-bounds, lem:appM-uniqueness}`. Proof: full — (ii) assumption + `lem:appM-derivative-bounds` with $\varrho=D-1-a$; (iii) `lem:appM-uniqueness`(a); (iv) (iii) applied to the pair $(a''=d_O-1,a)$. Error type: E-P (pointwise in $q$). Content grade [A].
- **例 `ex:appM-unreduced` — 未约化系数违反标度（锐化值）**. Massive $\varphi^4$, $A=B=\varphi^2/2$, $a=4$; $\gamma^*$ = line $\ell_1$ ($V_A$–$V_B$) plus path $V_A$–$X_1$–$X_2$–$V_B$ (each $X$ with two external legs), $\omega(\gamma^*)=-4$; proper AB subparts unsubtracted. Then $c^{\gamma^*}_0(q)=\kappa\int d^4k\,\Delta(q-k)\Delta(k)^3=\frac{\kappa\pi^2}{2m^2}\frac1{q^2}\big(1+O(q^{-2}\ln q^2)\big)$ (using $\int d^4k\,\Delta^3=\pi^2/(2m^2)$): only $q^{-2}$, violating (iv); in the reduced coefficient $z_{\varphi^2\varphi^4}\tilde C^{(4)}_{\varphi^2}$ ($\tilde C^{(4)}_{\varphi^2}\simeq\Delta(q)$, $z_{\varphi^2\varphi^4}=-\kappa\int\Delta^3$) cancels: $\kappa\int d^4k\,[\Delta(q-k)-\Delta(q)]\Delta(k)^3=-\frac{\kappa\pi^2}{2q^4}+O(m^2q^{-6}\ln q)$ — **no logarithm and an $m$-independent leading term** (4D harmonicity of $1/x^2$: the region $k<q$ contributes $O(m^2\ln q/q^6)$, the region $k>q$ gives $2\pi^2\int_q^\infty dk\,k^3(k^{-2}-q^{-2})k^{-6}=-\pi^2/(2q^4)$). `\uses{thm:appM-scaling, cor:appM-reduction}`. VF-REF-14. Check: `refdoc/work_R5/scripts/check1.py` ($Gq^4\to-\pi^2/2$, $Gq^4/\ln q^2\to0$). Surpass: ERR-m23 (the reference stated only $O(q^{-4}\ln q^2)$); the reduced coefficient behaves like the massless one.
- **引理 `lem:appM-symbol` — 动量空间余项到坐标空间余项** (数学). Let $f\in C^\infty(\mathbb R^4)$ with $|\partial^\beta f|\le K_\beta(1+|q|)^{\varrho-|\beta|}\ln^k(2+|q|)$ and $g=\hat f$. For $0<|x|\le\frac12$: (a) $\varrho>-4$: $|g(x)|\le K|x|^{-4-\varrho}\ln^k(1/|x|)$; (b) $\varrho=-4$: $|g|\le K\ln^{k+1}(1/|x|)$; (c) $\varrho<-4$, $s=-4-\varrho$: $g\in C^{\lceil s\rceil-1}$ and $|g(x)-\sum_{|\beta|<s}\frac{x^\beta}{\beta!}\partial^\beta g(0)|\le K|x|^s\ln^{k+1}(1/|x|)$ (for non-integer $s$ even $\ln^k$). `% uses: none`. Proof: full (dyadic Littlewood–Paley decomposition + integration by parts).
- **推论 `cor:appM-coordinate` — 坐标空间的 OPE 余项** [R, R_cond]. （$R_{\rm cond}$ …）前提：\cref{asm:appM-remainder}。（本推论只涉及部分子态。）For $0<|x|\le\frac12$: $G_{AB}(x;p)=\sum_{d_O\le a}C_O(x)\tilde G_{N[O]}(p)+R_a(x;p)$ with (a) for $a\le d_A+d_B-2$: $|R_a|\le K|x|^{a+1-d_A-d_B}(1+|\ln|x||)^k$ (for $J^\mu J^\nu$: $|x|^{a-5}$, $a\le4$); (b) for $a\ge d_A+d_B-1$: $R_a=\sum_{|\beta|<a+1-d_A-d_B}x^\beta r_\beta(p)+O(|x|^{a+1-d_A-d_B}\ln^{k+1})$, $r_\beta=\int\frac{d^4q}{(2\pi)^4}\frac{(-iq)^\beta}{\beta!}\tilde N_a$. `\uses{asm:appM-remainder, thm:appM-scaling, lem:appM-symbol, lem:appM-contact}`. Proof: full (temperedness of $\tilde G^T$ and $\tilde C_O$ from the Bergère–de Calan–Malbouisson expansions, 文献待补, or from `Smirnov:1990rz`).
- **注 `rem:appM-polynomial` — 未识别的多项式项** (no tag). To recover Zimmermann's coordinate-space remainder $|x|^{a+1-d_A-d_B}$ for all $a$ the terms $x^\beta r_\beta$ must be identified as Green functions of $N_a[(\partial^\beta A)B]$; not done and not needed (Ch7 uses only the momentum-space statement).
- **[PENDING-VERIFY X3a] 命题 `prop:appM-remainder-discharge` — 余项估计的文献来源（经由唯一性）** — planned [R, R_PT] (cited). *Planned statement:* in the massive model of §M.2 the T-product admits a momentum-space asymptotic expansion with remainder $O(|q|^{D-1-a}\ln^k|q|)$, uniformly in direction, smooth in $p$ and $m$ with the same bounds for $p$-derivatives and $m\partial_m$ (theorem of `Anikin:1978tj` / `Zavyalov:1990kv`, scope to be verified: theory class, momentum vs coordinate space, uniformity); by `lem:appM-uniqueness`(a) its coefficients coincide with $\tilde C_O$ up to that order, hence `asm:appM-remainder` holds. *Evidence required:* X3 report quoting the theorem (location, hypotheses) and the derivation of the $\mathcal H_{a,\Gamma}$ bounds. *If PASS:* `asm:appM-remainder` status 「已解除（引用定理）」, `thm:appM-scaling` and `cor:appM-coordinate` become [R, R_PT] with `\uses{prop:appM-remainder-discharge,…}`. *Fallback:* keep the assumption; the 「地位」 field says "standard technique, not verbatim in the literature".

### M.5 `sec:appM-massless` 无质量 QCD (1.5 pp)

- *Target:* **`prop:dis-ope-euclid`** (Ch7, [A]) — 欧氏非例外动量下 Green 函数的逐图渐近展开: massless QCD ($n_f$ massless quarks, covariant gauge, $\overline{\rm MS}$), at each order, for Euclidean non-exceptional $(p,q)$ (no proper partial sum of external momenta vanishes): $\tilde G^T_{AB}-\sum_{d_O\le a}\tilde C_O(q;\mu)\tilde G_{[O]_\mu}=O(|q|^{D-1-a}\ln^k|q|)$ uniformly in direction and in compact non-exceptional $p$, in a basis including gauge-variant and ghost operators; $\tilde C_O$ is $|q|^{D-d_O}$ times a polynomial in $\ln(q^2/\mu^2)$ (dimensional analysis + polynomial $\ln\mu$ dependence) and unique (`lem:appM-uniqueness`(b)). *Proof location (sketch with citations):* (1) two rigorous frameworks — BPHZL with auxiliary mass $(s-1)M$, UV operators $t^{\delta}_{p,s}$ at $p=0,s=0$, IR operators $t^{\rho-1}_{p,s-1}$ at $p=0,s=1$ (`Lowenstein:1974qt`, `LowensteinZimmermann1975`; IR power counting at non-exceptional momenta, cf. `thm:ee-euclid-ir`) and DR+$\overline{\rm MS}$ with IR counterterms $R^*$ for coefficient functions (`Chetyrkin:1982zq`, `ChetyrkinTkachov1981`; graph-by-graph asymptotic expansions `Smirnov:1990rz`, `Smirnov2002`); (2) the two schemes differ by finite renormalizations **and** by finite non-invariant counterterms restoring the Slavnov–Taylor identities in BPHZL (`Breitenlohner:1975hg`, `Breitenlohner:1976te`; ERR-M28); (3) the OPE derivation in BPHZL parallels `thm:appM-ope-identity`; the DR asymptotic-expansion theorems are graph by graph and do not use gauge symmetry; (4) status [A] under the binding decision D3 (Ch25 open problem 4). Literature status (survey item 1; `Collins1984` §12.8 "proved in a gauge theory the same way"; `IzubuchiEtAl2018` Sec. II "widely assumed"); pure YM: `Hollands:2007zg`, `Frob:2015uqy`; massless superficially renormalizable theories (position space, remainder bounds; preprint): `Frob:2016mzv` — slot X3b.
- **注 `rem:appM-exceptional` — 例外动量与前向矩阵元** (no tag). The forward Compton configuration $(p,-p)$ is exceptional and on-shell partons are massless; in BPHZL the IR finiteness of zero-momentum gauge-invariant twist-2 insertions in off-shell forward kinematics can be checked at low orders but has no all-order proof; in DR the algebraic identities hold for any momenta but the remainder estimate needs an extension of the asymptotic-expansion theorems. Hence the rigorous perturbative OPE is at Euclidean non-exceptional momenta; the forward on-shell remainder is `asm:appM-forward`, and the hadronic transition is `asm:intro-np-ope`. `fig:appM-euclid-forward` (FIGURES_INDEX_v2 N19). Misconception guarded: 「前向在壳部分子矩阵元直接给出 Wilson 系数，无需额外步骤」 (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §6.9).
- **假设 `asm:appM-forward` — 前向在壳无质量部分子矩阵元的 OPE 余项** [\tagA]. Full text in `ASSUMPTIONS.md`. Content: massless QCD, DR (Laurent coefficients in $\varepsilon$), on-shell parton target $j$ ($p^2=0$, physical polarizations, spin averaged), Euclidean direction $\hat q$ with $2p\cdot\hat q$ purely imaginary: at each order $T^{\mu\nu}_j-\sum_{d_O\le N}\tilde C^{\mu\nu}_O(q;\mu)\langle p|[O]_\mu|p\rangle=O(|q|^{1-N}\ln^k|q|)$, uniformly in the direction. `\uses{def:dis-compton}`.
- **[PENDING-VERIFY X3b] 命题 `prop:appM-ope-qcd-scope` — 所引用的欧氏渐近展开定理对含夸克 QCD 的覆盖** — planned [R, R_PT] (cited). *Planned statement:* the cited theorems (list fixed by X3: `Smirnov:1990rz`/`Smirnov2002` graph-by-graph expansions; `LowensteinZimmermann1975` BPHZL convergence; `Frob:2016mzv` Thm 1 for massless superficially renormalizable theories) jointly establish the statement of `prop:dis-ope-euclid` for QCD with massless quarks, ghosts and gauge-variant operators. *If PASS:* `prop:dis-ope-euclid` is upgraded to [R, R_PT] (requires the lead author to revise decision D3 and Ch25 open problem 4; REFDOC S19/S63 item 1). *Fallback:* [A] stays.

### M.6 `sec:appM-brst` BRST、规范不变算符与扭度 (3.5 pp)

- Recap (no new environment): BRST in the binding conventions (`def:prelim-brst`; $sA^a_\mu=D^{ab}_\mu c^b$, $s\psi=igc^at^a\psi$, $sc^a=-\frac g2f^{abc}c^bc^c$, $s\bar c^a=B^a$, $sB^a=0$; $\mathcal L_{\rm gf}+\mathcal L_{\rm gh}=s(\bar c^a(\partial\cdot A^a+\frac\xi2B^a))$), nilpotency (`lem:prelim-brst-nilpotent`), Slavnov–Taylor identities (`thm:prelim-slavnov-taylor`), antifield master equation; no anomaly for vector-like QCD.
- **定义 `def:appM-alien` — 规范不变算符、BRST 恰当算符与运动方程算符** (no tag). Local operators of ghost number 0 and dimension $d$: (a) gauge invariant (colour-singlet polynomials in $\psi,\bar\psi,F_{\mu\nu}$ and covariant derivatives); (b) BRST exact $s\Lambda$; (c) equation-of-motion operators $X\cdot\delta S/\delta\phi$; (b)+(c) = **alien** operators $=\mathcal S_0(\phi^*X+\Lambda)|_{\phi^*=0}$. `\uses{def:prelim-brst}`.
- **定理 `thm:appM-local-cohomology` — 定域 BRST 上同调** (数学，经典，引用). For unintegrated local functions of ghost number 0: (a) $\mathcal S_0\Delta=0$ ⇒ $\Delta=\sum_jc_jO^{\rm GI}_j+\mathcal S_0\Xi$; (b) $X$ without antifields with $sX=0$ ⇒ $X=\sum_jc_jO^{\rm GI}_j+s\Lambda$. It is $H(s)$, not $H(s|d)$, because the insertions are unintegrated. `\uses{def:appM-alien, lem:prelim-brst-nilpotent}`. Proof: cited with sketch (Koszul–Tate differential with $H_k(\delta)=0$; longitudinal differential: $H(\gamma)$ = invariant polynomials of $F,\psi,\bar\psi$ and covariant derivatives times ghost primitives; $(\bar c,B)$ contractible) — `Henneaux:1993jn`, `Barnich:2000zw`, `Piguet:1995er`, `Joglekar:1975nu`, `Kluberg-Stern:1975ebk`.
- **注 `rem:appM-joglekar-lee` — Joglekar–Lee 混合定理的结构** (no tag). Pointer to `prop:appJ-local-operators` (App J; gauge-invariant operators mix only into gauge-invariant ⊕ BRST-exact ⊕ EOM operators, block-triangularly) with the proof sketch: BRST-invariant source $\rho$; quantum action principle and absence of anomalies ⇒ the generating functional satisfies the master equation; $\mathcal S_\Gamma\Gamma_\rho=0$; induction in loops with DR locality; `thm:appM-local-cohomology`(a); block-triangularity via the source of $\Xi$. Literature `Joglekar:1975nu`, `Joglekar:1976eb`, `Joglekar:1976pe`, `Kluberg-Stern:1974iel`, `Kluberg-Stern:1974nmx`, `DixonTaylor1974`.
- **引理 `lem:appM-alien-vanish` — 外来算符的物理矩阵元为零（非前向或红外正规化）** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$ …）（本引理只涉及部分子态。）(a) EOM operators: by the Schwinger–Dyson identity $\langle T[X\cdot\delta S/\delta\phi](z)\prod_k\phi_k(y_k)\rangle=i\sum_k(\pm)\delta^4(z-y_k)\langle TX(y_k)\prod_{l\ne k}\phi_l\rangle$ (the $\delta^4(0)$ term vanishes in DR), their matrix elements between on-shell partonic states vanish after LSZ **for non-zero momentum transfer, or with IR-regulated external legs (massive, or off-shell followed by the limit)**; at zero momentum transfer with regulated legs the vanishing follows from counting (two LSZ factors $(p^2-m^2)^2$ against at most one pole). (b) BRST-exact operators: $\langle\beta|s\Lambda|\alpha\rangle=i\langle\beta|[Q_B,\Lambda\}|\alpha\rangle=0$ for physical states (`KugoOjima1979`). `\uses{def:appM-alien, thm:prelim-slavnov-taylor}`. Proof: full. Surpass: ERR-M24 (the reference claimed "including forward" with an imprecise reason).
- **注 `rem:appM-forward-aliens` — 前向在壳胶子矩阵元的红外微妙之处** (no tag). At zero momentum transfer with massless on-shell gluons in DR the naive argument fails through IR singularities: Hamberg–van Neerven puzzle (`Hamberg:1991qt`) resolved by Collins–Scalise (`Collins:1994ee`); modern all-loop alien counterterms (`Falcioni:2022fdm`, `Gehrmann:2023ksf`). One-loop on-shell renormalization is safe; beyond one loop off-shell calculations need the alien operators.
- **假设 `asm:appM-brst-ope` — OPE 的 BRST 对称插入** [\tagA]. Full text in `ASSUMPTIONS.md`. Content: (B1) BRST-symmetric renormalized insertions — $[sO]$ exists with classical coefficients in the same basis, the Ward identities $\langle T[sO]\Phi\rangle=\mp\langle T[O]s\Phi\rangle$ hold, and $\langle T\,\tilde T^{\mu\nu}(q)\,s\Phi\rangle$ = (polynomial in $q$) × (local insertion); (B2) the remainder of `prop:dis-ope-euclid` also holds with $s\phi$ insertions. `\uses{def:appM-alien}`.
- *Target:* **`prop:dis-ope-gi`** (Ch7, [A]; implication [R, R_cond] under `asm:appM-brst-ope`) — 物理矩阵元只需规范不变算符: for $A=J^\mu$, $B=J^\nu$ ($D=2$) and every $d\ge D+1$, $X_d(q)\equiv\sum_{d_O=d}\tilde C_O(q;\mu)O$ is classically BRST closed, hence gauge-invariant plus BRST exact; between physical states only gauge-invariant operators survive; the twist-2 part is spanned by $O_q^{(n)},O_g^{(n)}$. *Proof plan (full, given the assumption):* expand $sO=\sum_Y\sigma_{OY}Y$ and the contact term $\pi_\Phi=\sum_Y\pi_Y(q)Y$ ($\pi_Y$ homogeneous of degree $D+1-d_Y$); `lem:appM-uniqueness`(a) gives $\sum_O\tilde C_O\sigma_{OY}-\pi_Y=O(|q|^{D-1-a}\ln^k)$; the log-polynomial structure makes it vanish identically; for $d_Y>D+1$, $\pi_Y=0$; then `thm:appM-local-cohomology`(b), `lem:appM-alien-vanish`(b), `lem:dis-twist-classification`. Literature: `Joglekar:1976eb`, `Joglekar:1976pe` (OPE of currents in gauge theories), `Hollands:2007zg` (pure YM, closes on gauge-invariant operators), `Frob:2016mzv` Thm 2 (preprint; BRST Ward identity of OPE coefficients if $H^{1,4}_{E(4)}(\hat s|d)=0$).
- *Target:* **`lem:dis-twist-classification`** (Ch7, [R, R_def]) — 扭度 2 规范不变算符的分类: boost weight $h$ ($+$ index $+1$, $-$ index $-1$, transverse 0; $\psi_+=\mathcal P_+\psi$, $\bar\psi_+=\bar\psi\mathcal P_-$: $h=+\frac12$; bad components $-\frac12$), **collinear twist** $t=d-h$, additive; table $\psi_\pm$: 1/2, $D^+$: 0, $D^j$: 1, $D^-$: 2, $F^{+j}$: 1, $F^{+-},F^{ij}$: 2, $F^{-j}$: 3 (here "$\psi_\pm$: 1/2" means $t(\psi_+)=1$, $t(\psi_-)=2$); total twist of $O^{+\cdots+}$ is $d-n$; a colour singlet needs ≥ 2 fields, so twist 2 means exactly two $t=1$ fields and only $D^+$: $\bar\psi_+\cdots\psi_+$ or $F^{+j}\cdots F^{+k}$; $\bar\psi\mathcal P_-\Gamma\mathcal P_+\psi\ne0$ only for $\Gamma\in\{\gamma^+,\gamma^+\gamma_5,\gamma^+\gamma^j\}$; $\gamma^+\gamma^j$ carries an uncontractible transverse index (mixed symmetry — transversity); $\gamma^+\gamma_5$ and $\epsilon^{jk}F^{+j}\cdots F^{+k}$ are pseudotensors (no spin-averaged unpolarized matrix element by parity); flavour off-diagonal bilinears absent; moving $D^+$ costs total derivatives; for odd $n$ the gluon operator is a total derivative. Hence, modulo total derivatives and EOM operators, the spin-averaged forward twist-2 symmetric traceless gauge-invariant operators are spanned by $O_q^{(n)}$ (each flavour) and $O_g^{(n)}$ ($n$ even). *Proof:* full (reader check: the span of $\mathcal P_-\Gamma\mathcal P_+$ has dimension 4).
- *Target:* **`lem:dis-twist-chirality`** (Ch7, [R, R_def]) — 扭度奇偶性与手征: for a bosonic ghost-free local polynomial in $\psi,\bar\psi,A$ with eigenvalue $\chi=\pm1$ under $\psi\to\gamma_5\psi$, $\bar\psi\to-\bar\psi\gamma_5$, in the symmetric traceless rank-$j$ representation with dimension $d$: $(-1)^{d-j}=\chi$; chiral-even gauge-invariant operators have even twist, odd twist means chiral odd. **Caveat (print):** false for mixed symmetry (transversity: twist 2 and chiral odd; ERR-m20). *Proof:* full ($t\equiv n_T\pmod 2$ for bosonic blocks; for bilinears $t\equiv n_T$ (chiral even), $t\equiv n_T+1$ (chiral odd); $\sum n_T$ even in $O^{+\cdots+}$; reader check over all Dirac words).
- *Target:* **`prop:dis-chiral-suppression`** (Ch7, [R, R_PT]) — 奇扭度系数的手征压低: in massless-quark PT every chiral-odd (odd-twist) coefficient vanishes at every order: an open quark line through an AB part has $n_V$ vertices and $n_V-1$ propagators $\slashed k$, an odd number of $\gamma$'s; the parity argument is DR safe and needs no $\gamma_5$; quark masses give odd powers of $m_q$. Non-perturbatively the suppression is part of `asm:intro-np-ope`(c) (chiral symmetry breaking; $\psi\to\gamma_5\psi$ on all flavours is a $U(1)_A$ rotation by $\pi/2$ shifting $\theta$ by $n_f\pi$; instantons $e^{-2\pi/\alpha_s(|q|)}$). *Proof:* full.
- *Target:* **`prop:dis-ope-rg`** (Ch7, [R, R_OPE]) — RG equation of the coefficients **derived from uniqueness**: if $\mu\,d[O_i]/d\mu=-\sum_j\gamma_{ij}[O_j]$, then $(\mu\partial_\mu+\beta\partial_{\alpha_s})\tilde C_i=\sum_j\tilde C_j\gamma_{ji}$ with solution $\tilde C(q;\mu)=\tilde C(q;|q|,\alpha_s(|q|))\,\mathcal P\exp(-\int_\mu^{|q|}\frac{d\mu'}{\mu'}\gamma)$. *Proof:* full — $\hat C\equiv\tilde C(\mu')U(\mu',\mu)$ satisfies the hypotheses of `lem:appM-uniqueness`(b), hence $\hat C=\tilde C$. Dictionary: $\mu\,dA/d\mu=-\gamma A$ ⇔ $dA/d\ln\mu^2=\Gamma A$ with $\gamma=-2\Gamma$ (row 18, VF-CONV-16); C-even/C-odd distinction of the continuations (`rem:pdf-ns-plusminus`, ERR-M10).
- **[PENDING-VERIFY X3d] 命题 `prop:appM-brst-ope-ym` — (B1)(B2) 在纯 Yang–Mills 中成立** — planned [R, R_PT] (cited) for pure YM; QCD part [A]. *Planned statement:* in pure Yang–Mills (semisimple group) the BRST Ward identities of the OPE coefficients hold and the OPE of gauge-invariant operators closes on gauge-invariant operators (`Hollands:2007zg`; `Frob:2016mzv` Thm 2, preprint, condition $H^{1,4}_{E(4)}(\hat s|d)=0$); for massless QCD with vector-like quarks the same holds provided that cohomology class contains no obstruction (only parity-odd classes). *If PASS:* `asm:appM-brst-ope` status 「纯 YM 已解除；QCD 部分解除」 and `prop:dis-ope-gi`'s status paragraph updated. *Fallback:* status unchanged.

### M.7 `sec:appM-forward` 固定阶的全自旋求和 (1.5 pp)

- *Target:* **`prop:dis-ope-forward`** (Ch7, [A]; implication [R, R_cond] under `asm:appM-forward`) — 前向在壳部分子矩阵元: massless QCD, DR, on-shell parton target $j$; assume (a) `asm:appM-forward`; (b) only gauge-invariant operators of dimension ≥ 3 contribute (`prop:dis-ope-gi`); (c) $T_2^{(j)}$ analytic in $|\omega|<1$ (`prop:dis-compton-analytic`, PT). Then $T^{(j)}_2=4\sum_{n\ {\rm even}}\omega^{n-1}\sum_iC^{(n)}_{2,i}A^{(n)}_{i/j}$, uniformly convergent on $|\omega|\le r<1$ ($A^{(n)}_{i/j}$ = tree value plus pure IR poles). *Proof plan (full, given (a)–(c)):* (1) scaleless loops make $\langle p|[O]|p\rangle$ a polynomial in $p$ only with $r=d_O-2$; $r\ge2$ and twist ≥ 2 force twist exactly 2 and kill traces ($p^2=0$); (2) along a Euclidean direction with $2p\cdot\hat q$ purely imaginary, $\omega=c_0/t$ and the remainder is $O(t^{2-N}\ln^kt)$; (3) $R=\sum_kL^kR_k(\omega)$: a non-zero Taylor coefficient below $\omega^{N-3}$ would give $|R|\gtrsim t^{1-n_*}$ — contradiction. *Remark (amplitude form, print):* $T^{(j)}_2(\omega)=\sum_q\int_{-1}^1d\xi\,\hat H_q(\xi\omega)f_{q/j}(\xi)+\int_0^1d\xi\,\hat H_g(\xi\omega)f_{g/j}(\xi)$, $\hat H_i(w)=4\sum_{n\ {\rm even}}C^{(n)}_{2,i}w^{n-1}$ (quark odd-extended by $f_q(-\xi)=-f_{\bar q}(\xi)$; the gluon only on $[0,1]$, otherwise a factor 2); tree $\hat H_q=4e_q^2w/(1-w^2)$; no $d\xi/\xi$ (the $1/\xi$ is absorbed by $\hat P\hat P/(P\cdot q)$); momentum-space form of `ChristHasslacherMueller1972`, `BrandtPreparata1971`, `Anikin:1978tj`. VF-CONV-14, VF-DIS-8.
- **例 `ex:appM-forward-oneloop` — 单圈精确到 $\varepsilon$ 的检验**. At $O(\alpha_s)$ the Taylor coefficients in $\omega$ of the hard-region forward Compton amplitude of an on-shell massless parton equal the Mellin moments of the bare cut results **exactly in $\varepsilon$** for $N=1..8$ (quark; $F_2$ and $F_L$) and even $N\le8$ (gluon; odd moments vanish by crossing); the $\overline{\rm MS}$-renormalized coefficients reproduce $C^q_{2,N}$, $C^q_{L,N}$ ($N\le6$) and $C^g_{2,N}$, $C^g_{L,N}$ ($N=2,4,6$) of VF-DIS-8; pole residues $-P_{qq,N}$, $-P_{qg,N}$; no $1/\varepsilon^2$. Print the table $N=1..8$ (VF-DIS-8). `\uses{prop:dis-ope-forward}`. VF-DIS-13. Checks: `research/c_dis/ope_check.py` (`logs/ope_check_N8.log`), `ope_wilson.py` (`logs/ope_wilson_N6.log`). Meaning: a one-loop instance of `asm:appM-forward` for finitely many $N$ — evidence, not proof.
- **[PENDING-VERIFY X3c] 命题 `prop:appM-forward-oneloop-alln` — 单圈前向余项对所有 $N$ 成立** — planned [R, R_PT] (statement restricted to $O(\alpha_s)$). *Planned statement:* at $O(\alpha_s)$, for every $N$ (even $N$ for the gluon), the Taylor coefficient of $\omega^{N-1}$ of the hard-region forward amplitude equals the $N$-th Mellin moment of the bare partonic structure function exactly in $\varepsilon$ (closed-form generating function in $\omega$ from the master integrals $\int d^Dl\,(p\cdot l)^a/[(l^2)^\alpha((l+sq)^2)^\beta]$ of `ope_check.py`); hence `asm:appM-forward` holds at one loop. *Fallback:* `ex:appM-forward-oneloop` only.
- **[PENDING-VERIFY X3e] 定理 `thm:appM-forward-expansion` — 前向在壳展开定理（所有阶）** — planned [R, R_PT]. *Planned statement:* massless QCD at each order, dimensional plus analytic regularization (line powers $a_l$): for every forward Compton graph with an on-shell massless parton leg ($p^2=0$), the Taylor coefficient of $\omega^n$ of the hard-region amplitude equals the $n$-th term of the naive Taylor expansion of the integrand in $p$ (a massless propagator-type integral), identically in $(a_l,D)$; the IR poles of the naive terms are those of the partonic matrix elements; hence `asm:appM-forward` holds at every order. *Evidence required:* the X3 report (structural conditions on the Symanzik polynomials — domination of $F_1$ by $F_0$ in $F=F_0+\omega F_1$, full-dimensional Newton polytope of the Lee–Pomeransky polynomial $U+F_0$ (`LeePomeransky2013`), admissibility of the DR line, invariance of the polytope for $|\omega|<1$ — with the classification of degenerate graphs), checks `verify/extend/X3-ope/scripts/f1_forward_graphs.py` and `f2_triangle.py` (one-loop forward triangle: equality of the exact ${}_2F_1$ coefficients with the naive terms identically in $(a_1,a_2,a_3,D)$). *If PASS:* `asm:appM-forward` status 「已解除」, X3c and `ex:appM-forward-oneloop` become illustrations; `prop:dis-ope-forward` remains [A] unless `prop:dis-ope-gi` is also upgraded (X3d for QCD). *Fallback:* X3c or `ex:appM-forward-oneloop` only.

### M.8 `sec:appM-np` 非微扰层 (5 pp)

Opening paragraph: obstacles — no four-dimensional non-abelian gauge theory has been constructed (Wightman/Osterwalder–Schrader; Yang–Mills existence and mass gap is a Clay problem; partial results: Bałaban's UV stability, Magnen–Rivasseau–Sénéor with IR cutoff — 文献待补 for both); composite fields need point splitting, which presupposes OPE-type asymptotics; perturbation theory is not summable (renormalons, instantons), so "every order" implies nothing non-perturbative and at most asymptoticity can be hoped for (two-dimensional Gross–Neveu: Schwinger functions are Borel sums, `Feldman:1986ax`, `Gawedzki:1985ez`).

- **表 `tab:appM-axioms` — 每条公理/假设在何处被使用** (new table, owner App M): rows (W1) locality + spectral condition → `lem:appM-forward-tube` (forward-tube holomorphy), edge of the wedge; (W2) spectral condition in the target superselection sector → support of $\nu_Q$ on $[1,\infty)$, `lem:appM-positivity`; isolation → Haag–Ruelle states only (`Haag:1958vt`); (W3) temperedness → Vladimirov bound, $Q$-independent $A,B$; (W4) covariant retarded extension → definition of $T_2$; polynomial ambiguity → finitely many low $t_n$; `asm:intro-composite` → twist-2 basis, RG, light-ray operators (`thm:pdf-moments-local`); `asm:intro-np-ope` → (O1) for hadrons, covariance, chiral suppression (`lem:dis-ope-segment`); `asm:intro-af-coeff` → asymptotic $\overline{\rm MS}$ coefficients (`thm:dis-moments-np`(i)); `asm:intro-growth` → $n<n_0$ (`prop:dis-low-moments`). Commentary only: the assumptions are **stated once** in Ch1 (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §4.4 item 5). Correction note (ERR-M25): (W2) is formulated in the superselection sector of the target's conserved flavour numbers (false in the chiral limit and for $m_u\ne m_d$ if formulated for the whole baryon-number-1 sector); massive light quarks are assumed and reconciled with the massless $\overline{\rm MS}$ coefficients by treating $m_q^2/Q^2$ and chiral-odd $m_qM/Q^2$ terms as power corrections.
- **引理 `lem:appM-forward-tube` — 前向管全纯性与与 $Q$ 无关的增长指数** [R, R_cond]. （$R_{\rm cond}$ …）前提：\cref{asm:intro-wightman}。非微扰地位：见前提（MA0）。The Fourier transform $\tilde R^{\mu\nu}(q)$ of the retarded commutator of (W4) is holomorphic in the forward tube ${\rm Im}\,q\in V_+$ with $|\tilde R^{\mu\nu}(q)|\le C(1+|q|)^a\big(1+{\rm dist}({\rm Im}\,q,\partial V_+)^{-b}\big)$ (Vladimirov-type bound; `Streater:1989vi`, `Jost:1965yxu`; Vladimirov's monograph 文献待补). With the parametrization $q^0=w$, $\mathbf q=(0,0,s)$, $s^2=w^2+Q^2$ (rest frame) one has ${\rm Im}\,q\in V_+$ and ${\rm dist}\ge w_2Q^2/(2\sqrt2(|w|^2+Q^2))$ ($w_2={\rm Im}\,w$); consequently $|T_2(\omega)|\le C_Q(1+|\omega|)^A|{\rm Im}\,\omega|^{-B}$ with $A,B$ **independent of $Q$** and $C_Q$ locally bounded. `\uses{asm:intro-wightman, def:dis-compton}`. Proof: full given the tube theorem (the geometric bound re-derived; reader check: 20 000 random points, 0 failures).
- **引理 `lem:appM-hatP-zero` — $\hat P=0$ 点的不变量论证** [R, R_def]. （$R_{\rm def}$ …）At $w=iQ$ ($\hat P=0$, where the projector of $T_2$ is singular) expand in complex $\mathbf k$: $\tilde R^{ij}=a\,\delta^{ij}+b\,k^ik^j$, so $T_2=(Q^4/\nu)(b+a/Q^2)$ is analytic there. `\uses{def:dis-compton}`. Proof: full.
- **引理 `lem:appM-positivity` — 不连续性的正性（$Q^2$ 抹平）** [R, R_cond]. （$R_{\rm cond}$ …）前提：\cref{asm:intro-wightman}。非微扰地位：见前提（MA0）。${\rm Disc}\,T_2=T_2(\omega+i0)-T_2(\omega-i0)=4\pi i\,\nu_Q$ on $(0,\infty)$ with $\nu_Q\ge0$ supported on $[1,\infty)$, $\nu_Q(d\omega)=F_2(1/\omega)\,d\omega$ (elastic point included), $\int x^{n-2}F_2\,dx=\int\omega^{-n}d\nu_Q$; positivity from spectral measures ($F_1\ge0$, $F_2\ge\nu F_1/\hat P^2\ge0$); the restriction of four-dimensional distributional boundary values to the one-dimensional slice at fixed $Q^2$ (needed both for positivity and for the edge-of-the-wedge step) is justified by smearing in $Q^2$ (`Epstein1960`; repairs ERR-m24/R5 E9). `\uses{asm:intro-wightman, lem:appM-forward-tube, prop:dis-W-structure}`. Proof: full.
- **引理 `lem:appM-green` — 几乎解析延拓与 Green 公式：不连续性测度的增长** (数学). If $F$ is holomorphic in ${\rm Im}\,\omega>0$ with $|F(\omega)|\le C(1+|\omega|)^A|{\rm Im}\,\omega|^{-B}$ and its boundary value has imaginary part the positive measure $\nu$ on $[1,\infty)$, then $\nu([1,R])\le C'R^{A+1}$. `% uses: none`. Proof: full (almost-analytic extension $\tilde\chi$ of a cutoff with $|\bar\partial\tilde\chi|=O(|{\rm Im}\,\omega|^{B})$, Green's formula; Hörmander, 文献待补).
- *Target:* **`prop:dis-dispersion-np`** (Ch7, [R, R_cond: `asm:intro-wightman`]) — 由 Wightman 公理得到的有限减除色散关系: (a) $T_2$ analytic in $\mathbb C\setminus((-\infty,-1]\cup[1,\infty))$, odd, real-analytic; (b) $|T_2(\omega)|\le C_Q(1+|\omega|)^A|{\rm Im}\,\omega|^{-B}$, $A,B$ independent of $Q$; (c) ${\rm Disc}\,T_2=4\pi iF_2(1/\omega)\,d\omega\ge0$; (d) for every even $n_0>A+2$: $\int_0^1x^{n_0-2}F_2<\infty$ and $T_2(\omega)=\sum_{n<n_0}t_n\omega^{n-1}+4\omega^{n_0-1}\int_0^1dx\,\frac{x^{n_0-2}F_2(x)}{1-x^2\omega^2}$, so $t_n=4\int_0^1x^{n-2}F_2$ for even $n\ge n_0$. *Proof plan (full):* (a) `lem:appM-forward-tube` + edge of the wedge (`Epstein1960`, with the $Q^2$-smearing of `lem:appM-positivity`) + crossing + `lem:appM-hatP-zero`; (b) `lem:appM-forward-tube`; (c) `lem:appM-positivity`; (d) `lem:appM-green` gives $\nu_Q([1,R])\le CR^{A+1}$; the dispersive integral $D(\omega)$ has the same discontinuity; $H=T_2-\sum_{n<n_0}t_n\omega^{n-1}-D$ is entire with polynomial bounds, hence a polynomial (`lem:appK-entire`), and $H\equiv0$ by comparing its growth on $i\mathbb R$ with its zero of order $n_0-1$ at 0. Reader checks (R5): the dist formula, $T_2=(Q^4/\nu)(b+a/Q^2)$, the $\bar\partial\tilde\chi$ formula, Green's-formula orientation, ${\rm Disc}$ normalization $4\pi i$. VF-REF-4. This replaces v1's "drop the circle at infinity under MA3" (OUR-3).
- *Target:* **`lem:dis-ope-segment`** (Ch7, [R, R_cond: `asm:intro-np-ope`]) — OPE 在线段上的多项式逼近: if (O1) holds to level $N$ (no identity operator; $T^{\mu\nu}$ symmetric and transverse) with covariant coefficients $|\tilde C_j|\le K|q|^{2-d_j}\ln^k|q|$, matrix elements $\langle P|O_j|P\rangle$ = combinations of $M^{d_j-2-r}\times$ ($r$ factors $P$ and $g,\epsilon$) with $d_j-r\ge2$, chiral suppression $|\tilde C_j|\le Km|q|^{1-d_j}\ln^k$ for odd $d_j-r$, and $d_j-r=2$ only from $O_q^{(n)},O_g^{(n)}$ ($r=n$), then there is an odd polynomial $\mathcal P_N(\omega)=\sum_{n\le N-2}\pi^{(N)}_n\omega^{n-1}$ with $\sup_{\mathcal I_Q}|T_2-\mathcal P_N|\le K_N(M/Q)^{N-2}\ln^kQ$, $|\pi^{(N)}_n|\le K\ln^kQ$, and $\pi^{(N)}_n=4\sum_iC^{(n)}_{2,i}A^{(n)}_i+O\big(\frac{M^2+mM}{Q^2}\ln^kQ\big)$. *Proof plan (full):* (0) transversalize with $\Pi=g-qq/q^2$ (bounded at Euclidean points); (1) the general symmetric transverse tensor is $A\tilde g+B\hat P\hat P$; $\tau_j=\nu B_j$ is a polynomial in $\omega$ of degree $\le d_j-3$ and the $r$-term contributes only parity $r-1$; (2) projector $T_2=\frac{3\nu}{2(\hat P^2)^2}[\hat P_\mu\hat P_\nu-\frac{\hat P^2}3g_{\mu\nu}]T^{\mu\nu}$, $\hat P=P-\frac{P\cdot q}{q^2}q$ (VF-REF-1); on $\mathcal I_Q$ its coefficient is $\le CQ/M$, so $|\tau_j|\le C(M/Q)^{d_j-3}\ln^k$ and the remainder is $\le K_N(M/Q)^{N-2}\ln^k$; (3) `lem:appK-markov` for the coefficients; case analysis $d_j\ge n+4$ / $d_j=n+3$ (forced $r=n$; odd ⇒ $mM/Q^2$) / $d_j=n+2$ (twist 2). Segment: `lem:dis-euclid-segment` (VF-REF-1).
- *Target:* **`thm:dis-fixed-n`** (Ch7, [R, R_cond]) — 由定域 OPE 得到固定 $n$ 的 Taylor 系数: assume `lem:dis-ope-segment` for every even $N$; $T_2$ analytic and odd in $|\omega|<1$; $F_2\,dx\ge0$ on $[0,1]$ (elastic point included); a $Q$-independent even $n_0$ with $t_n=4\int x^{n-2}F_2$ for even $n\ge n_0$. Then for every even $n\ge2$: $t_n(Q^2)=4\sum_iC^{(n)}_{2,i}A^{(n)}_i+O\big(\frac{M^2+mM}{Q^2}\ln^kQ\big)$; no sum over spins and no interchange of limits. *Proof plan (full),* $N\ge\max(n+4,n_0+2)$ fixed: (1) positivity tail $0\le t_{n'}\le t_{n_0}$ for $n'\ge n_0$ ⇒ $|\sum_{n'\ge N}t_{n'}\omega^{n'-1}|\le2t_{n_0}|\omega|^{N-1}$ on $|\omega|\le\frac12$; (2) $h=\sum_{n'\le N-2}(t_{n'}-\pi_{n'})\omega^{n'-1}$ satisfies $|h|\le\varepsilon_Q=K_N(M/Q)^{N-2}\ln^k+2t_{n_0}r_Q^{N-1}$ on $\mathcal I_Q$ ($r_Q=\sqrt3M/Q$), so $|t_{n'}-\pi^{(N)}_{n'}|\le c_N\varepsilon_Qr_Q^{-(n'-1)}$ (`lem:appK-markov`); (3) bootstrap at $n'=n_0$: $t_{n_0}\le K\ln^kQ$; (4) $|t_n-\pi_n|\le K(M/Q)^3\ln^k$, then the last clause of `lem:dis-ope-segment`. Misconception M34 (precise form).
- *Target:* **`prop:dis-low-moments`** (Ch7, [R, R_def]) — 低阶矩与增长条件的等价: if (d) of `prop:dis-dispersion-np` holds for some $n_0\ge4$, then $t_n=4\int_0^1x^{n-2}F_2$ for all even $2\le n<n_0$ **iff** $\lim_{y\to\infty}T_2(iy)/(iy)=0$, i.e. $\int F_2<\infty$ and no real "fixed-pole" term $c\,\omega$; Regge behaviour of $F_2$ is neither necessary nor sufficient; fixed poles are invisible in $F_2$; hence the momentum sum rule needs `asm:intro-growth`. *Proof plan (full):* descending induction via $\frac{T_2(iy)}{iy}=\sum_{n<k-2}t_n(iy)^{n-2}+(iy)^{k-4}\big[t_{k-2}-4\int x^{k-4}F_2\frac{x^2y^2}{1+x^2y^2}\big]$. VF-REF-4.
- **引理 `lem:appM-norm-bound` — RG 演化矩阵的范数界（质量混合的重标度）** [R, R_cond]. （$R_{\rm cond}$ …）前提：\cref{asm:intro-composite}、\cref{asm:intro-af-coeff}。非微扰地位：见前提（MA0、MA4）。After rescaling each operator $O_i$ by $M_0^{d_i-d_{\min}}$ with a fixed mass $M_0$ (so that the mass-mixing entries $\propto m^{d_i-d_j}$ of $\gamma$ become dimensionless and bounded, with running masses $m(\mu')\le C\,m(\mu_0)$ for $\mu'\ge\mu_0$), the evolution matrix obeys $\|U(\mu_0,\mu)^{\pm1}\|\le\big(\ln(|q|/\mu_1)/\ln(\mu/\mu_1)\big)^{Cc}$ for $\mu\le|q|$, from $\alpha_s(\mu')\le c/\ln(\mu'/\mu_1)$ and $\|\gamma(\alpha)\|\le C\alpha$. `\uses{asm:intro-composite, asm:intro-af-coeff}`. Proof: full (Grönwall). Repairs ERR-m24/R5 E10.
- *Target:* **`thm:dis-moments-np`** (Ch7, [R, R_cond: `asm:intro-wightman`, `asm:intro-composite`, `asm:intro-np-ope`, `asm:intro-af-coeff`; (ii) all even $n$ with `asm:intro-growth`]) — DIS 矩因子化的非微扰条件定理: (i) the coefficients defined at $\mu$ by the exact operator RG from $\mu_0$ satisfy (O1) at every $\mu$ and the RG equation exactly; $|\tilde C_i|\le K|q|^{2-d_i}\ln^k$; $C^{(n)}_{2,i}(\frac{Q^2}{\mu^2},\alpha_s(\mu))=\sum_jC^{(n)}_{2,j}(1,\alpha_s(Q))[\mathcal P\exp(-\int_\mu^Q\frac{d\mu'}{\mu'}\gamma^{(n)})]_{ji}$ with $C(1,\alpha_s(Q))$ asymptotic to the $\overline{\rm MS}$ series; (ii) for even $n\ge n_0$: $\int_0^1x^{n-2}F_2=\sum_iC^{(n)}_{2,i}A^{(n)}_i+O(\frac{\Lambda_h^2}{Q^2}\ln^kQ)$; with `asm:intro-growth` for all even $n\ge2$; (iii) under (Φ): `cor:dis-weak-xspace`. *Proof plan (full):* exact operator relation $O_i(\mu_0)=\sum_jU_{ij}O_j(\mu)$; `lem:appM-norm-bound`; verify the hypotheses of `thm:dis-fixed-n`: `lem:dis-ope-segment` from `asm:intro-np-ope` (with (W1) parity and (W4)), analyticity/oddness and positivity from `prop:dis-dispersion-np`(a)(c), $n_0$ from (d); $n<n_0$ from `prop:dis-low-moments`.
- *Target:* **`cor:dis-weak-xspace`** (Ch7, [R, R_cond]) — $x$ 空间的弱收敛: if for large $Q$ there are finite signed measures $\Phi_Q$ on $[0,1]$ with $\int x^{n-2}d\Phi_Q=\sum_iC^{(n)}_{2,i}A^{(n)}_i$ for even $n\ge n_0$, then (i) $\int x^{n_0-2}\phi\,d(F_2dx-\Phi_Q)=O(\frac{M^2+mM}{Q^2}\ln^kQ)$ for polynomials $\phi$ in $x^2$; (ii) if the masses of $x^{n_0-2}F_2dx$ and $x^{n_0-2}|\Phi_Q|$ are uniformly bounded, $\int x^{n_0-2}\phi\,d(F_2dx-\Phi_Q)\to0$ for all $\phi\in C[0,1]$; a pointwise, $x$-uniform $O(1/Q^2)$ is impossible from the OPE (`rem:dis-nonuniform`, VF-MISC-3); existence of $\Phi_Q$ is a Hausdorff moment problem (assumed; practical substitute: truncated $\Phi_{Q,K}$ with errors $O(\alpha_s(Q)^{K+1})$). *Proof plan (full):* `lem:appK-hausdorff`, `thm:appK-even-moments` ($x^2$ separates points), Stone–Weierstrass; weak-convergence statement of App A `sec:appA-plus`. Pointer: `rem:appK-counterexamples` ($\delta'$, $\sin(Q^2x)$, Hilbert-matrix instability VF-MISC-2).
- **注 `rem:appM-svz` — 实用 OPE、基的重排与重整子吸收** (no tag). The practical (SVZ) OPE with a factorization scale $\Lambda\ll\mu_F\ll Q$ and a hard cutoff has power-divergent mixing ($\mu_F^2O_2$ in $O_4(\mu_F)$), is routing dependent and gauge breaking unless a gauge-invariant smooth cutoff is found (pointer `rem:power-practical-ope`, Ch21); rearrangement freedom $O'_i=O_i+\sum_{d_j<d_i}\kappa_{ij}\Lambda^{d_i-d_j}O_j$ leaves asymptotic expansions unchanged and makes twist-2 coefficients meaningful only to relative $O(\Lambda^2/Q^2\ln^kQ)$ unless normalized non-perturbatively (pointer `prop:power-rearrangement`, Ch21; rearranging only twist ≥ 4 changes twist-2 coefficients by $o(\alpha_s^K)$, so `thm:dis-moments-np` is unaffected); renormalon absorption into twist 4 has exactly two conjectural ingredients (Borel continuation along $\mathbb R_+$ near the singularities; factorization of the residue with RG-matched $\alpha_s$ dependence), verified at large $\beta_0$ and in the large-$N$ O($N$) σ-model (`Beneke:1998eq`, `David:1982qv`, `Novikov:1984ac`); general case `conj:power-renormalon-ht`.
- **注 `rem:appM-supporting` — 支持性的严格结果** (no tag). Convergence of the OPE at finite distance in massive and massless $\varphi^4_4$ at fixed loop order (`Hollands:2011gf`, `Holland:2014pna`); all-order bounds for gauge-invariant correlators in Yang–Mills (`Frob:2015uqy`); existence and recursion of the OPE in Yang–Mills (`Frob:2016mzv`, preprint), associativity and recursive construction (`Holland:2015tia`, `Holland:2014ifa`); OPE from phase-space properties in AQFT (`Bostelmann:2005rr`); curved spacetime (`Hollands:2006ag`, `Hollands:2007zg`); Borel summability in the Gross–Neveu model (`Feldman:1986ax`, `Gawedzki:1985ez`); lattice short-distance comparisons are evidence, not proof. Status for QCD with quarks per frontier/others §2.7 (convergence [C]).

### M.9 `sec:appM-status` 状态 (1 pp)

Chiral suppression is an independent input (`asm:intro-np-ope`(c)).
- **注 `rem:appM-vacuum` — 真空 OPE** (no tag). For the vacuum (`asm:intro-np-ope`(a)), only scalar VEVs survive; there is no gauge-invariant dimension-2 scalar in the massless limit, so the Adler function has no $1/Q^2$ term; the leading power corrections come from $\frac{\alpha_s}\pi G^2$ and $m_q\bar qq$; pointer `thm:ee-ope`, `conj:ee-np-ope`, `rem:ee-complex-ope` (Ch6).

## Appendix N — Collins 减除法的精确形式 / The precise form of Collins' subtraction method

`\label{app:appN}` · `appN_subtraction.tex` · **24 pp** · appendix (proofs) · written **first** among M/N/O

**Purpose.** Turn "the subtraction step" of every factorization proof into (i) a deliberately elementary conditional theorem (`thm:appN-subtraction`: Collins conditions (CS1)–(CS3) ⇒ remainder power suppressed in an integral norm uniform in $\varepsilon$), (ii) the exact combinatorics that reduce (CS1)(CS2) to grouped power counting (chains, pairing, product formula), (iii) explicit idempotent approximators with their $i0$ and a complete error table, (iv) process-by-process statements with their inputs listed by label (DIS, SIA, thrust, DY, TMD, exclusive), (v) the assembled factorization structure `thm:appN-structure`, and (vi) a complete one-loop example with the exact remainder and a two-loop ladder that shows why grouping is necessary. The numbered inputs (`asm:appN-*`) are exactly the [A]-level content of Collins 2011 ch. 10 and of CSS, now named one by one.

**Depends on.** Ch4 `ir`: `def:ir-regions`, `def:ir-glauber`, `thm:ir-leading-regions`, `prop:ir-power-counting`, `rem:ir-regions-values`, `rem:ir-scaling-vs-bound`. Ch9 `method`: `def:method-regions`, `def:method-power-suppressed`, `def:method-neighbourhoods`, `def:method-collins-conditions`, `def:method-collinear-approx`, `def:method-soft-approx`, `def:method-subtraction`, `lem:method-ward-multi`, `prop:method-collinear-wilson`, `prop:method-soft-wilson`, `lem:method-soft-unitarity`, `prop:method-fsi` and the statements proved here. Ch10 `disall`, Ch11 `coll` (`lem:coll-final-poles`, `lem:coll-unobserved-jet`), Ch14 `tmd` (`def:tmd-unsub`, `def:tmd-soft`, `def:tmd-collins`, `lem:tmd-sqrt-identity`, `lem:tmd-soft-oneloop`, `rem:tmd-order-of-limits`), Ch15 `scet` (`def:scet-zero-bin`, pointer), Ch19 `excl` (`def:excl-da`), Ch8 `pdf` (`thm:pdf-moments-local`, `rem:pdf-ff-multren`, `prop:pdf-partonic-dr`). App O (`cor:appO-interface`, `prop:appO-tmd`) for DY. Ch2 `qcd` (`thm:prelim-composite-renorm`), App J (`prop:appJ-local-operators`).

**Box 「本附录证明了什么／没有证明什么」 (draft).**
- 证明了：`thm:appN-subtraction`（\tagR，$R_{\rm cond}$，前提 `asm:appN-contour`）；第 9 章 `lem:method-meets`、`lem:method-covering`、`prop:method-chains`、`lem:method-pairing`、`prop:method-product`、`lem:method-no-pole`、`lem:method-telescoping`（数学）、`lem:method-i0-compat`（$R_{\rm PT}$）、`lem:method-local-accuracy`、`cor:method-removing-eps`（$R_{\rm cond}$）的证明；`lem:appN-error-table`（$R_{\rm cond}$）；第 10 章 `prop:disall-two-routes`、`prop:disall-factorized-subtraction`、`thm:disall-renorm-compat`，第 14 章 `prop:tmd-zero-bin-soft`，第 19 章 `prop:excl-soft-cancel` 的证明（$R_{\rm cond}$）；`prop:appN-sia`、`prop:appN-thrust`、`prop:appN-dy`、`prop:appN-tmd-window`、`thm:appN-structure`（$R_{\rm cond}$）；`prop:appN-triangle-exact`、`prop:appN-ladder`（$R_{\rm PT}$）。
