### N.1 `sec:appN-setting` 设定 (2.5 pp)

- **定义 `def:appN-integrand` — 被积函数、幂次参数、围道与测度** (no tag). Units $Q=1$. An **integrand** $\Gamma$ is one graph, or a sum of graphs with a common loop-momentum routing (for cross sections: the cut-summed integrand of `asm:appN-cutsum`), with UV subdivergences removed at the integrand level (`asm:appN-uv`), so that $\Gamma$ is a single function on loop-momentum space, not a formal sum of graphs and counterterm graphs. $\lambda\in(0,\lambda_0]$ is the power parameter (IR regulator over $Q$, e.g. $m/Q$; under `asm:appN-cutsum` also a measured variable such as $\tau^{1/2}$ or $q_T/Q$), $L\equiv\ln(1/\lambda)$; $d=4-2\varepsilon$, $\varepsilon\in(0,\varepsilon_0]$. $\Omega$: a $dL_\Gamma$-dimensional contour (real space or a complex deformation equal to real space at large $\|k\|$), $\mu$ its measure (Jacobian included). $\mathcal V$: finite sums of rational functions of the loop momenta (propagators with their $i0$, eikonal denominators) times measurement functions. Identities in $\mathcal V$ are identities of rational functions times measurement functions, valid almost everywhere on $\Omega$; they involve no integrability. $\mathfrak S(A)$ is `def:method-power-suppressed` (Ch9). `\uses{def:method-power-suppressed}`.
- **引理 `lem:appN-suppressed-sets` — 幂次压低类的基本性质** (数学). $\mathfrak S(A)$ is a linear space; $A'\subset A$ ⇒ $\mathfrak S(A)\subset\mathfrak S(A')$; $\mathfrak S(A_1)\cap\mathfrak S(A_2)\subset\mathfrak S(A_1\cup A_2)$; for a finite cover $\Omega=\bigcup_iA_i$: $F\in\mathfrak S(A_i)\ \forall i$ ⇒ $F\in\mathfrak S(\Omega)$. Remarks to print: why an integral norm (measurement functions such as $\theta(\tau-\tau(X))$ are wrong by $O(1)$ on thin layers — pointwise bounds fail, integral bounds can hold); why uniformity in $\varepsilon$ needs a single integrand (with $\overline{\rm MS}$ counterterm graphs the cancellation is not pointwise and $\int|\Gamma|\sim1/\varepsilon$). `\uses{def:appN-integrand, def:method-power-suppressed}`. Proof: full (triangle inequality).
- *Target:* **`lem:method-meets`** (Ch9, 数学) — regions are per-line mode assignments $R(j)\in\{S,A,B,H\}$ with $S<A<H$, $S<B<H$, $A\wedge B=S$; vertex mode = supremum of adjacent line modes; pinch sets $\pi_R=\{k: l_j(k)=0\ (R(j)=S),\ l_j(k)\in\mathbb Rn_A\ (R(j)=A),\ l_j(k)\in\mathbb Rn_B\ (R(j)=B)\}$ are affine subspaces; per-line order and meet. Claim: (a) $R'\le R$ ⇒ $\pi_{R'}\subseteq\pi_R$; (b) $\pi_{R\wedge R'}=\pi_R\cap\pi_{R'}$; (c) if $\pi_R\cap\pi_{R'}\ne\emptyset$ and $R\wedge R'\in\mathcal R$ then $R\wedge R'$ is the greatest lower bound of $R,R'$ in $\mathcal R$; if $\pi_R\cap\pi_{R'}=\emptyset$ they have no common lower bound in $\mathcal R$. *Proof:* full, line by line ($\{0\}\subset\mathbb Rn_A$; $\mathbb Rn_A\cap\mathbb Rn_B=\{0\}$; $H$ imposes nothing).
- **注 `rem:appN-hard-soft-lines` — 区域集合的两条约定** (no tag). (i) Per-line meets can produce non-canonical assignments, e.g. a soft line with both ends at hard vertices; replacing such a line by zero inside $\mathsf H_R$ would create scaleless UV divergences $\int d^dk/k^2$ and violate (CS3), so $T_R$ does **not** replace soft lines with both ends in $\mathsf H_R$ (they are treated as hard; the pinch set is still defined by the labels, `lem:method-meets` unaffected). (ii) Soft lines attached to one jet only are subleading but may stay in $\mathcal R$; with rapidity-regularized TMD approximators they must stay (for bookkeeping of the convention of `rem:appN-spacelike-selfenergy`).
- **引理 `lem:appN-beta` — 邻域指数的可行选择** (数学). With $h(R)$ = length of the longest chain below $R$ in $\mathcal R$ and $h_*=1+\max_{R\ne R_{\max}}h(R)$, the choice $\beta_R=3^{h(R)-h_*}$ ($R\ne R_{\max}$) satisfies $0<\beta_R\le\frac13$ and $R'<R\ne R_{\max}$ ⇒ $\beta_R\ge3\beta_{R'}$. Examples: off-shell triangle $\beta_S=\frac19$, $\beta_{C_i}=\frac13$; two-loop planar vertex $\beta_{(S,S)}=\frac1{81}$, $\beta_{(C_i,S)}=\frac1{27}$, $\beta_{(C_i,C_i)}=\beta_{(H,S)}=\frac19$, $\beta_{(H,C_i)}=\frac13$. `\uses{def:method-neighbourhoods}`. Proof: full.
- *Target:* **`lem:method-covering`** (Ch9, 数学) — $\bigcup_{R\in\mathcal R}U_R=\Omega$ with $N_R=\{\sigma_{R(j)}(l_j)\le\lambda^{\beta_R}\ \forall R(j)\ne H\}$, $\sigma_A(l)=|l^-|+|\mathbf l_T|$, $\sigma_B(l)=|l^+|+|\mathbf l_T|$, $\sigma_S(l)=\|l\|\equiv|l^+|+|l^-|+|\mathbf l_T|$, $U_R=N_R\setminus\bigcup_{R'<R}N_{R'}$, $N_{R_{\max}}=\Omega$. *Proof:* full ($\mathcal A(k)=\{R:k\in N_R\}\ni R_{\max}$; a minimal element $R_0$ of $\mathcal A(k)$ gives $k\in U_{R_0}$). If a jet cannot be used to deform away from the Glauber region (SIDIS target jet), add for soft lines attached to it the condition $|n_A\cdot l|\ge\lambda^{\beta_R}|\mathbf l_T|$; removed points are assigned to some $U_{R'}$ by this lemma, and `asm:appN-separation` must hold there.
- **例 `ex:appN-vertex-regions` — 两圈平面顶点的九个领头区域**. Inner gluon $k_1$, outer gluon $k_2$: of the 16 mode combinations exactly 9 are leading pinches, $(H,H),(H,C_1),(H,C_2),(C_1,C_1),(C_2,C_2),(H,S),(C_1,S),(C_2,S),(S,S)$ (line modes listed per region; e.g. $(C_1,S)$: $J_1=\{k_1,p_1-k_2,p_1-k_1-k_2\}$, $H=\{p_2+k_1+k_2\}$ plus the photon vertex, $S=\{k_2\}$); the set is closed under per-line meets, e.g. $(C_1,C_1)\wedge(H,S)=(C_1,S)$, $(H,C_1)\wedge(H,C_2)=(H,S)$, $(C_1,C_1)\wedge(C_2,C_2)=(S,S)$; 12 covering relations including the non-obvious $(C_1,C_1)<(H,C_1)$. Only meet closure is checked here, not (GA3)(GA4) or (CS1)–(CS3). `\uses{def:method-regions, lem:method-meets, lem:appN-beta}`. Figure `fig:method-hasse` (owner Ch9). Check: `refdoc/work_R6/check_algebra.py` (Hasse diagram, $\beta_R$, Lemmas on the 9-element poset).

### N.2 `sec:appN-inputs` 几何—解析输入 (2.5 pp)

Notation: $t_R\equiv\lambda^{\beta_<(R)}$, $\beta_<(R)\equiv\max_{R'<R}\beta_{R'}$ (0 if none) — a lower bound on the distance to smaller pinch sets on $U_R$; $s_R\equiv\lambda^{\beta_R}$ — an upper bound on the small components on $U_R$. Names (GA1)–(GA4) replace the reference's (A1)–(A4) (no clash with the tag [A]).

- **假设 `asm:appN-contour` — (GA1) 围道** [\tagA]. There is a contour $\Omega$ (a complex deformation of real space, equal to it as $\|k\|\to\infty$) such that (a) for $\Gamma$ and every $C_R\Gamma$ the homotopy from real space to $\Omega$ crosses no singularity, so the $\Omega$-integral (when it exists) equals the real-space integral defined with each denominator's own $i0$ (as $i\eta$, $\eta\to0^+$); (b) each $\int_\Omega C_R\Gamma\,d\mu$ (defined for $\varepsilon\in(0,\varepsilon_0]$ by (CS3)) continues to a meromorphic function near $\varepsilon=0$. `\uses{def:appN-integrand, def:method-subtraction}`. Status and users: `ASSUMPTIONS.md`.
- **定义 `def:appN-meet-closed` — (GA2) 对逐线交封闭的区域集合** (no tag). $\mathcal R$ is meet-closed if $R,R'\in\mathcal R$ and $\pi_R\cap\pi_{R'}\ne\emptyset$ imply $R\wedge R'\in\mathcal R$. It is a **choice** of $\mathcal R$ (close the leading regions of `thm:ir-leading-regions` under per-line meets), used only in `lem:method-pairing` and `prop:method-product`; `thm:appN-subtraction` does not need it. `\uses{def:method-regions, lem:method-meets}`.
- **假设 `asm:appN-envelope` — (GA3) 幂次计数包络** [\tagA]. For every $R_0\in\mathcal R$ there is $\Phi_{R_0}\ge0$ on $U_{R_0}$ with $\int_{U_{R_0}}\Phi_{R_0}\,d\mu\le CL^K$ uniformly in $\varepsilon$, such that (i) every partially approximated integrand of the telescoping sum of `lem:method-telescoping` (each factor $i$ taken as $f_i$, as $T_{R_0}f_i$, or as the envelope $g_i$ of rows 3, 4, 6 of `tab:method-errors`) is $\le C\Phi_{R_0}$ on $U_{R_0}\cap\{\|k\|\le1\}$; (ii) on $U_{R_0}\cap\{\|k\|\ge1\}$ every term of the telescoping sum is uniformly integrable with integral $\le C\lambda^aL^K$ (the error factor itself need not decay); (iii) (pointwise boost bound) on $U_{R_0}\cap\{\|k\|\le1\}$, for rows 3, 4, 6: $|f_i-T_{R_0}f_i|\le C(s_{R_0}/t_{R_0})\,g_i$. `\uses{def:method-neighbourhoods, def:method-collinear-approx, def:method-soft-approx}`.
- **假设 `asm:appN-separation` — (GA4) 分离性** [\tagA]. On $U_R$: (i) every line of $\mathsf H_R$ has $|l^2-M_l^2|\ge c\,t_R^2$ (Łojasiewicz-type inequality with exponent 2: "distance $\gtrsim t_R$ from smaller pinch sets" ⇒ "virtuality $\gtrsim t_R^2$"); (ii) every jet line changed by $T_R$ (lines from $\mathsf J_{R,A}$ entering $\mathsf H_R$ that receive projections; lines carrying a soft momentum of $R$) has $|l^+|\ge c\,t_R$ ($B$: $|l^-|$) — $\mathcal R$ contains the (generally subleading) endpoint assignments that make such lines soft; (iii) for every soft momentum $k$ of $R$ and every $A$-jet line $l=p+k$ it flows through ($p$ the $A$-collinear part): $|k^+k^-|\ge c\,\mathbf k_T^2$ and $|\tilde D|\ge c\,(|p^+||k^-|+v_A(p))$, with $v_A(p)\equiv|p^+p^-|+\mathbf p_T^2$ and $\tilde D=(p+\pi_-(k))^2-M_l^2+i0=p^2-M_l^2+2p^+k^-+i0$; $B$ jets with $+\leftrightarrow-$. The first inequality of (iii) says that $\Omega\cap U_R$ contains no Glauber region. `\uses{def:method-neighbourhoods, def:ir-glauber}`.
- **注 `rem:appN-inputs-status` — 各输入的地位** (no tag). (GA2) is a choice. (GA3)(i)(ii) are the integral form of the power counting of Sterman and Collins (`Sterman1978a`, `LibbySterman1978a`, `Collins2011` ch. 5), never written in this integral form at all orders; (GA3)(iii) is the pointwise form of the boost argument, which gives scalings, not pointwise inequalities (where one component vanishes and another does not, "bad suppressed relative to good" has no pointwise meaning), hence the bounds relative to envelopes (`rem:ir-scaling-vs-bound`). (GA1) is genuine analytic input: for $e^+e^-$ and DIS it follows from pole-location arguments (`lem:coll-final-poles`, `lem:method-i0-compat`); for DY it fails for single cut graphs and is supplied in the one-sided form by App O (`cor:appO-interface`). (GA4)(i): the exponent 2 is an independent assumption. (GA4)(ii) requires endpoint assignments in $\mathcal R$; in the scalar triangle only the external lines $p_1,p_2$ are changed (automatic). (GA4)(iii)-first = the corrected no-Glauber condition, compatible with `lem:method-i0-compat`: the deformation of one component is limited by the soft line's own pole to $\kappa\sim\mathbf k_T^2/|k^+|$, which gives exactly $|k^+k^-|\gtrsim\mathbf k_T^2$; it **replaces** the common claim "$\kappa\sim|\mathbf k_T|$" (OUR-4, misconception M31); the existence of simultaneous deformations of several soft momenta is proven only for $\mathcal G_0$ and graded configurations (`prop:appO-target`). (GA4)(iii)-second: reasonable at finite distance from true pinches in IR-regulated amplitudes, verified in the triangle (§N.7), unproven in general.

### N.3 `sec:appN-theorem` 定理与组合恒等式 (3.5 pp)

$T_R$ ($R\in\mathcal R$) are linear maps on $\mathcal V$ (§N.4); $C_R\Gamma=T_R(\Gamma-\sum_{R'<R}C_{R'}\Gamma)$ (`def:method-subtraction`); $X_{R_0}\equiv\Gamma-\sum_{R<R_0}C_R\Gamma$, $C_{R_0}\Gamma=T_{R_0}X_{R_0}$. The Collins conditions (CS1)–(CS3) are `def:method-collins-conditions` (Ch9): (CS1)$_{R_0}$ $(1-T_{R_0})X_{R_0}\in\mathfrak S(U_{R_0})$; (CS2)$_{R_0}$ $C_R\Gamma\in\mathfrak S(U_{R_0})$ for all $R\not\le R_0$; (CS3) $\Gamma\in L^1(\Omega)$ with $\int|\Gamma|$ bounded uniformly in $\varepsilon$, and $C_R\Gamma\in L^1(\Omega)$ for all $R$ and $\varepsilon\in(0,\varepsilon_0]$.

- **定理 `thm:appN-subtraction` — 减除定理（条件形式）** [R, R_cond]. （$R_{\rm cond}$：在所列编号假设之下证明完整；结论的地位不高于其中最弱的假设。）前提：\cref{asm:appN-contour}(a)。（本定理只涉及部分子态。）Let $\Gamma$ be an integrand with region set $\mathcal R$. If (CS1)$_{R_0}$ and (CS2)$_{R_0}$ hold for every $R_0\in\mathcal R$ and (CS3) holds, then
  $\int_\Omega\big|\Gamma-\sum_{R\in\mathcal R}C_R\Gamma\big|\,d\mu\le C\lambda^aL^K$ for all $\lambda\in(0,\lambda_0]$, $\varepsilon\in(0,\varepsilon_0]$;
  in particular $\int\Gamma=\sum_R\int C_R\Gamma+O(\lambda^aL^K)$, each $\int C_R\Gamma$ converges absolutely and equals the real-space integral in which every denominator keeps its own $i0$. `\uses{asm:appN-contour, def:method-collins-conditions, def:method-subtraction, lem:method-covering, lem:appN-suppressed-sets}`. Proof: full and deliberately elementary — split $\mathcal R$ into $R<R_0$, $R=R_0$, $R\not\le R_0$; pointwise on $U_{R_0}$: $r\equiv\Gamma-\sum_RC_R\Gamma=(1-T_{R_0})X_{R_0}-\sum_{R\not\le R_0}C_R\Gamma\in\mathfrak S(U_{R_0})$ by (CS1)(CS2); finite cover (`lem:method-covering`, `lem:appN-suppressed-sets`) ⇒ $r\in\mathfrak S(\Omega)$; (CS3) ⇒ linearity of the integral; the last sentence by (GA1)(a). All content is in (CS1)(CS2). Error type E-N. Content grade [A].
- *Target:* **`prop:method-chains`** (Ch9, algebraic identity, no tag) — chains $c=\{R_1>R_2>\dots>R_j\}$, $T_c\equiv T_{R_1}\cdots T_{R_j}$ (largest leftmost, acts last), $T_\emptyset=1$; $\mathcal O_{\mathcal P}\equiv\sum_{c\subset\mathcal P}(-1)^{|c|}T_c$. Then $C_R\Gamma=\sum_{c:\max c=R}(-1)^{|c|-1}T_c\Gamma$, $X_{R_0}=\mathcal O_{\downarrow R_0}\Gamma$ ($\downarrow R_0=\{X<R_0\}$), $r=\mathcal O_{\mathcal R}\Gamma$; for totally ordered $\mathcal P=\{P_1<\dots<P_n\}$: $\mathcal O_{\mathcal P}=(1-T_{P_n})\cdots(1-T_{P_1})$. *Proof:* full (induction along the order; $(R',c')\mapsto\{R\}\cup c'$ is a bijection onto chains with maximum $R$ and length ≥ 2).
- *Target:* **`lem:method-pairing`** (Ch9, 数学) — pairing decomposition: $\mathcal R$ meet-closed; fix $R_0$; $\uparrow R=\{X>R\}$; $\mathcal I'$ ($\mathcal I''$) = regions incomparable with $R_0$ whose pinch sets meet (do not meet) $\pi_{R_0}$; $R_\wedge\equiv R\wedge R_0$. Pointwise
  $r=\mathcal O_{\uparrow R_0}\big[(1-T_{R_0})X_{R_0}\big]-\sum_{R\in\mathcal I'}\mathcal O_{\uparrow R}T_R\big[(1-T_{R_\wedge})X_{R_\wedge}\big]-\sum_{R\in\mathcal I''}\mathcal O_{\uparrow R}T_R\Gamma$.
  *Proof:* full (split chains: $\mathcal C_1$ with all elements comparable with $R_0$; $\mathcal C_2',\mathcal C_2''$ according to the smallest element $R^*(c)$ incomparable with $R_0$; elements below $R^*$ are $<R_0$, hence $\le R_\wedge$ by `lem:method-meets`(c); none for $R^*\in\mathcal I''$). Remark to print: expanding the alternating sums $\mathcal O_{\uparrow R}$ regenerates non-integrable terms (`prop:appN-ladder`(c)); the decomposition says which combinations must be estimated, it does not reduce the problem to single approximation errors. Optional figure `fig:appN-chain-pairing` (FIGURES_INDEX_v2 §4.6).
- *Target:* **`prop:method-product`** (Ch9, 数学) — product formula: assume all pairwise pinch sets intersect, $\mathcal R$ meet-closed, each $T_R$ idempotent, (Com) $T_RT_{R'}=T_{R'}T_R$, (Abs) $T_RT_{R'}=T_RT_{R\wedge R'}T_{R'}$ for incomparable $R,R'$. Then $C_R\Gamma=T_R\prod_{R'<R}(1-T_{R'})\Gamma$, $X_{R_0}=\prod_{R<R_0}(1-T_R)\Gamma$, $r=\prod_{R\in\mathcal R}(1-T_R)\Gamma$; and (CS1)$_{R_0}$ ⇔ $\prod_{R\le R_0}(1-T_R)\Gamma\in\mathfrak S(U_{R_0})$, (CS2)$_{R_0}$ ⇔ $G_{R_0}(R)\equiv T_R\prod_{R'\in\downarrow R\cup\{R_0\}}(1-T_{R'})\Gamma\in\mathfrak S(U_{R_0})$ for $R\not\le R_0$. *Proof:* full — Möbius function of the meet-semilattice: (Com)(Abs) ⇒ $T_{\mathcal S}=T_{\bar{\mathcal S}}$ (meet closure); $\prod(1-T_R)=\sum_{\mathcal K}T_{\mathcal K}\sum_{\mathcal S:\bar{\mathcal S}=\mathcal K}(-1)^{|\mathcal S|}$ over meet-closed $\mathcal K$; with $\mathcal M\subset\mathcal K$ the meet-irreducible elements, $\bar{\mathcal S}=\mathcal K$ iff $\mathcal M\subset\mathcal S\subset\mathcal K$, so the inner sum is $(-1)^{|\mathcal M|}\sum_{\mathcal A\subset\mathcal K\setminus\mathcal M}(-1)^{|\mathcal A|}=0$ unless $\mathcal K=\mathcal M$, i.e. $\mathcal K$ is a chain; for incomparable $R$: $T_R(1-T_{R_\wedge})T_{R_0}=0$. Remark: $(1-T_{R_0})$ supplies the small factor on $U_{R_0}$ (`lem:appN-error-table`); the other $(1-T_{R'})$ supply no smallness but prevent new unregulated pinches when $T_R$ removes IR regulators — why single chain terms $T_c(1-T_{R_0})\Gamma$ are not integrable while $G_{R_0}(R)$ is. Reader check: the 9-element poset with a coordinate-zeroing model (`check_algebra.py`).
- **注 `rem:appN-forest` — 与 BPHZ 森林公式的正确类比** (no tag). The chain expansion resembles Zimmermann's forest formula (`Zimmermann1969`) but differs: forests are families of pairwise nested **or disjoint** renormalization parts, whereas chains correspond only to nested families; incomparable regions never appear in one chain even if their pinch sets intersect, and they interact only through meets (`lem:method-pairing`) — the analogue of overlapping divergences. Under (Com)(Abs) the chain expansion is the product $\prod(1-T_R)$, as the forest formula for a single nested string. As in BPHZ, rigorous estimates exist only for the whole product or suitable groupings, never for expanded single terms (critic O10; misconception M35).
- *Target:* **`lem:method-no-pole`** (Ch9, 数学) — a function meromorphic near $\varepsilon=0$ and bounded on $(0,\varepsilon_0]$ has no pole at 0 ($\varepsilon^kF\to c\ne0$ would contradict boundedness). *Proof:* full.
- *Target:* **`cor:method-removing-eps`** (Ch9, [R, R_cond: `thm:appN-subtraction`, `asm:appN-contour`(b)]) — if $\int\Gamma$ is analytic near $\varepsilon=0$, then $F(\varepsilon)=\sum_R\int C_R\Gamma$ has no pole at $\varepsilon=0$ and $|\int\Gamma-F|_{\varepsilon=0}\le C\lambda^aL^K$. *Proof:* full ($F$ meromorphic by (GA1)(b); on $(0,\varepsilon_0]$, $F=\int\Gamma-\int r$ is bounded by the uniform bound and continuity of $\int\Gamma$; `lem:method-no-pole`; the bound survives $\varepsilon\to0^+$). Meaning: the UV poles of single subtraction terms cancel in the sum over $R$, graph by graph (`prop:appN-triangle-exact`(c)); their organisation into renormalization constants is §N.5 (`thm:disall-renorm-compat`).
- **假设 `asm:appN-values` — 发散链项的取值规则** (no tag: convention). There is a linear rule $T_c\Gamma\mapsto\langle T_c\Gamma\rangle$ (e.g. besides DR, raise every denominator changed by an approximator to the power $1+\delta$, continue in $(\varepsilon,\delta)$, then $\delta\to0$) with **(V1)** $\sum_{c:\max c=R}(-1)^{|c|-1}\langle T_c\Gamma\rangle=\int_\Omega C_R\Gamma\,d\mu$ (compatibility with the absolutely convergent subtraction terms) and **(V2)** scaleless chain terms have value zero. Used only to identify subtraction-term values with term-by-term region-method values ("subtracted jet function = unsubtracted", "hard coefficient = on-shell form factor with IR poles removed"); no rigorous estimate uses it. (V1) is non-trivial because single chain terms are not absolutely convergent for any $\varepsilon$ (`ex:method-naive-induction`(c)), so $\int C_R\Gamma=\sum\pm\int T_c\Gamma$ is not linearity of the integral. Renamed items: the reference's (D1)(D2) are (V1)(V2). `\uses{def:method-subtraction, prop:method-chains}`.
- **假设 `asm:appN-collins` — 一般图的 Collins 条件** [\tagA]. For every leading-power integrand $\Gamma$ of the process (a single graph, or a family of graphs with a common routing), including multi-direction processes and non-adapted routing, with its region set $\mathcal R$ (meet-closed, containing the endpoint assignments required by `asm:appN-separation`): (CS1)$_{R_0}$ and (CS2)$_{R_0}$ hold for every $R_0\in\mathcal R$, and (CS3) holds. `\uses{def:method-collins-conditions, def:appN-meet-closed}`. Full status/users/upgrade path in `ASSUMPTIONS.md`; in the text the 「地位」 field lists what **is** provable: (CS1) for minimal $R_0$ (`lem:method-local-accuracy`); under (Com)(Abs) the reduction to grouped power counting (`prop:method-product`); the triangle and the ladder (§N.7, slots X2a–X2c); the obstacles (term-by-term conditions false: `prop:appN-ladder`(c); (Abs) failure: `rem:method-abs-failure`; non-adapted routing: `rem:appN-routing`); `CollinsSoperSterman1989` §10.1 "there is as yet in the literature no complete and systematic subtraction procedure in QCD… even in the case of deeply inelastic scattering" (survey item 6, [txt]).

### N.4 `sec:appN-approximators` 近似子、误差与 Ward 结构 (3.5 pp)

Recap of the idempotent approximators of Ch9 (`def:method-collinear-approx`, `def:method-soft-approx`; no new environment): hard–collinear — in all factors of $\mathsf H_R$, $k_e\to\pi_+(k_e)$ for lines entering from $\mathsf J_{R,A}$ ($\pi_-$ from $\mathsf J_{R,B}$), IR regulators set to zero in $\mathsf H_R$; quark projection $\mathcal P_+=\frac12\gamma^-\gamma^+$ (out-flowing $\mathcal P_-$, since $\overline{\psi_+}=\bar\psi\mathcal P_-$); gluon projector $\mathcal P_A{}^\mu{}_\nu=g_T{}^\mu{}_\nu+n_A^\mu n_{B\nu}$, $H_\mu J^\mu\to H^-J^+-\mathbf H_T\cdot\mathbf J_T$ (longitudinal K term + physical transverse term); soft — $k\to\pi_-(k)$ in all factors of $\mathsf J_{R,A}$, projection $n_A^\mu n_{B\nu}$, $\mathsf S_\mu\mathsf J_A^\mu\to\mathsf S^-\mathsf J_A^+$; factors of $\mathsf S_R$ kept exactly. **No new denominators** are created: the soft-approximated $\tilde D=p^2-M^2+2p^+k^-+i0$ inherits its $i0$; Wilson-line denominators $1/(n\cdot k\pm i0)$ arise only after the Ward identity; the collinear-PDF prescription is irrelevant ($W[w,+\infty n_B]W[+\infty n_B,0]=W[w,-\infty n_B]W[-\infty n_B,0]=W[w,0]$), the TMD one is not.

- **定义 `def:appN-tmd-approx` — TMD 近似子与快度正规化** (no tag). For $q_T\ll Q$ ($\lambda=q_T/Q$; modes $A$, $B$ collinear, soft $Q(\lambda,\lambda,\lambda)$): $T_R^{(y_A,y_B)}$ as in Ch9 with three changes: (i) in $\delta^{(2)}(\mathbf q_T-\sum\mathbf l_T)$ all collinear and soft transverse momenta are kept exactly (only $\mathsf H_R$ factors are projected); (ii) the soft projection uses the space-like $n_A(y_A)=(1^+,-e^{-2y_A},\mathbf 0_T)$: $\pi_-^{(y_A)}(k)=(n_A(y_A)\cdot k)\,n_B$, projection $n_A(y_A)^\mu n_{B\nu}$ ($B$ side: $n_B(y_B)=(-e^{2y_B},1^-,\mathbf 0_T)$); (iii) in the hard–collinear approximation the $A$-collinear entering momentum is projected by $\pi_+^{(y_B)}(k)=(n_B(y_B)\cdot k)\,n_A$ and the longitudinal term becomes $n_A^\mu n_B(y_B)_\nu$ ($g_T+n_A\otimes n_B(y_B)$ still idempotent) — Wilson-line direction $n_B\to n_B(y_B)$. Light-like limit $y_A\to+\infty$, $y_B\to-\infty$ after combining (`def:tmd-collins`). Relative errors $O(e^{-2(y_A-y_k)})$, $O(e^{-2(y_k-y_B)})$ ($y_k$ the gluon rapidity). Caveats: $\pi_+^{(y_B)}\pi_-^{(y_A)}(k)\ne0$, so (Com)(Abs) hold only to $O(e^{-2|y|})$; the collinear factor of $T_R^{(y_A,y_B)}$ at fixed $x$ differs from the light-front matrix element by $e^{2(y_B-y_k)}$ (`rem:tmd-order-of-limits`). `\uses{def:method-collinear-approx, def:method-soft-approx, def:tmd-collins}`.
- **定义 `def:appN-excl-approx` — 遍举近似子与分布振幅投影** (no tag). For a meson jet entering $\mathsf H_R$ through a valence pair with momenta $k$, $P-k$: replace them in $\mathsf H_R$ by $\pi_+(k)=xP$, $\pi_+(P-k)=\bar xP$ and insert the DA projection $\mathsf J_{\alpha\beta}\mapsto\frac14(\gamma^-\gamma_5)_{\alpha\beta}\,{\rm Tr}[\gamma_5\gamma^+\mathsf J]\otimes\frac{\delta_{ij}}{N_c}{\rm Tr}_c[\cdot]$ (idempotent: $\frac14{\rm Tr}[\gamma_5\gamma^+\gamma^-\gamma_5]=1$; projects onto the $\gamma^+\gamma_5$ structure of `def:excl-da` and onto the colour singlet; $\gamma^-\gamma_5$ is the $\slashed p\gamma_5$ structure since $\slashed p=p^+\gamma^-$); longitudinal gluons with $\mathcal P_A$; soft gluons as in `def:method-soft-approx`. Endpoint region $R_{\rm end}$: one line of the pair soft ($x\to0$ or $\bar x\to0$), $R_{\rm end}<R$. `\uses{def:method-collinear-approx, def:method-soft-approx, def:excl-da}`.
- **引理 `lem:appN-algebra` — 近似子的代数** (数学). Suppose global loop momenta can be chosen such that for every $R$ and every factor $f$ the momentum substitutions set certain light-cone components of the loop and external momenta to zero (set $Z_R(f)$; "adapted routing"). Then (i) $T_R^2=T_R$; (ii) $T_RT_{R'}=T_{R'}T_R$ and $Z_{RR'}(f)=Z_R(f)\cup Z_{R'}(f)$; (iii) for momentum substitutions and mass zeroing, (Abs) ⇔ $Z_{R\wedge R'}(f)\subseteq Z_R(f)\cup Z_{R'}(f)$ for every factor; this inclusion holds for per-line meets except possibly in the configuration of `rem:appN-abs-edge`; (iv) for polarization projections the longitudinal (K) part is absorbed exactly but the transverse $g_T$ part is not: if a direction-flipping line $e$ exists ($R(e)=A$, $R'(e)=B$), $T_RT_{R'}-T_RT_{R\wedge R'}T_{R'}$ is the coupling of the transverse polarization of $e$ at that connection — leading if $e$ is a minimal (physical) connection (gluon-initiated subprocess), suppressed by $\sigma(e)/|e^\pm|$ only inside grouped expressions otherwise. Scalar vertices (triangle) and one-direction processes (DIS) have no flipping lines: (Abs) exact; one-loop graphs, ladders and the two-loop planar vertex have adapted routings. `\uses{def:method-collinear-approx, def:method-soft-approx, lem:method-meets}`. Proof: full (case analysis: commuting idempotent coordinate/mass zeroings; constant projectors; factor-by-factor check of (iii); $\mathcal P_A$ keeps $V^-W^+$, $\mathcal P_B$/soft keep $V^+W^-$). Reader check: projector algebra with explicit Dirac matrices (`check_algebra.py`).
- **注 `rem:appN-abs-edge` — (Abs) 的一个未被覆盖的情形** (no tag) — **[PENDING-VERIFY X2d]**. Configuration: a line $e$ soft in $R$ with both ends at hard vertices of $R$ (so $T_R$ leaves it alone by `rem:appN-hard-soft-lines`(i)), $A$-collinear in $R'$ entering $\mathsf H_{R'}$ at factor $f$ ($T_{R'}$ zeroes only $e^-$, $\mathbf e_T$ in $f$), soft in $R\wedge R'$ with one endpoint in $\mathsf J_{R\wedge R',A}$ (possible if the other endpoint $v$ has lines $(e,g,h)$ of modes $(S,H,H)$ in $R$ and $(A,A,A)$ in $R'$, hence $(S,A,A)$ in $R\wedge R'$): then $T_{R\wedge R'}$ zeroes all components of $e$ in $f$ including $e^+\notin Z_R(f)\cup Z_{R'}(f)$, and (Abs) fails by a momentum-substitution term. Fix: exclude such assignments from $\mathcal R$, or include the case in `asm:appN-collins`. Plausible; not checked whether such an $R$ arises as a meet of leading regions (X2d).
- **注 `rem:appN-routing` — 没有适配动量分配时** (no tag). In general multi-loop graphs different regions' hard subgraphs have different internal loop momenta; substitutions are then not coordinate zeroings in global variables, and (Com)(Abs) hold only modulo shifts of hard-subgraph loop momenta (integral unchanged, integrand and integral norm changed). `thm:appN-subtraction` needs only (CS1)–(CS3), but `prop:method-product` is not literally usable; the verification of (CS1)(CS2) is then part of `asm:appN-collins`.
- *Target:* **`lem:method-i0-compat`** (Ch9, [R, R_PT]) — a soft momentum $k$ flows through a string of $J_A$ lines $l=p+k$ ($p$ $A$-collinear) along which all $p^+$ have the same sign $\eta=\pm1$ (all final-state: $\eta=+1$), $|k^+|<|p^+|$; deform $k^-\to k^-+i\eta\kappa$ ($\kappa\ge0$). (a) ${\rm Im}\,D=2\eta\kappa(p^++k^+)\ge0$ and ${\rm Im}\,\tilde D=2\eta\kappa p^+\ge0$ (exact, soft-approximated and $M\to0$ denominators never vanish along the deformation, so this part of (GA1)(a) holds for $C_R\Gamma$ built from them); (b) the soft line's own propagator $k^2+i0=2k^+k^--\mathbf k_T^2+i0$ has ${\rm Im}\,k^2=2\eta\kappa k^+$, non-negative only for $\eta k^+\ge0$; for $\eta k^+<0$ its pole $k^-=(\mathbf k_T^2-i0)/(2k^+)$ lies on the deformation side and the contour must return near ${\rm Re}\,k^-\approx\mathbf k_T^2/(2k^+)$: the achievable amplitude is $\kappa\sim\mathbf k_T^2/|k^+|$, **not** $|\mathbf k_T|$, giving only $|k^+k^-|\gtrsim\mathbf k_T^2$; if $k$ also flows through a line with opposite-sign $p^+$ the two denominators require opposite deformations — the Glauber pinch. *Proof:* full (direct computation). Illustration: in the triangle, for $k=(z,\lambda^2/z,\lambda)$, $\lambda^2\ll z\ll1$, the $D_g$ and $D_1$ poles lie on opposite sides, $\sim\lambda^2/z$ apart, and the contour can cross the real axis only in that gap. Pointer: `fig:method-glauber-direction` (Ch9), App O `lem:appO-no-crossing` (two-component deformation).
- **引理 `lem:appN-error-table` — 误差表各行的证明** [R, R_cond]. （$R_{\rm cond}$ …）前提：\cref{asm:appN-separation}、\cref{asm:appN-envelope}(iii)。（本引理只涉及部分子态。）Let $\beta_R\ge3\beta_<(R)$ (`lem:appN-beta`). On $U_R\cap\{\|k\|\le1\}$ the terms deleted by $T_R$ from single factors obey the bounds of `tab:method-errors` (Ch9): rows 1 (hard–collinear line of $\mathsf H_R$: deleted $2(h+\pi_+k_e)\cdot(k_e-\pi_+k_e)+\dots$, relative $s_R/t_R^2$), 2 (masses and external $p^-$: relative $\lambda^2/t_R^2$), 5 (soft, $J_A$ line $p+k$: deleted $2p^-k^+-2\mathbf p_T\cdot\mathbf k_T+k^2$, kept $p^2-M^2+2p^+k^-$, relative $(s_R/t_R)^{1/2}$), 7 (TMD rapidity regularization: relative $e^{-2(y_A-y_k)}+e^{-2(y_k-y_B)}$), 8 (exclusive valence pair, as row 1 with $k_e^+=xP^+$) are pointwise relative bounds $|f-T_Rf|\le\delta|T_Rf|$ using only (GA4); rows 3, 4, 6 (quark projection, gluon projection, soft polarization: absolute $(s_R/t_R)\|\mathsf H\|\mathcal J_+$ resp. $(s_R/t_R)\|\mathsf S\|\mathcal J_+$) are absolute bounds relative to an envelope (= (GA3)(iii)); row 9 (event-shape measurement $\tau(X)-\tau_R(X)$, thin layer) is an integral bound (needs `asm:appN-cutsum`(CU3), applied in `prop:appN-thrust`). Except row 7, all $\delta\le C\big(\frac{s_R}{t_R^2}+\frac{\lambda^2}{t_R^2}+s_R+(\frac{s_R}{t_R})^{1/2}\big)\le C'\lambda^{a_R}$, $a_R=\frac13\beta_R$ ($R_{\max}$: only $\lambda^2/t_R^2$, $a=2-2\beta_<(R_{\max})$); row 7 is estimated only on $Y\ge c_0L$, $|\mathbf k_T|\ge\lambda^{1+a}$ (`prop:appN-tmd-window`). For $\|k\|\ge1$ relative errors need not be small; control there is (GA3)(ii). `\uses{asm:appN-separation, asm:appN-envelope, lem:appN-beta, def:method-collinear-approx, def:method-soft-approx, def:appN-tmd-approx, def:appN-excl-approx}`. Proof: full, row by row — row 1: $l=h+\sum_ec_ek_e$, $\delta_e=k_e-\pi_+(k_e)=(0,k_e^-,\mathbf k_{Te})$, $|{\rm exact}-{\rm kept}|\le2\|l\|\sum\sigma_A(k_e)+C\sum\sigma_A(k_e)^2+M_{\rm IR}^2\le C(s_R+\lambda^2)$, (GA4)(i): exact $\ge ct_R^2$, kept $\ge\frac c2t_R^2$; rows 3, 4: the factor $s_R/t_R$ from the boost argument (bad components carry $|\mathbf k_{Te}|/|k_e^+|$, $\mathsf J^-/\mathsf J^+\sim|k_e^-|/|k_e^+|$ with $|\mathbf k_{Te}|,|k_e^-|\le s_R$, $|k_e^+|\ge ct_R$ by (GA4)(ii)); row 5: with (GA4)(iii) $\mathbf k_T^2\le|k^+||k^-|/c$ and $|\tilde D|\ge c(|p^+||k^-|+v_A(p))$: $|k^2|\le C|k^+||k^-|$, $|2p^-k^+|\le2v_A(p)|k^+|/|p^+|$, and with $\alpha=(|k^+|/|p^+|)^{1/2}$, $2|\mathbf p_T||\mathbf k_T|\le\alpha v_A(p)+\alpha|p^+||k^-|/c$ ⇒ $|\Delta|\le C'(|k^+|/|p^+|)^{1/2}|\tilde D|\le C(s_R/t_R)^{1/2}|\tilde D|$ (no on-shellness needed); row 6 as row 4 with factor $\sigma_A(p)/|p^+|$, absolute because the kept term can vanish (soft gluon with both ends on the same jet: $S^\mu\propto n_A^\mu$, $S^-=0$ — subleading assignments); row 7: $n_A(y_A)\cdot k-n_A\cdot k=-e^{-2y_A}k^+$; row 8: $k\notin N_{R_{\rm end}}$ gives $x\ge ct_R$ by (GA4)(ii); row 9: in the scaling of $R$, $\tau(X)=\tau_R(X)[1+O(s_R)]$, so $\theta(\tau-\tau(X))$ changes only in a layer of width $O(s_R\tau)$; finally $s_R/t_R^2=\lambda^{\beta_R-2\beta_<}\le\lambda^{\beta_R/3}$, $(s_R/t_R)^{1/2}=\lambda^{(\beta_R-\beta_<)/2}\le\lambda^{\beta_R/3}$, $\lambda^2/t_R^2\le\lambda^{4/3}$. Error types: rows 1, 2, 5, 7, 8 E-P; rows 3, 4, 6 E-P relative to an envelope; row 9 E-N.
- *Target:* **`lem:method-telescoping`** (Ch9, 数学) — $(1-T)\prod_if_i=\sum_i\big[\prod_{j<i}Tf_j\big](f_i-Tf_i)\big[\prod_{j>i}f_j\big]$ (projections $\mathcal P$, $1-\mathcal P$ count as factors). *Proof:* full.
- *Target:* **`lem:method-local-accuracy`** (Ch9, [R, R_cond: `asm:appN-envelope`, `asm:appN-separation`]) — $(1-T_{R_0})\Gamma\in\mathfrak S(U_{R_0})$ for every $R_0$; in particular (CS1)$_{R_0}$ for minimal $R_0$ ($X_{R_0}=\Gamma$). *Proof:* full — on $U_{R_0}\cap\{\|k\|\le1\}$ each term of the telescoping sum is $\le C\lambda^a$ times a partially approximated integrand $\le C\Phi_{R_0}$ ((GA3)(i), `lem:appN-error-table`); row 9 by (CU3); TMD row 7 by `prop:appN-tmd-window` and (T1); on $\|k\|\ge1$ (GA3)(ii). Remark to print: for non-minimal $R_0$, (CS1) also needs accuracy on smaller subtraction terms and (CS2) the suppression of larger/incomparable ones — under (Com)(Abs) the grouped expressions of `prop:method-product`, whose telescoping still contains a small factor but whose other factors are altered by other approximations and need the $(1-T_{R'})$ factors for integrability; the "term-by-term stability" condition $T_c(1-T_{R_0})\Gamma\in\mathfrak S(U_{R_0})$ fails (`prop:appN-ladder`(c); `prop:appN-triangle-exact`).
- *Target content of* **`rem:method-abs-failure`** (Ch9, no tag): `lem:appN-algebra`(iv) + `rem:appN-abs-edge`; consequence: the general product formula is part of `asm:appN-collins`.
- **假设 `asm:appN-ward-product` — Ward 恒等式的乘积结构与减除的重组** [\tagA]. At fixed order, after summing all graphs and all attachments of longitudinal and soft gluons: **(WI1)** each chain term's approximated integrand factorizes by the Ward identities (`lem:method-ward-multi`, `prop:method-collinear-wilson`, `prop:method-soft-wilson`) into "hard ⊗ collinear matrix elements with Wilson lines ⊗ soft matrix element", and the Ward identities apply equally to integrands already modified by other approximators; **(WI2)** grouped by the order of the $A$-collinear part, $\sum_{\rm graphs,R}C_R\Gamma$ reorganizes into the products used in `prop:tmd-zero-bin-soft`(b) and `thm:appN-structure`. Renamed items: the reference's (W1)(W2) are (WI1)(WI2) here (no clash with (W1)–(W4) of `asm:intro-wightman`). `\uses{lem:method-ward-multi, prop:method-collinear-wilson, prop:method-soft-wilson, def:method-subtraction}`.
- **假设 `asm:appN-brst-nonlocal` — 多定域光线算符的 BRST 分类** [\tagA] (new register entry, see `ASSUMPTIONS.md` §00.3; lead-author decision flagged). The BRST-invariant multilocal light-ray operators that appear in the leading-region expansion of Green functions with longitudinal (K) gluon attachments (`Collins2011` eqs. (11.12)–(11.17)) are, modulo BRST-exact (class A) and equation-of-motion (class B) operators, gauge invariant; classes A and B have vanishing matrix elements between physical states and vanishing insertions into Green functions of gauge-invariant operators at separated points. `\uses{def:appM-alien, prop:appJ-local-operators}`. Used by `lem:method-ward-multi` (Ch9; the core outline adds it to that lemma's `\uses`).

### N.5 `sec:appN-processes` 过程 (5 pp)

Opening paragraph: single cut graphs of cross sections have real–virtual cancelling soft and collinear IR divergences; when $\lambda$ is a measured variable rather than an IR regulator, a single cut graph is not absolutely integrable for any $\varepsilon>0$; different cuts have integration manifolds of different dimension and cut lines cannot be deformed — hence a cut-summed representation is needed. Gluon masses for all lines are no alternative (they break the Ward identities in the non-abelian theory).

- **假设 `asm:appN-cutsum` — 割图求和的公共表示** [\tagA]. $\sigma[M]=\sum_G\sum_{\mathcal C}\int\Gamma_{G,\mathcal C}M$ ($G$ forward graphs, $\mathcal C$ final-state cuts compatible with the measurement $M$). **(CU1)** For each $G$ there are a common domain $\Omega_G$ (e.g. light-cone-ordered PT: all minus components integrated by residues for uncut and by $\delta$ functions for cut lines, leaving the $(+,T)$ components) and an integrand $F_G$ (rational functions × measurement) with $\sum_{\mathcal C}\int\Gamma_{G,\mathcal C}M=\int_{\Omega_G}F_G\,d\mu_G$. **(CU2)** The approximators on cut graphs induce linear maps on $F_G$ (commuting with the cut sum: cutting replaces a propagator by $\delta^+$ without changing its momentum; eikonal lines obey the same cutting rules), and (GA1)–(GA4), (CS1)–(CS3) hold for $F_G$, $\lambda$ possibly a measured variable. **(CU3)** (event shapes) for all $R$ and $s\in(0,1)$: $\int_{U_R\cap\{|\tau_R-\tau|\le s\tau\}}|F_G|\,d\mu_G\le C\,s\,L^K$. Renamed items: the reference's (X1)–(X3) are (CU1)–(CU3) (no clash with the verify extensions X1–X3). `\uses{def:appN-integrand, def:prelim-cut}`.
- **假设 `asm:appN-uv` — 被积函数层次的紫外重整化** [\tagA]. **(U1)** $\Gamma$ is renormalized at the integrand level — by DR + $\overline{\rm MS}$ with the subtraction of UV subdivergences organized as local integrand counterterms ($R$ operation in DR), or by Zimmermann's forest formula with BPHZ(L) Taylor subtractions **supplemented by the finite non-invariant counterterms that restore the Slavnov–Taylor identities** (algebraic renormalization; `Breitenlohner:1975hg`, `Breitenlohner:1976te`, `Piguet:1995er`) — so that $\Gamma$ is a single function on $\Omega$ with $\int|\Gamma|$ uniformly bounded near $\varepsilon=0$; graphical Ward identities hold exactly for DR graphs; **(U2)** for every $R$ and every renormalization part $\gamma$, $T_R$ and $t_\gamma$ commute up to polynomials in the external momenta of $\gamma$ (finite local counterterms) that give no leading contribution in the power counting of $T_R$. Corrects ERR-M28 (the passage to $\overline{\rm MS}$ is not merely a finite renormalization of couplings and fields). `\uses{def:appN-integrand, thm:prelim-composite-renorm}`.
- **假设 `asm:appN-multren` — 共线因子的乘法重整化（维数正规化路线）** [\tagA]. **(Z1)** In moment space the bare collinear factor renormalizes multiplicatively, $f_N^{\rm bare}=Z_Nf_N^{\rm ren}$ (matrix in parton species, $Z_N$ a pure $\overline{\rm MS}$ pole series, target independent) — a theorem for gauge-invariant local twist-2 operators between on-shell states (`thm:prelim-composite-renorm`, `prop:appJ-local-operators`, `thm:pdf-moments-local`; off shell, mixing with BRST-exact and EOM operators has non-zero matrix elements), an independent input for fragmentation functions (`rem:pdf-ff-multren`: FF moments are not local-operator matrix elements); **(Z3)** (DR route) for on-shell massless partonic targets with DR as the only IR regulator: (i) the factorization identity holds with the same $\hat{\mathsf H}^{\rm bare}$ (bare partonic PDFs scaleless, $f^{\rm bare}_{\cdot/j}=\delta$; consistent with `asm:appN-values`(V2) but not derived from it); (ii) $C\equiv\hat{\mathsf H}^{\rm bare}Z$ has no pole at $\varepsilon=0$ (mass factorization: the IR poles of the DR partonic forward amplitude are exactly those of $Z^{-1}$; verified at fixed orders by the finiteness of the NNLO/N3LO coefficient functions). (Z3)(ii) is what the process theorems use; under (Z2) it follows from (Z1) and (Z3)(i) by `thm:disall-renorm-compat`. The register's (Z2) is the separate entry `asm:appN-ir-regulator`. `\uses{thm:pdf-moments-local, prop:pdf-partonic-dr}`.
- **假设 `asm:appN-ir-regulator` — 保持 Ward 恒等式的部分子靶红外正规化** [\tagC]. **(Z2)** There exist partonic targets $j$ with an IR regularization (parameter $\lambda$) preserving the Ward identities used in `prop:disall-factorized-subtraction`, such that `prop:disall-two-routes`(a)(b) hold and $f^{\rm ren}_{N,\cdot/j}$ is finite at $\varepsilon=0$ and invertible order by order. Status: genuine input — off-shell external partons contradict on-shellness in (Z1) (off-shell PDFs are gauge dependent; BRST/EOM mixing), a gluon mass breaks the non-abelian Ward identities, on-shell massive quarks regulate quark targets but invertibility of $f_{i/j}$ as a matrix needs gluon targets, and no on-shell-gluon regulator preserving the Ward identities and regulating its collinear divergences is known. Partial realizations: non-singlet (quark) targets with massive on-shell quarks; gluon targets only off shell with alien counterterms (`Collins:1994ee`, `Falcioni:2022fdm`), at the price of replacing (Z1) by its off-shell form with BRST-exact and EOM operators. Used only by `thm:disall-renorm-compat` (whose content grade is therefore [C]); the process theorems use the DR route (Z1)(Z3)(ii) and never this entry. `\uses{asm:appN-multren}`.
- *Target:* **`prop:disall-two-routes`** (Ch10, [R, R_cond]) — 从非物理区前向振幅到矩与 $x$ 空间的两条途径: (a) for $|\omega|<1$ ($|x|>1$) the leading regions of $T^{\mu\nu}$ contain only target-collinear and hard modes (no final state; soft lines attached only to the target jet stay in $\mathsf J$), only the hard–collinear approximation is used (rows 1–4), $\mathcal R$ = meet closure (may contain non-canonical assignments, e.g. hard components without photon vertex), one direction, no flipping lines, (Com)(Abs) exact with adapted routing; under (GA1)(GA3)(GA4) and `asm:appN-collins`: $T^{\mu\nu}=\sum_RC_RT^{\mu\nu}+O(\lambda^aL^K)$ for every $\omega\in[-\rho,\rho]$, $\rho<1$; (b) if the bounds of (a) hold uniformly for complex $|\omega|\le\rho$, the Cauchy estimate gives for every moment of $W^{\mu\nu}$ a factorization remainder $O(\rho^{-N}\lambda^aL^K)$ ($N$ the moment order); real-$\omega$ bounds give no derivative bounds — complex uniformity is extra input; (c) real-segment bounds alone do **not** control the discontinuity: the $x$-space statement at fixed $x<1$ needs the cut-graph analysis under `asm:appN-cutsum` (the hard final state merged into $H$; its internal final-state collinear and soft pinches cancel after cut summation); uniformity fails as $x\to1$ (threshold: an extra current-jet collinear region, plus distributions; pointer Ch13). *Proof plan:* full for (a)(b) and the counter-remark: (a) meet-closure by `lem:method-meets` (disjoint pinch sets — $q$ cannot flow through hard lines from one photon vertex to the other — have no common lower bound; (CS2) then directly requires $C_R\Gamma\in\mathfrak S(U_{R'})$); **$i0$ reason (corrected, ERR-m25/R6 E2):** the hard–collinear approximation creates no new denominators, the projected hard denominators are bounded away from zero on $U_R$ by (GA4)(i), and the unphysical region has no final-state cuts — `lem:method-i0-compat` is not needed; errors by `lem:appN-error-table`; (CS1) for minimal regions by `lem:method-local-accuracy`; the rest by `asm:appN-collins`; `thm:appN-subtraction`; (b) $|r_N|\le\rho^{-N}\sup_{|\omega|=\rho}|\int r(\omega)|$, Taylor coefficients of $T^{\mu\nu}$ = moments of $W$; (c) `asm:appN-cutsum` + `thm:appN-subtraction` on $F_G$, as `prop:appN-sia`. Fixes OUR-6.
- *Target:* **`prop:disall-factorized-subtraction`** (Ch10, [R, R_cond: `asm:appN-ward-product`]) — in DIS, $R'<R$ ⇒ $\mathsf J_{R'}\supset\mathsf J_R$, $\mathsf H_{R'}\subset\mathsf H_R$, so every $T_{R'}$ changes only factors of $\mathsf H_R$ and the projections between $\mathsf J_R$ and $\mathsf H_R$, never $\mathsf J_R$'s internal factors; with (Com)(Abs) and adapted routing (`lem:appN-algebra`), $\sum_{\rm graphs}C_R\Gamma=\hat{\mathsf H}_R\otimes\mathsf J_R^{\rm bare}$ with $\mathsf J_R^{\rm bare}$ the $d$-dimensional partonic matrix element of the operator of `def:pdf-quark`/`def:pdf-gluon` (no operator renormalization) and $\hat{\mathsf H}_R$ depending only on projected momenta. *Proof:* full (by `prop:method-product`, $C_R\Gamma=T_R\prod_{R'<R}(1-T_{R'})\Gamma$ = subtracted hard factor at projected momenta × exact $\mathsf J_R$; K terms → Wilson lines by (WI1)). Remark: if some $R'<R$ turns lines of $\mathsf J_R$ soft ($e^+e^-$, event shapes, DY, TMD), $T_{R'}$ changes $\mathsf J_R$'s factors and the bare collinear factor must be replaced by "collinear factor minus its zero-bin" (triangle: $C_{C_1}\Gamma=T_{C_1}\Gamma-T_{C_1}T_S\Gamma$); the general factorized form is (WI2).
- *Target:* **`thm:disall-renorm-compat`** (Ch10, [R, R_cond: `asm:appN-multren`(Z1)(Z3)(i), `asm:appN-ir-regulator`, `asm:appN-contour`(b)]; content grade [C] through (Z2) — **no [A] statement may use it**: `thm:disall-factorization`, `thm:appN-structure` and the other process theorems use `asm:appN-multren`(Z3)(ii) instead; this theorem is the rigorous consistency statement of the regulator route) — 减除与 $\overline{\rm MS}$ 重整化的相容性（矩空间）: DIS unphysical forward amplitude, $N$-th moment; assume `prop:disall-two-routes`(a)(b) and `prop:disall-factorized-subtraction`, so that $T_{(j)}=\sum_i\hat{\mathsf H}_i^{\rm bare}f_{i/j}^{\rm bare}+r_{(j)}$ with $|r_{(j)}|\le C\lambda^aL^K$. (a) [premises (Z1), `asm:appN-ir-regulator`] $C_i\equiv\sum_k\hat{\mathsf H}_k^{\rm bare}Z_{ki}$ is finite at $\varepsilon=0$; (b) $C_i$ is independent of the target and of its IR regularization; [premises (Z1)(Z3)] it equals the moments of the DR partonic matching coefficients (`prop:pdf-partonic-dr`); (c) order by order $C^{[n]}=\lim_{\lambda\to0}\big[T^{[n]}_{(j)}-\sum_{m=0}^{n-1}C^{[m]}f^{{\rm ren}[n-m]}_{\cdot/j}\big]$ (each term diverges like $\ln\lambda$, only the combination has a limit; with DR IR drop $\lim_{\lambda\to0}$). *Proof:* full — (a) by (Z1) $\hat{\mathsf H}^{\rm bare}Z=(T_{(j)}-r_{(j)})(f^{\rm ren}_{\cdot/j})^{-1}$; the left side is independent of $\lambda$ and of the target and meromorphic near $\varepsilon=0$ ((GA1)(b) + pure poles); the right side is bounded on $(0,\varepsilon_0]$ ($T_{(j)}$ is a moment of a conserved-current correlator, UV finite and IR regulated; $r_{(j)}$ regular at $\varepsilon=0$ by `cor:method-removing-eps` and uniformly small; $(f^{\rm ren})^{-1}$ finite) ⇒ no poles (`lem:method-no-pole`); (b) $C$ defined by the left side; in DR $f^{\rm bare}=\delta$, $C=T_{(j)}(f^{\rm ren})^{-1}$; (c) expand $f^{\rm ren}=\delta+\sum_{k\ge1}f^{{\rm ren}[k]}$. Everything in moment space; $x$ space via `thm:pdf-dis-xspace` and App K.
- **命题 `prop:appN-sia` — $e^+e^-\to hX$ 的减除结构** [R, R_cond]. （$R_{\rm cond}$ …）前提：\cref{asm:appN-collins}、\cref{asm:appN-cutsum}、\cref{asm:appN-contour}、\cref{asm:appN-envelope}、\cref{asm:appN-separation}、\cref{asm:appN-ward-product}（及 \cref{lem:coll-unobserved-jet}）。（本命题只涉及部分子态。）Regions: observed jet $\mathsf J_h$, unobserved jets in arbitrary directions $w_i$, soft subgraph; errors rows 1–6. Then `thm:appN-subtraction` holds for the cut-summed integrand, and with (WI1) the soft factor and the unobserved-jet factors reduce after cut summation to 1 and to free-field values (`lem:method-soft-unitarity`, `lem:coll-unobserved-jet`). `\uses{asm:appN-collins, asm:appN-cutsum, asm:appN-contour, asm:appN-envelope, asm:appN-separation, asm:appN-ward-product, thm:appN-subtraction, lem:coll-final-poles, lem:coll-unobserved-jet, lem:method-soft-unitarity, lem:method-i0-compat}`. Proof: full given the premises — $i0$: all soft lines attach to final-state lines, $\eta=+1$, `lem:method-i0-compat` for every jet (`lem:coll-final-poles`); multi-direction: $\pi_{w_i}(v)=(\bar w_i\cdot v)w_i$ are not coordinate zeroings in one frame, (Com)(Abs) only approximate and `prop:method-product` not literally usable; example: a $w_3$-collinear gluon $k$ on the $w_1$-jet line $p_1$: in $(1-T_{C_3})T_S\Gamma$ the changed factor is $2(\bar w_1\cdot p_1)(w_1\cdot k)$ minus its value at $k\to(\bar w_3\cdot k)w_3$, relative difference $\le s_R/t_R$ on $U_{C_3}$ — the same order as $T_{C_3}$'s own error; in general multi-direction (CS1)(CS2) are part of `asm:appN-collins`. Error type E-N. Pointer: `thm:coll-sia` (Ch11).
- **命题 `prop:appN-thrust-zerobin` — 推力的零箱积分无标度** [R, R_cond]. （$R_{\rm cond}$ …）前提：\cref{asm:appN-values}(V2)。（本命题只涉及部分子态。）(a) The zero-bin $T_AT_S$ of an $A$-jet graph (soft gluon exchanged between the $n_A$ eikonal line from $T_S$ and the $n_B$ Wilson line from the K term of $T_A$, the measurement constraining only $k^-$ through $p_a^2\to2p_a^+k^-$) is homogeneous of degree $-\varepsilon$ under $k^+\to ak^+$, $\mathbf k_T\to\sqrt a\,\mathbf k_T$ ($k^-$ fixed): gluon propagator or $\delta(k^2)$ gives $a^{-1}$, the eikonal $1/k^+$ gives $a^{-1}$, the measure $dk^+d^{2-2\varepsilon}k_T$ gives $a^{2-\varepsilon}$; (b) hence it is scaleless and by (V2) its value is zero: the subtracted jet function equals the unsubtracted one. `\uses{asm:appN-values, def:method-collinear-approx, def:method-soft-approx}`. Proof: full. Agrees with the SCET zero-bin (`def:scet-zero-bin`; Manohar–Stewart) — pointer only (Ch15 cites this proposition).
- **命题 `prop:appN-thrust` — 双喷注事件形状（推力）的减除结构** [R, R_cond]. （$R_{\rm cond}$ …）前提：\cref{asm:appN-collins}、\cref{asm:appN-cutsum}、\cref{asm:appN-ward-product}、\cref{asm:appN-values}。（本命题只涉及部分子态。）$\tau\to0$ ($\lambda^2\sim\tau$); regions $H$, $A$/$B$ collinear $Q(1,\lambda^2,\lambda)$, soft $Q(\lambda^2,\lambda^2,\lambda^2)$; approximations include additivity of the measurement $\tau\to\tau_A+\tau_B+\tau_S$. For $\Sigma(\tau)=\int_0^\tau d\tau'\,d\sigma/d\tau'$: $\Sigma=\sum_R\int C_R+O(\lambda^aL^K)$; the zero-bins vanish (`prop:appN-thrust-zerobin`), the subtracted jet function equals the unsubtracted one, the soft function is the thrust soft function (pointer `thm:scet-thrust`, Ch15). `\uses{asm:appN-collins, asm:appN-cutsum, asm:appN-ward-product, asm:appN-values, thm:appN-subtraction, prop:appN-thrust-zerobin, lem:appN-error-table}`. Proof: full given the premises (final-state jets, $i0$ as in `prop:appN-sia`; measurement error = row 9: $\theta(\tau-\tau(X))-\theta(\tau-\tau_R(X))\ne0$ only in $|\tau_R(X)-\tau|\le Cs_R\tau$, by (CU3) $O(s_RL^K)$).
- **命题 `prop:appN-dy` — DY 共线因子化的条件结构** [R, R_cond]. （$R_{\rm cond}$ …）前提：\cref{asm:appN-collins}、\cref{asm:appN-cutsum}、\cref{asm:appN-ward-product}、\cref{asm:appO-rewritten-cs}、\cref{asm:appO-deformation}(b)(c)（及 \cref{cor:appO-interface}、\cref{prop:method-collinear-wilson}）。（本命题只涉及部分子态。）Let $G$ be a DY forward graph with cut-summed integrand $F_G$ (only $Q^2$, $y$ measured). Assume (h1) for each $\Phi\in\{1\}\cup\{C_R\}$, $\int\Phi F_G$ converges absolutely in real space with each denominator's own $i\eta$ for every $\eta>0$ and the limit $\eta\to0^+$ exists (the DY part of `asm:appN-cutsum`(CU2)); (h2) (CS1)$_{R_0}$, (CS2)$_{R_0}$ hold for the one-sided forms $\tilde F_{G,R_0}$ on $h_1^{(R_0)}(U_{R_0})$ for every $R_0$ (the DY form of `asm:appN-collins`, stated as `asm:appO-rewritten-cs`; $F_G$ itself is stuck at the Glauber pinch in real space and need not satisfy (CS3)). Then $\big|\int F_G\,d\mu-\sum_{R\in\mathcal R}\int C_RF_G\,d\mu\big|\le C\lambda^aL^K$ (integrals as $\eta\to0^+$ limits) — `thm:appN-subtraction` in **integral**, not integral-norm, form; on each $R_0$-dominated block the subtraction values can be computed with the one-sided form (`cor:appO-interface`(e)); after summing graphs and attachments ((WI1)) the Wilson lines point to the past, the soft factor is 1 by `lem:appO-past-unitarity` (the past version of `lem:method-soft-unitarity`), and the collinear factors equal the DIS PDFs (direction irrelevant: `prop:method-collinear-wilson`). `\uses{asm:appN-collins, asm:appN-cutsum, asm:appN-ward-product, asm:appO-rewritten-cs, asm:appO-deformation, cor:appO-interface, lem:appO-past-unitarity, thm:appN-subtraction, lem:method-soft-unitarity, prop:method-collinear-wilson, lem:method-i0-compat}`. Proof: full given the premises — order $\mathcal R$ arbitrarily; disjoint blocks $V_{R^{(m)}}=U_{R^{(m)}}\setminus\bigcup_{m'<m}U_{R^{(m')}}$ (in the set algebra $\mathfrak B$, covering); $r[G']\equiv G'-\sum_RC_RG'$ linear; for $\eta>0$ additivity, (Gla-a) for the limit; on $V_{R_0}$ replace $F_G$ by $\tilde F_{G,R_0}$; (Gla-c) to move to $h_1(V_{R_0})$; pointwise $r=(1-T_{R_0})X_{R_0}-\sum_{R\not\le R_0}C_R$ and (CS1)(CS2); $i0$: by (Gla-b), `lem:method-i0-compat` with $\eta=-1$ for every jet denominator of $\tilde F_{G,R_0}$. Note to print: DY collinear factorization = cut-summed Glauber cancellation (App O) + the combinatorial/power-counting part of the subtraction method, the latter identical to $e^+e^-$. Error type E-W→E-N (weak equivalence on blocks, then integral bound). Pointer: `thm:dy-factorization` (Ch12) status layer 2.
- **假设 `asm:appN-tmd` — TMD 输入** [\tagA]. For SIDIS, $e^+e^-\to h_1h_2X$ and DY in the TMD region ($y_A=-y_B=Y$, approximators `def:appN-tmd-approx`): **(T1)** tails — constants $a>0$, $c_1\ge c_0>1+2a$, $J$ such that for $c_0L\le Y\le c_1L$, on every $U_R$ and on the subset where some soft or collinear line has $|\mathbf k_T|\le\lambda^{1+a}$, the integral norm of integrand minus approximants is $O(\lambda^aL^KY^J)$, and the constants of (CS1)(CS2) grow at most like $Y^J$; **(T2)** asymptotic linearity in $b$ space — $\ln\tilde S(y_1,y_2)=a_S+(y_1-y_2)\tilde K+O(e^{-(y_1-y_2)})$, $\ln\tilde f_A^{\rm unsub}(y_B)=\alpha_A-y_B\tilde K+O(e^{y_B-y_{P_A}})$ with the **same** slope $\tilde K$ ($B$ alike); **(T3)** $b$-space multiplicative renormalization $\tilde f^{\rm bare}=Z_{\rm TMD}(\mu,\zeta)\tilde f^{\rm ren}$, soft-factor renormalization by the cusp anomalous dimension; **(T4)** in the combination divided by the soft factor, $y_B\to-\infty$ ($y_A\to+\infty$) can be taken under the integral, so that the $O(e^{2(y_B-y_k)})$ difference of `def:appN-tmd-approx` disappears and the collinear factor equals the light-front TMD of `def:tmd-unsub`. `\uses{def:appN-tmd-approx, def:tmd-unsub, def:tmd-soft}`.
- **注 `rem:appN-spacelike-selfenergy` — 类空正规子线的自能** (no tag). For space-like $n_A(y_A)$, $n_A(y_A)\cdot k=-\sqrt2|\mathbf k_T|e^{-y_A}\sinh(y_k-y_A)$ vanishes **inside** the real-gluon phase space; the cut self-energy $\propto n_A^2/[(n_A\cdot k+i0)(n_A\cdot k-i0)]$ behaves like $1/\sinh^2(y_k-y_A)$ — a non-integrable double pole (Wilson-line length divergence), not removable by a principal value (VF-REF-19; c_tmd R4; `lem:tmd-soft-oneloop`). It must be excluded, or defined by an explicit convention, consistently in $\tilde f^{\rm unsub}$ and $\tilde S$; any constant $\sigma$ so produced cancels identically in Collins' combination (`lem:tmd-sqrt-identity`): $\tilde f_A^{\rm unsub}(y_B)$ contains $+\sigma_B$, the square root contributes $\frac12[\sigma_A+\sigma_n-(\sigma_A+\sigma_B)-(\sigma_n+\sigma_B)]=-\sigma_B$. (Time-like lines give finite constants, $\sigma=1$ in the normalization of VF-TMD-8.) Corrects ERR-M27.
- **引理 `lem:appN-absorption` — 一阶零箱吸收关系** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$ …）（本引理只涉及部分子态。）Let $R'<R$ be obtained from $R$ by turning some $A$-collinear gluons into soft ones and the hard lines they attach to in $R$ into $B$-collinear lines, such that these lines carry only the on-shell external $B$ momentum $p_B$ and the new soft momenta (all one-loop graphs are of this type). Then $T_R^{(Y)}T_{R'}^{(Y)}=T_{R'}^{(Y)}$, exactly if $p_B^+=0$ and to relative $O(e^{-2Y}p_B^+/p_B^-)$ otherwise; in particular the one-loop $A$-collinear subtraction is $C_A\Gamma=T_A\Gamma-T_S\Gamma$ (same accuracy). `\uses{def:appN-tmd-approx, def:method-subtraction}`. Proof: full — $(p_B+\pi_+^{(y_B)}(k))^2-M^2=2p_B^-(n_B(y_B)\cdot k)$ on shell, while $(\pi_-^{(y_A)}(p_B)+\pi_+^{(y_B)}(k))^2=2(n_A(y_A)\cdot p_B)(n_B(y_B)\cdot k)$ with $n_A(y_A)\cdot p_B=p_B^--e^{-2y_A}p_B^+$; $(g_T+n_A\otimes n_B(y_B))\cdot{\rm soft}={\rm soft}$; if the $B$ lines also carry a $B$-collinear loop momentum $q$, $T_R$ projects $q\to\pi_-(q)$ and $T_{R'}$ keeps $q$ — equal only for on-shell $q$ (the restriction; general case in (WI2)). Reader check: `check_algebra.py`.
- **命题 `prop:appN-tmd-window` — TMD 快度窗口中的减除定理** [R, R_cond]. （$R_{\rm cond}$ …）前提：\cref{asm:appN-collins}、\cref{asm:appN-cutsum}、\cref{asm:appN-tmd}(T1)、\cref{asm:appN-separation}（DY 另需 \cref{prop:appO-tmd}）。（本命题只涉及部分子态。）For SIDIS, $e^+e^-\to h_1h_2X$ and DY (lepton-pair $\mathbf q_T$ measured, spectators not), with $T_R^{(Y,-Y)}$: for $c_0L\le Y\le c_1L$ ($c_0>1+2a$) `thm:appN-subtraction` holds (DY in the integral form of `prop:appN-dy`), with constants growing at most polynomially in $Y$; the limit $Y\to\infty$ is taken only after summing regions and organizing the result by `prop:tmd-zero-bin-soft` and `lem:tmd-sqrt-identity`. `\uses{asm:appN-collins, asm:appN-cutsum, asm:appN-tmd, asm:appN-separation, prop:appO-tmd, thm:appN-subtraction, lem:appN-error-table}`. Proof: full — row 7 acts only on momenta projected by $T_R$: (i) a soft $k$ ($n_A$ if attached to $A$, $n_B$ to $B$) with $|\mathbf k_T|\ge\lambda^{1+a}$, $\|k\|\le1$: by (GA4)(iii) $|k^-|\ge c\mathbf k_T^2/|k^+|\ge c\lambda^{2+2a}$, likewise $|k^+|$, so $|y_k|\le(1+a)L+O(1)$; (ii) an $A$-collinear $k$ entering $\mathsf H_R$ via $\pi_+^{(y_B)}$: relative error $e^{2(y_B-y_k)}=e^{2y_B}|k^-/k^+|\le e^{-2Y}/(ct_R)$ by (GA4)(ii); (iii) internal $\mathsf J_A$ lines are not projected; hence for $Y\ge(1+2a)L+O(1)$ row 7 $\le C\lambda^{2a}$; $|\mathbf k_T|<\lambda^{1+a}$ by (T1) (integral norms take absolute values: the oscillation $e^{i\mathbf b_T\cdot\mathbf k_T}$ does not help); the measurement keeps transverse momenta exactly; $Y$ dependence of the constants $\times Y^J$, harmless for $Y\le c_1L$; $i0$: $e^+e^-\to h_1h_2X$ all final state; SIDIS current jet final state only, target jet by the one-sided condition of `lem:method-covering`; DY by `prop:appO-tmd`. Pointer: `rem:tmd-rapidity-window` (Ch14).
- *Target:* **`prop:tmd-zero-bin-soft`** (Ch14, [R, R_cond: `asm:appN-ward-product`, `asm:appN-tmd`]) — 零箱减除即软因子: (a) one loop (unconditional; by `lem:appN-absorption`): $C_A\Gamma=T_A\Gamma-T_S\Gamma$, which after the Ward identity equals in $b$ space $\mathsf H^{(0)}[\tilde f_A^{{\rm unsub}(1)}(y_B)-\tilde S^{(1)}(y_A,y_B)]$; (b) all orders: $\sum_{\rm graphs}\sum_R\int C_R\Gamma=\big[\mathsf H\,\tilde f_A^{\rm unsub}(x_A,\mathbf b_T;y_B)\,\tilde f_B^{\rm unsub}(x_B,\mathbf b_T;y_A)/\tilde S(\mathbf b_T;y_A,y_B)\big]^{[N]}$ as formal power series at each order $\alpha_s^N$ ($\mathsf H$ recursively defined by `thm:appN-structure`(d)); the $O(\lambda^aL^K)$ difference to the true cross section is `prop:appN-tmd-window`, a separate statement. *Proof:* full — (a) `lem:appN-absorption`; (b) induction: define $X_A$ by $\tilde f_A^{\rm unsub}=X_A\tilde S$; by (WI1)(WI2) the subtracted contributions with $A$-collinear part of order $N$ sum to $\tilde f_A^{{\rm unsub}(N)}-\sum_{K=1}^N\mathcal X_A^{(N-K)}\tilde S^{(K)}$ times the rest ($\mathcal X_A^{(M)}$ = subtracted $A$-collinear contribution of order $M$); induction gives $\mathcal X_A^{(N)}=X_A^{(N)}$; pure soft regions give $\tilde S$; total $X_AX_B\tilde S=\tilde f_A^{\rm unsub}\tilde f_B^{\rm unsub}/\tilde S$. One-loop check: c_tmd ($\tilde S^{(1)}=-\frac{\alpha_sC_F}\pi(Y\coth Y-\sigma)L_b$; equal slopes $\tilde K=-\frac{\alpha_sC_F}\pi\ln\frac{\mu^2b_T^2}{b_0^2}$; $y_B$ and $\sigma$ cancel; VF-TMD-1, VF-TMD-8). The square-root identity and the CS equation from (T2) are `lem:tmd-sqrt-identity` (Ch14, VF-REF-13).

### N.6 `sec:appN-exclusive` 遍举与结构定理 (2 pp)

- **假设 `asm:appN-endpoint` — 端点条件** [\tagA]. For every graph of the pion form factor (and of the meson side of $\gamma_L^*$ meson production), at each order: **(E1)** endpoint integrability of meson-jet subgraphs — integrating the meson-jet subgraph over its internal loop momenta at fixed fraction $x$ (with absolute values), $|J|(x)$ satisfies $\int_0^t|J|(x)\,dx+\int_{1-t}^1|J|(x)\,dx\le C\,t\,L^K$ for $0<t<1$ (the partonic analogue of "the DA vanishes linearly at the endpoints"); **(E2)** the hard kernel is at most $1/x$, $1/\bar x$ singular at the endpoints; **(E3)** $\int_\Omega|C_{R_{\rm end}}\Gamma|\,d\mu\le C\lambda^aL^K$ and $C_R\Gamma\in\mathfrak S(U_{R_{\rm end}})$ for every $R>R_{\rm end}$. (E1)(E2) make row 8 hold on $U_R$ and give $O(t)$ bounds for $\int_0^tdx\,|J|(x)/x$; (E3) makes the endpoint region and the leakage of larger regions into it power suppressed. Inner names (E1)–(E3) as in WRITER_SPEC_v2 §4.4 item 4 (scoped; not the error types E-S…). `\uses{def:appN-excl-approx, def:method-collins-conditions}`.
- *Target:* **`prop:excl-soft-cancel`** (Ch19, [R, R_cond: `asm:appN-endpoint`, `asm:appN-collins`, `asm:appN-ward-product`] — the premise sentence must contain `asm:appN-ward-product` because step (i) uses (WI1); report to the core outline if its wording lacks it) — 色单态介子的软胶子在被积函数层次相消: (a) pion form factor — regions $H$, collinear jets of the incoming and outgoing pion (each entering $H$ by a valence pair plus longitudinal gluons), soft subgraph connecting them, endpoint regions: (i) for every region with soft lines attached to meson jets, after summing all attachments of the soft lines to the meson jets, $\sum C_R\Gamma=0$ **exactly** (integrand-level algebraic identity); (ii) on $U_S$ the attachment-summed exact graphs are power suppressed — soft regions do not contribute at leading power; (iii) endpoint contributions are power suppressed; (iv) `thm:appN-subtraction` gives factorization with only hard–collinear subtraction terms; (b) $\gamma_L^*$ meson production: as (a) on the meson side; the proton side is a single forward jet (non-forward matrix element → GPD), soft lines attached to it belong to it, Glauber excluded by (GA4)(iii) — an assumption for DVMP; (c) if the hard kernel is more singular than $1/x$ at an endpoint (twist-3 contributions, transverse photons) so that $\int_0dx\,|J|(x)|\mathsf H(x)|$ diverges, (E2) and (GA3) fail on $U_{R_{\rm end}}$: the premise of `thm:appN-subtraction` is violated. *Proof plan:* full given the premises — $i0$: no final-state cut; soft momenta connecting the two meson jets flow through one orientation per jet; `lem:method-i0-compat`; (i) $T_R$ replaces soft attachments by $n_A^\mu n_{B\nu}$ and soft momenta by $\pi_-(k)$; by (WI1) the attachment sum gives eikonal factors $gn^\mu t^a/(n\cdot k)$ and $-gn^\mu t^a/(n\cdot k)$ on the quark and the antiquark entering $H$ (independent of the fractions after $\pi_-$), which cancel on the colour singlet of the DA projection, ${\rm Tr}_c[t^aM]-{\rm Tr}_c[Mt^a]=0$ (colour transparency); this holds for every integrand $T_R$ acts on, hence for all chain terms with maximum $R$, hence for $\sum C_R\Gamma$; (ii) the same for $T_S\Gamma$: $\sum T_S\Gamma=0$, so $\sum\Gamma=\sum(1-T_S)\Gamma\in\mathfrak S(U_S)$ by `lem:method-local-accuracy` — "the dipole cancellation is the smallness of the approximation error, not of single graphs"; (iii) (E3); (iv) `thm:appN-subtraction`; (c) no integrable envelope. Corrections: transverse-photon mechanism = logarithmic WW divergence because $g_\perp^{(v)}(0)=\frac12\int_0^1du\,\phi_\parallel(u)/u\ne0$ plus double poles $(\xi\mp x-i0)^{-2}$ (VF-REF-15, status T: the writer re-derives $g_\perp^{(v)}(0)$ or prints 「（待核实）」, WRITER_SPEC_v2 §6.4; `Mankiewicz:1999tt`; leading-twist $\gamma_T^*\to V_T$ vanishes by chirality, `Diehl:1998pd`, `CollinsDiehl2000`; ERR-M17); remainder of $F_\pi$ is $O(\frac{\Lambda^2}{Q^4}\ln\frac{Q^2}{\Lambda^2})$ (ERR-m15). Pointers: `thm:excl-pion-ff`, `thm:excl-meson-L`, `prop:excl-meson-T`, `prop:excl-endpoint` (Ch19).
- **定理 `thm:appN-structure` — 由减除法得到的因子化结构（条件形式）** [R, R_cond]. （$R_{\rm cond}$ …）前提：\cref{asm:appN-contour}、\cref{asm:appN-envelope}、\cref{asm:appN-separation}、\cref{asm:appN-collins}、\cref{asm:appN-uv}、\cref{asm:appN-multren}、\cref{asm:appN-ward-product}、\cref{asm:appN-brst-nonlocal}，以及各过程所列者：(a) DIS 与 $e^+e^-\to hX$——\cref{asm:appN-cutsum}（$x$ 空间与 SIA）；(b) 推力——\cref{asm:appN-cutsum}、\cref{asm:appN-values}；(c) DY——\cref{cor:appO-interface}、\cref{asm:appN-cutsum}；(d) TMD——\cref{asm:appN-tmd}、\cref{asm:appN-cutsum}（DY 另需 \cref{prop:appO-tmd}）；(e) 遍举——\cref{asm:appN-endpoint}。（本定理只涉及部分子态。）At each order in $\alpha_s$, at leading power, with error $O(\lambda^aL^K)$ for partonic integrands: (a) DIS (moments need complex-$\omega$ uniformity, `prop:disall-two-routes`) and $e^+e^-\to hX$ (`prop:appN-sia`) have the collinear-factorized structure with hard coefficients $C=\hat{\mathsf H}^{\rm bare}Z$, finite by `asm:appN-multren`(Z3)(ii) and given order by order by the DR recursion $C^{[n]}=[T^{\rm DR}_{(j)}Z]^{[n]}$ (on-shell massless partonic targets $j$); (b) thrust (`prop:appN-thrust`): $H=|C|^2$, $C^{[n]}$ = $n$-th order on-shell massless $q\bar q$ form factor minus all its collinear and soft subregion subtraction terms, which by (V2) are scaleless, so $C$ is the on-shell form factor with IR poles removed; (c) DY (`prop:appN-dy`): collinear structure with the DIS PDFs; hard coefficients by the same recursion with two partonic targets; (d) TMD (`prop:appN-tmd-window`, `prop:tmd-zero-bin-soft`, `lem:tmd-sqrt-identity`): for $q_T\ll Q$ Collins' structure with $\tilde f_{A,B}$ given by the square-root construction in the limit $y_A\to\infty$, $y_B\to-\infty$; $H^{[n]}$ = $n$-th order leading-power partonic cross section minus $\sum_{m<n}H^{[m]}[\tilde f_A\tilde f_B]^{[n-m]}_{\rm partonic}$ (its identification with the modulus squared of the on-shell form factor with IR poles removed requires the TMD analogue of (Z3) — not verified for TMDs); $q_T\sim Q$ and $q_T\sim\Lambda$ not covered; (e) exclusive (`prop:excl-soft-cancel`): $T_H^{[n]}$ = partonic amplitude (on-shell collinear $q\bar q$ in/out) minus $\sum\phi^{[k]}\otimes T_H^{[m]}\otimes\phi^{[l]}$ ($k+m+l=n$, $m<n$). `\uses{asm:appN-contour, asm:appN-envelope, asm:appN-separation, asm:appN-collins, asm:appN-uv, asm:appN-multren, asm:appN-ward-product, asm:appN-brst-nonlocal, asm:appN-cutsum, asm:appN-values, asm:appN-tmd, asm:appN-endpoint, cor:appO-interface, prop:appO-tmd, thm:appN-subtraction, prop:disall-two-routes, prop:disall-factorized-subtraction, prop:appN-sia, prop:appN-thrust, prop:appN-dy, prop:appN-tmd-window, prop:tmd-zero-bin-soft, lem:tmd-sqrt-identity, prop:excl-soft-cancel, lem:method-soft-unitarity}`. Proof: full deduction — `thm:appN-subtraction` per graph (or cut-summed integrand); sum graphs; DIS by `prop:disall-factorized-subtraction`, others by (WI1)(WI2) (with `asm:appN-brst-nonlocal` behind `lem:method-ward-multi`): each $C_R$ summed over graphs = subtracted hard ⊗ bare factors (minus zero-bins); regroup — fix the hard part and sum jet/soft graphs → full operator matrix elements; fix the factors and sum hard graphs with recursive subtractions → target-independent coefficients; soft factor 1 by unitarity (inclusive collinear, `lem:method-soft-unitarity`), $S_T$ for thrust, split between the TMDs, zero for exclusive; (Z1) and (Z3)(ii) of `asm:appN-multren` ((T3) for TMD) replace bare by renormalized factors (the regulator route `thm:disall-renorm-compat` is a consistency check under (Z2), not a premise). Content grade [A] (weakest premises `asm:appN-collins` and the other \tagA inputs). This is the precise form of the deduction inside `thm:method-template`'s `proofsketch` (Ch9 uses it as a forward-to-appendix premise, allowed).

### N.7 `sec:appN-examples` 例子 (3.5 pp)

Setting of the one-loop off-shell triangle (used by `ex:method-naive-induction`, `ex:method-sudakov-subtraction`, Ch5 `ex:sudakov-offshell`): scalar triangle with massless internal lines; measure $\mu^{2\varepsilon}\int d^dk/(i\pi^{d/2})$; $p_1=(P,-\frac{m^2}{2P},\mathbf 0_T)$, $p_2=(\frac{m^2}{2P'},-P',\mathbf 0_T)$, $p_i^2=-m^2$; $\tilde Q^2\equiv2PP'$, $Q^2\equiv-(p_1+p_2)^2=(\tilde Q^2+m^2)^2/\tilde Q^2=\tilde Q^2+2m^2+m^4/\tilde Q^2$; $D_g=-k^2$, $D_1=-(p_1-k)^2=m^2+2p_1\cdot k-k^2$, $D_2=-(p_2+k)^2=m^2-2p_2\cdot k-k^2$ (all with $-i0$), $\Gamma=1/(D_gD_1D_2)$; $e_1=m^2+2Pk^-$, $h_1=2Pk^-$, $t_1=2Pk^--k^2$; $e_2=m^2+2P'k^+$, $h_2=2P'k^+$, $t_2=2P'k^+-k^2$; $T_S:(D_1,D_2)\mapsto(e_1,e_2)$, $T_H:(D_1,D_2)\mapsto(t_1,t_2)$, $T_{C_1}:D_2\mapsto h_2$, $T_{C_2}:D_1\mapsto h_1$ (each also acting on already approximated denominators: $T_He_i=h_i$, $T_St_i=h_i$, $T_{C_1}e_2=h_2$, …; $h_i-t_i=k^2=-D_g$); $\mathcal R=\{S<C_1,C_2<H\}$, $\beta_S=\frac19$, $\beta_C=\frac13$.

- **命题 `prop:appN-triangle-exact` — 单圈离壳三角图：Collins 减除的完整计算与精确余项** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$：在重整化微扰论中对红外正规化的部分子态严格。）（本命题只涉及部分子态。）In the setting above:
  (a) *(closed forms)* On each quark line the approximations form the four-element semilattice $\{1,e,t,h\}$ ($e\circ t=t\circ e=h$, $h\circ x=h$, $x\circ x=x$), so (Com) and (Abs) hold exactly and, by `prop:method-product`, $C_S\Gamma=\frac1{D_ge_1e_2}$, $C_{C_1}\Gamma=\frac1{D_gh_2}\big(\frac1{D_1}-\frac1{e_1}\big)$, $C_{C_2}\Gamma=\frac1{D_gh_1}\big(\frac1{D_2}-\frac1{e_2}\big)$, $C_H\Gamma=\frac1{D_g}\big(\frac1{t_1}-\frac1{h_1}\big)\big(\frac1{t_2}-\frac1{h_2}\big)=\frac{D_g}{t_1h_1t_2h_2}$, and $r=\frac1{D_g}\big[\Delta_1\Delta_2-\Delta_1'\Delta_2'-\delta_1\delta_2\big]$ with $\Delta_i=\frac1{D_i}-\frac1{h_i}$, $\Delta_i'=\frac1{e_i}-\frac1{h_i}$, $\delta_i=\frac1{t_i}-\frac1{h_i}$;
  (b) *(subtraction terms as absolutely convergent iterated integrals)* after the $k^-$ integral by residues, every $C_R\Gamma$ is an absolutely convergent $(k^+,\mathbf k_T)$ integral for $0<\varepsilon<1$; e.g. for $0<k^+<P$: $\int\frac{dk^-}{2\pi}C_{C_1}\Gamma=\frac i2\frac{m^2k^+/(2PP')}{[k^+m^2(1-k^+/P)+P\mathbf k_T^2][k^+m^2+P\mathbf k_T^2]}$ (vanishing linearly as $k^+\to0$ while each of its two terms diverges like $1/k^+$); for $k^+>P$ only the tail of $-T_{C_1}T_S\Gamma$, $\propto-1/[k^+(k^+m^2+P\mathbf k_T^2)]$; for $k^+<0$ zero. Values: $\int C_H\Gamma=\tilde Q^{-2}(\mu^2/\tilde Q^2)^\varepsilon\mathcal A$, $\int C_{C_1}\Gamma=\int C_{C_2}\Gamma=-\tilde Q^{-2}(\mu^2/m^2)^\varepsilon\mathcal A$, $\int C_S\Gamma=\tilde Q^{-2}(\mu^2\tilde Q^2/m^4)^\varepsilon\mathcal B$, with $\mathcal A=\frac{\Gamma(1+\varepsilon)\Gamma(1-\varepsilon)^2}{\varepsilon^2\Gamma(1-2\varepsilon)}=-\Gamma(\varepsilon)J(\varepsilon)$, $J(\varepsilon)=\frac{\Gamma(-\varepsilon)\Gamma(1-\varepsilon)}{\Gamma(1-2\varepsilon)}$, $\mathcal B=\frac{\Gamma(1+\varepsilon)^2\Gamma(1-\varepsilon)}{\varepsilon^2}$ (the $C_S$ $k^+$ integral is a Beta function, $\int_0^\infty dk^+(k^+)^{-\varepsilon}/(m^2+2P'k^+)=(m^2)^{-\varepsilon}(2P')^{\varepsilon-1}\Gamma(\varepsilon)\Gamma(1-\varepsilon)$, not $J$);
  (c) *(sum)* the poles cancel ($\varepsilon^{-2}$: $1-2+1=0$; $\varepsilon^{-1}$: $\ell_Q-2\ell_m+(2\ell_m-\ell_Q)=0$, $\ell_Q=\ln\frac{\mu^2}{\tilde Q^2}$, $\ell_m=\ln\frac{\mu^2}{m^2}$) and $\sum_R\int C_R\Gamma\big|_{\varepsilon\to0}=\tilde Q^{-2}\big[\ln^2\frac{\tilde Q^2}{m^2}+\frac{\pi^2}3\big]$;
  (d) *(exact remainder)* with $I=\Phi^{(1)}(t,t)/Q^2$, $t=m^2/Q^2$, $L=\ln(Q^2/m^2)$:
  $\int r=-\frac{4m^2}{Q^4}+\frac{m^4}{Q^6}\Big(L^2-15+\frac{\pi^2}3\Big)+O\Big(\frac{m^6}{Q^8}L^2\Big)$ —
  a full power $\lambda^2$ with **no logarithm at next-to-leading power**: the logarithms of the NLP of $I$, $t(2L^2-4L-4+\frac{2\pi^2}3)$, are reproduced by the subtraction terms through the exact kinematics $\tilde Q^2\ne Q^2$; by contrast the general argument (`lem:appN-error-table`) guarantees only relative errors $O(\lambda^{1/9})$ on $U_S$ here;
  (e) *(values and the convention)* in the light-cone representation the zero-bin $T_{C_1}T_S\Gamma$ is the formal expression $\frac{\Gamma(1+\varepsilon)}{\varepsilon\tilde Q^2}(\frac{\mu^2}{m^2})^\varepsilon\int_0^\infty\frac{d\sigma_1}{\sigma_1^{1+\varepsilon}}=(\dots)\big(\frac1{\varepsilon_{\rm UV}}-\frac1{\varepsilon_{\rm IR}}\big)$ ($\sigma_1=k^+/P$; divergent for every $\varepsilon$), to which (V2) assigns 0; (V1) holds: the Feynman-parameter continuation of $T_{C_1}\Gamma=1/(D_gD_1h_2)$, $\Gamma(1+\varepsilon)\mu^{2\varepsilon}\int_0^1dx\,x^{-1-\varepsilon}\int_0^\infty d\sigma[(1-x)m^2+\sigma\tilde Q^2]^{-1-\varepsilon}=\Gamma(\varepsilon)J(\varepsilon)\tilde Q^{-2}(\mu^2/m^2)^\varepsilon$, equals the absolutely convergent $\int C_{C_1}\Gamma$; a parametric soft expansion over $[0,1]$ would instead give $-1/\varepsilon$ (representation dependence, `rem:ir-regions-values`); a direct Feynman parametrization of $1/(D_ge_1h_2)$ gives $\propto B(-\varepsilon,\varepsilon)=0$.
  **Status sentence (print verbatim in substance):** the identities, the values and the remainder are exact; the uniform integral bounds of `thm:appN-subtraction` on a deformed four-dimensional contour have not been written even for this example, which therefore illustrates `asm:appN-collins` without verifying it in integral form [unless slot X2a passes]. `\uses{prop:method-product, lem:appN-algebra, asm:appN-values}` — note: `asm:appN-values` is cited in (e) only to name the convention; the rigorous content of (e) is the computation, so the writer may treat it as a pointer (report it). Proof: full — closed forms by `prop:method-product`; $k^-$ residues (close the contour; for fixed $(k^+,\mathbf k_T)$ each term decays like $|k^-|^{-2}$); $\int d^{2-2\varepsilon}k_T(a+bk_T^2)^{-1}=\pi^{1-\varepsilon}\Gamma(\varepsilon)a^{-\varepsilon}b^{\varepsilon-1}$ (partial fractions first for $C_H$); $k^+$ integrals → $J(\varepsilon)$ for $C_{C_i},C_H$ (the first integral of $J$ is analytic for ${\rm Re}\,\varepsilon<1$) and a Beta function for $C_S$; UV ends need $\varepsilon>0$, the endpoint $k^+\to P$ needs $\varepsilon<1$; (d) from the closed form $Q^2I=\Phi^{(1)}(t,t)=\frac{4{\rm Li}_2(-\rho t)+\ln^2(\rho t)+\pi^2/3}{\sqrt{1-4t}}$, $\rho=\frac2{1-2t+\sqrt{1-4t}}$ (`UsyukinaDavydychev1993a`), expanded as $L^2+\frac{\pi^2}3+t(2L^2-4L-4+\frac{2\pi^2}3)+t^2(6L^2-14L-11+2\pi^2)+O(t^3L^2)$, minus (c) written with $\tilde Q^2=Q^2[1-2t-\dots]$ — a Table-5-type comparison of $I$ with $L^2+\pi^2/3$ in $Q^2$ does **not** test the remainder (ERR-m25). Checks: `refdoc/work_R6/check_remainder_d4.py` (the $d=4$ remainder density $\tilde r(k^+,\mathbf k_T^2)=\rho_\Gamma-\sum_R\rho_{C_R}$ integrates to $-0.0173434339581$ at $m^2=0.02$, $P=0.8$, $P'=1.3$, 12 digits); `check_algebra.py` (50/50); `check_triangle_numerics.py` (values (b) at $\varepsilon=0.3$, $P=0.8$, $P'=1.3$, $m^2=0.1$, $\mu=1$: $C_{C_1}$ $-7.26621198874$ vs $-7.26621198785$; $C_S$ $27.6979046630$ vs $27.6979046621$; $C_H$ $2.92340552296$ vs $2.92340552583$); `j07.py` ($J(\varepsilon)$ to 25 digits); `refdoc/work_SYN/triangle_remainder.py` (closed form vs 1D integral $<10^{-49}$; remainder $Q^2\int r/t\to-4$; $t^2$ coefficient residuals $O(tL^2)$); `spec_v2/checks/fig_data.py` (figure data). VF-REF-2, VF-REF-3, VF-REF-18. Figures `fig:appN-triangle-regions` (N20), `fig:appN-triangle-remainder` (N21; tested starter code in FIGURES_INDEX_v2). Error type of (d): exact. Sharpened result, not marked new (S59; verify N12).
- *Target details for* **`ex:method-naive-induction`** (Ch9, [R, R_PT]): (a) $T_{C_2}\Gamma=1/(D_gh_1D_2)$ has an unregulated massless collinear pinch in $C_1$ (the $D_g$ pole $k^-=(\mathbf k_T^2-i0)/(2k^+)$ below, the $h_1$ pole $k^-=+i0$ above, pinching at $k^-=\mathbf k_T=0$, $k^+\sim Q$; $\int d^{2-2\varepsilon}k_T/\mathbf k_T^2$ converges only for $\varepsilon<0$), while $C_{C_2}\Gamma=T_{C_2}(\Gamma-T_S\Gamma)=\frac{m^2}{2PP'}\frac1{D_gD_2e_2}-\frac1{h_1D_2e_2}$ (using $e_2-D_2=(m^2/P')k^--D_g$; each term contains only one of the two pinching poles) is suppressed; (b) $C_S\Gamma=1/(D_ge_1e_2)\sim\lambda^{-4}Q^{-6}$ is leading in $C_1$, so $T_{C_1}$ must be accurate on $T_S\Gamma$: $(1-T_{C_1})T_S\Gamma=\frac1{D_ge_1}\big(\frac1{e_2}-\frac1{h_2}\big)$, relatively $O(m^2/(Qk^+))$; (c) $T_{C_1}\Gamma=1/(D_gD_1h_2)$ is not absolutely integrable for any $\varepsilon$: at $k=(a^2\kappa^+,\kappa^-,a\boldsymbol\kappa_T)$, $a\to0$ (gluon collinear to $p_2$; rapidity end, genuine pinch of the $h_2$ pole $k^+=+i0$ with the $D_g$ pole) the integrand is $\sim a^{-4}$, the measure $\sim a^{4-2\varepsilon}$ — needs $\varepsilon<0$; in the UV direction $k=(\kappa^+,a\kappa^-,\sqrt a\boldsymbol\kappa_T)$, $a\to\infty$ — needs $\varepsilon>0$; the same for the scaleless $T_{C_1}T_S\Gamma$; $T_H\Gamma$ converges only for $\varepsilon<0$. VF-REF-18. Lessons (print): accuracy on $X_{R_0}$ ⇒ (CS1); suppression of whole subtraction terms ⇒ (CS2); integrability only of grouped expressions ⇒ (CS3), (GA1); $\lambda$-dependent neighbourhoods; contour; UV. Misconception M35.
- *Target details for* **`ex:method-sudakov-subtraction`** (Ch9): the off-shell triangle in light-cone variables — $k^-$ residues; the four regions with $\beta_S=\frac19$, $\beta_C=\frac13$; which grouped expression supplies the small factor on each $U_R$: on $U_H$ the (CS1) object $r=\frac1{D_g}[(\Delta_1-\delta_1)\Delta_2+\delta_1(\Delta_2-\delta_2)-\Delta'_1\Delta'_2]$ with $\Delta_i-\delta_i=(t_i-D_i)/(D_it_i)$ and $\Delta'_i=-m^2/(e_ih_i)$, both $O(m^2/t_H^2)=O(\lambda^{4/3})$ relative to $1/D_i$; at $\|k\|\to\infty$, $r\sim m^2/\|k\|^6$; on $U_{C_1}$ ($|k^+|\ge\lambda^{1/9}/2$, $|k^-|,|\mathbf k_T|\le\lambda^{1/3}$) the (CS1) object $(1-T_{C_1})(1-T_S)\Gamma=\frac1{D_g}[\frac1{D_1}\Delta_2-\frac1{e_1}\Delta'_2]$, small factors relatively $O(\lambda^{2-1/9}+\lambda^{1/3})$, and the (CS2) objects $C_{C_2}\Gamma$ ($O(\lambda^{2/3}L)$ over the box, needs the $k^-$ contour away from 0 by a power of $\lambda$) and $C_H\Gamma$; on $U_S$ the (CS1) object $(1-T_S)\Gamma$ with relative error $\le C|k^+|/P\le C\lambda^{1/9}$ on a contour with (GA4)(iii) (here $v_A(p_1)=m^2/2$); **the soft approximation holds only on a contour that crosses the real axis near the gluon pole, ${\rm Re}\,k^-\sim\mathbf k_T^2/(4k^+)$, and fails at $k^-\approx0$** (there $|e_1|\approx m^2$ against the deleted $\mathbf k_T^2\approx m^2$) — the simplest instance of "the approximation holds only on the deformed contour"; the values, the pole cancellation, (c) and the exact remainder of `prop:appN-triangle-exact`; the zero-bin and its representation dependence; figure `fig:method-sudakov-plane` (Ch9). The existing DR on-shell paragraph is its last part.
- **命题 `prop:appN-ladder` — 两级梯形图：嵌套区域与余项的乘积结构** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$ …）（本命题只涉及部分子态。）DIS forward ladder (two rungs), external quark off shell ($p^2=-m^2$), internal lines massless; routing $(k_1,k_2)$; $\Gamma=\mathsf H_0(k_2)K_2(k_2,k_1)K_1(k_1,p)$ ($\mathsf H_0$ the top quark line with both photon vertices; $K_2$, $K_1$ the upper and lower rungs). Regions form the chain $R_2<R_1<R_H$ ($R_2$: both rungs collinear; $R_1$: only the lower rung collinear; $R_H$: all hard); $T_2$: in $\mathsf H_0$, $k_2\to\pi_+k_2$ and the Dirac projection $\mathcal P$; $T_1$: in $\mathsf H_0$ and $K_2$, $k_1\to\pi_+k_1$ and $\mathcal P$; $T_H$: $p\to\hat p=\pi_+(p)$, $m\to0$; (Com) holds (`lem:appN-algebra`). With $\Delta\mathsf H_0\equiv\mathsf H_0(k_2)-\mathsf H_0(\pi_+k_2)\mathcal P$, $\Delta K_2\equiv K_2(k_2,k_1)-K_2(k_2,\pi_+k_1)\mathcal P$, $\Delta K_1\equiv K_1(k_1,p)-K_1(k_1,\hat p)$: (a) $r=(1-T_H)(1-T_1)(1-T_2)\Gamma=\Delta\mathsf H_0\,\Delta K_2\,\Delta K_1$; $C_2\Gamma=[\mathsf H_0(\pi_+k_2)\mathcal P]K_2K_1$, $C_1\Gamma=\Delta\mathsf H_0[K_2(k_2,\pi_+k_1)\mathcal P]K_1$, $C_H\Gamma=\Delta\mathsf H_0\,\Delta K_2\,K_1(k_1,\hat p)$; (b) $C_1\Gamma$ integrated over $k_2$ is the one-loop hard coefficient $\hat{\mathsf H}^{[1]}(k_1^+)$ (unsubtracted one-loop hard part minus tree ⊗ one-loop partonic collinear factor), $C_H\Gamma$ gives the two-loop coefficient, and expanding $\Delta\mathsf H_0\Delta K_2$ produces the two subtractions $\hat{\mathsf H}^{[1]}\otimes f^{[1]}$ and $\mathsf H^{[0]}\otimes f^{[2]}$ — the integrand form of `thm:disall-renorm-compat`(c) at $n=2$; (c) the single chain term $T_H(1-T_2)\Gamma=\Delta\mathsf H_0K_2(k_2,k_1)K_1(k_1,\hat p)$ is not in $L^1(U_{R_2})$ (a massless collinear singularity at $k_1\parallel\hat p$, convergent only for $\varepsilon<0$, where $\Delta\mathsf H_0K_2$ does not vanish), whereas the grouped $C_H\Gamma=T_H(1-T_1)(1-T_2)\Gamma$ is — the grouping of intermediate $(1-T)$ factors of `prop:method-product` is necessary. `\uses{prop:method-product, lem:appN-algebra}`. Proof: full (algebra re-derived; (c) by the scaling at the collinear singularity). Pointers: `ex:disall-ladder` (Ch10) and `fig:disall-ladder-regions` (owner Ch10); three regions including the hard one (ERR-M11).
