## Appendix O — 光锥排序微扰论与 Drell–Yan 的 Glauber 相消 / Light-cone-ordered perturbation theory and the Glauber cancellation in Drell–Yan

`\label{app:appO}` · `appO_glauber.tex` · **32 pp** · appendix (proofs) · written after the App N labels of §N.3–§N.5 are frozen

**Purpose.** Replace "the Glauber region cancels after the sum over cuts" by a conditional theorem whose every step is either proved or a numbered assumption, for colour-singlet inclusive Drell–Yan at fixed order. The proof follows the hybrid light-cone ordering of Aybat–Sterman (`AybatSterman2009`: $x^+$-ordering for one jet, $x^-$-ordering for the other; the pinch becomes a mismatch of deformation directions; final-state phases cancel by unitarity) and the light-cone treatment of Diehl–Gaunt–Ostermeier–Plößl–Schäfer (`Diehl:2015bca`), on the skeleton of CSS and Bodwin (`CollinsSoperSterman1985b`, `CollinsSoperSterman1988`, `Bodwin1985`, `Collins2011` §14.3–§14.4). Steps: (O-1) regions, domain and windows; (O-2) leading-power form in LCOPT; (O-3) cut sum and one-sided rewriting (unitarity, equal-light-cone-time localization, entire carrier); (O-4) consistency of the subtraction (chain) terms with the rewriting; (O-5) simultaneous deformation out of the Glauber region; (O-6) eikonal approximation and past-pointing Wilson lines; (O-7) soft factor $=1$ and collinear factors $=$ DIS PDFs; (O-8) interface with App N (`cor:appO-interface` → `prop:appN-dy`). Beyond the existing treatments it proves the LCOPT representation at finite $\eta_0$, the cut-position identity with insertions, the equal-time localization with its contact term, the joint localization with ordering weights (replacing an internally inconsistent fixed-order assumption), the deformation target for the class $\mathcal G_0$ and graded configurations with explicit constants, and the eikonal bounds on the deformed contour; it shows that the non-abelian "strict-window residual" is a truncation artefact (exact eikonal box and crossed box, §O.8).

**Depends on.** Ch3 `prelim`: `def:prelim-lightfront`, `def:prelim-wilson`, `prop:prelim-wilson-rules`, `def:prelim-cut`, `thm:prelim-cutting-rules`. Ch4 `ir`: `def:ir-glauber`, `def:ir-regions`, `thm:ir-leading-regions`, `prop:ir-glauber-trapping`, `rem:ir-scaling-vs-bound`. Ch9 `method`: `def:method-regions`, `def:method-neighbourhoods`, `def:method-collinear-approx`, `def:method-soft-approx`, `def:method-subtraction`, `lem:method-i0-compat`, `lem:method-ward-multi`, `prop:method-collinear-wilson`, `prop:method-soft-wilson`, `lem:method-soft-unitarity`, `rem:method-two-component`, `fig:method-glauber-direction` (owner Ch9). Ch12 `dy`: `def:dy-kin`, `prop:dy-regions`, `prop:dy-glauber-pinch`, `prop:dy-abelian-model`, `ex:dy-spectator-lcopt`, `ex:dy-future-vs-past`, `rem:dy-one-time-per-jet`, `rem:dy-common-ordering` and the statements proved here. Ch14 `tmd`: `def:tmd-soft`, `def:tmd-unsub`. App J: `lem:appJ-cut-hermiticity` (Hermiticity of the cut rules for ghost and three-gluon vertices). App M: `thm:appM-weinberg` (absolute convergence). App N: `def:appN-integrand`, `asm:appN-envelope`, `asm:appN-separation`, `asm:appN-ward-product`, `def:appN-tmd-approx`, `lem:appN-error-table`.

### O.1 `sec:appO-overview` 架构 (2 pp)

- The problem in one picture (`prop:dy-glauber-pinch`, Ch12): a soft momentum exchanged between the spectators of $A$ and $B$ is trapped in $k^-$ by an initial-state and a final-state denominator of $J_A$ and in $k^+$ by $J_B$; in a single cut graph the contour cannot leave the Glauber region, where the soft approximation fails. A general proof must say after which sums, in which sense (pointwise, integral norm, weak), on which configurations and to which accuracy the cancellation holds.
- **定义 `def:appO-leading-data` — 领头区域的数据** (no tag). $\Gamma$ a DY forward graph for $A(P_A)+B(P_B)\to\gamma^*(q)+X$ (amplitude and conjugate glued along the final state); $\Gamma_C$ the cut graph of the cut $C$; $R$ a leading region (`thm:ir-leading-regions`, `prop:dy-regions`) with the unobserved final jets merged into $H$. (a) *Subgraphs:* $H$ (amplitude side) and $H^\dagger$ (conjugate side); $J_A$, $J_B$, $S$ span the cut; $J_A$ connects to $H$ by one physical parton and any number of longitudinally polarized gluons on each side (likewise $J_B$); soft lines do not attach to $H$, $H^\dagger$ at leading power. (b) *Hard vertex:* for $J_A$ the subgraph $H$ ($H^\dagger$) acts as one vertex $V_H$ ($V_H^\dagger$) (justified in `lem:appO-hard-locality`). (c) *Active and spectator lines:* lines of $J_A$ with an endpoint on $V_H$ or $V_H^\dagger$ are active, all others spectators; total active momenta $l_A$, $l'_A$ ($l_B$, $l'_B$). (d) *Soft momenta and sides:* connection momenta $k_1,\dots,k_s$ oriented **into** the jet they attach to; a direct $J_A$–$J_B$ line is counted once, with $k_j$ into $J_A$ ($-k_j$ into $J_B$); $\sigma_j=+1$ ($-1$) if the attachment vertex is on the amplitude (conjugate) side; an uncut soft line lies on one side. (e) *Class* $\mathcal G_0$: $(\Gamma,R)\in\mathcal G_0$ iff the soft subgraph consists only of direct $J_A$–$J_B$ lines (any number, possibly cut, any colour structure); lines with both ends on one jet are not direct. External hadrons are colour singlets (split into partons by colour-singlet point vertices); coloured external partons are excluded (`rem:dy-dft-premises`). `\uses{def:ir-regions, def:method-regions, def:dy-kin}`.
- The steps (O-1)–(O-8) (names binding, WRITER_SPEC_v2 §4.4 item 4) with their outputs and error types: (O-1) `def:appO-leading-data`, `def:appO-windows`, `def:appO-weak-equivalence` — no error; (O-2) `lem:appO-glauber-pc` (E-S/E-P), `lem:appO-lcopt`, `lem:appO-numerators`, `lem:appO-hard-locality`, `lem:dy-poles-orientation` — exact under `asm:appO-lcopt`; (O-3) `lem:appO-unitarity` (exact), `lem:appO-equal-time`, `lem:appO-joint-localization`, `prop:appO-ss-localize` (E-F), `prop:appO-one-sided` (E-W, via `asm:appO-envelope`); (O-4) chain-term consistency inside `prop:appO-one-sided` (needs `asm:appN-separation`(ii)); (O-5) `lem:appO-no-crossing`, `prop:appO-target` (exact geometry), `asm:appO-deformation`; (O-6) `prop:appO-eikonal` (E-P on the deformed contour), `prop:appO-past-wilson` ([A]); (O-7) `lem:appO-past-unitarity`; (O-8) `cor:appO-interface`. Three error types appear (print this): *scaling statements* (E-S; integral forms by `asm:appN-envelope`), *fibre-integrated relative errors* (E-F; the rewriting is not a pointwise approximation: in a window of width $\sim\nu_A$ the original integrand has pinched poles and the rewritten one is smooth; integrated over the rest by `asm:appO-envelope`), and *conditional terms* (coincident connections under `asm:appO-mixing`(c); soft-angle region under `asm:appO-deformation`(c)).
- `fig:appO-flowchart` (FIGURES_INDEX_v2 S14): solid boxes for (O-1)…(O-8) with the labels above; orange dashed boxes for `asm:appO-lcopt`, `asm:appO-envelope`, `asm:appO-mixing`, `asm:appO-deformation`, `asm:appO-rewritten-cs` with arrows to their uses; green box App N (`prop:appN-dy`); margin labels 「$J_A$：时间 $x^+$，能量 $l^-$」「$\sum_C$，最大时刻配对」「$k^-\to k^-+i\sigma\kappa_-$，$k^+\to k^+-i\sigma\kappa_+$」.
- Precedents and credit (print): `AybatSterman2009` (hybrid light-cone ordering; "colour representation the same in amplitude and conjugate"); `Diehl:2015bca` (light-cone perturbation theory for single and double DY; clarifications of the single-DY Glauber argument); `CollinsSoperSterman1985b`, `CollinsSoperSterman1988`, `Bodwin1985` (all-order skeleton); `Collins2011` §14.4.8 (remaining issues). LCOPT rules: `Kogut:1969xa`, `Chang:1973qi`, `Brodsky:1997de`; equivalence with covariant PT: `Ligterink:1994tm`; light-front vacuum: `Collins:2018aqt`; largest-time equation and cutting: `Veltman1963`, `Cutkosky1960`, `tHooftVeltman1974`.

### O.2 `sec:appO-lcopt` 光锥序微扰论 (4 pp)

- **定义 `def:appO-lcopt` — 光锥时间、中间态与能量分母** (no tag). $J$ a connected subgraph (vertex set $V$, $N=|V|\ge2$, internal lines $L$, no self-loops), all plus and transverse components fixed, $l^+\ne0$ on every line. (i) For $J_A$: time $\tau_v\equiv x_v^+$, line energy $l^-$, on-shell energy $E(l)\equiv(\mathbf l_T^2+m_l^2-i0)/(2l^+)$, so $l^2-m_l^2+i0=2l^+(l^--E(l))$ (row 9; $E(l)$ in the lower half-plane for $l^+>0$); for $J_B$ swap $+\leftrightarrow-$. (ii) Each internal line is oriented along $l^+>0$ (antiparticle reading for fermions), tail $t(l)$, head $h(l)$; $q_v^-$ is the minus momentum flowing into $J$ at $v$. (iii) A *compatible ordering* is a bijection $\pi:\{1,\dots,N\}\to V$ with $\pi^{-1}(t(l))<\pi^{-1}(h(l))$; ${\rm Ord}(J)$ their set; the $i$-th intermediate state $S_i^\pi=\{l:\pi^{-1}(t(l))\le i<\pi^{-1}(h(l))\}$ (never empty, $J$ connected), injected energy $Q_i^\pi=\sum_{j\le i}q^-_{\pi(j)}$, denominator $D_i^\pi=Q_i^\pi-\sum_{l\in S_i^\pi}E(l)$. Fourier identity (print): $\int\frac{dl^-}{2\pi}e^{-il^-\tau}\frac{i}{2l^+(l^--E(l))}=\frac{\theta(l^+)\theta(\tau)+\theta(-l^+)\theta(-\tau)}{2|l^+|}e^{-iE(l)\tau}$. `\uses{def:prelim-lightfront}`.
- **引理 `lem:appO-lcopt` — 由 Feynman 积分导出光锥序微扰论（有限 $\eta_0$）** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$ …）（本引理只涉及部分子态。）Let $J$ be as in `def:appO-lcopt` with numerators $N_l$ independent of internal minus components ("reduced numerators") and vertex factors dropped as constants. For $q^-\in\mathbb R^N$ let $I_J(q^-)=\int\prod_l\frac{dl^-}{2\pi}\prod_v2\pi\delta\big(q_v^-+\sum_{h(l)=v}l^--\sum_{t(l)=v}l^-\big)\prod_l\frac{iN_l}{2l^+(l^--E(l))}$. With $i0\to i\eta_0$, $\eta_0>0$: (a) the integral without the overall $\delta$ converges absolutely; (b) pointwise in $q^-$,
  $I_J(q^-)=2\pi\delta\Big(\sum_vq_v^-\Big)\prod_l\frac{N_l}{2l^+}\sum_{\pi\in{\rm Ord}(J)}\prod_{i=1}^{N-1}\frac{i}{D_i^\pi}$;
  (c) the limit $\eta_0\to0^+$ exists in the sense of tempered distributions in $q^-$ (the $N-1$ forms $Q_i^\pi$ are linearly independent). `\uses{def:appO-lcopt, thm:appM-weinberg}`. Proof: full, five steps — (1) single-line time representation $g_l(\Delta)=N_l\theta(\Delta)e^{-iE(l)_{\eta_0}\Delta}/(2l^+)$, $\|g_l\|_{L^1}=|N_l|/\eta_0$; (2) absolute convergence by Weinberg's theorem (`thm:appM-weinberg`): $|L_W|>\dim W$ for every non-zero subspace $W$ of loop flows, since flows in $W$ are cycles of $(V(L_W),L_W)$ and without self-loops $\dim W\le|L_W|-c_W\le|L_W|-1$; (3) time representation $I_J=\int\prod_vd\tau_ve^{-i\sum_vq_v^-\tau_v}\prod_lg_l(\tau_{h(l)}-\tau_{t(l)})$ (Gaussian damping, Fubini, zero circulation); (4) sector decomposition and summation by parts, $\int_0^\infty d\Delta_i\,e^{i\Delta_iD_i^\pi}=i/D_i^\pi$ (${\rm Im}\,D_i^\pi=\sum_{S_i}\eta_0/(2l^+)>0$); (5) distribution limit. Literature: `Kogut:1969xa`, `Chang:1973qi`, `Brodsky:1997de` (rules), `Ligterink:1994tm` (equivalence; minus integrals). Check: `refdoc/work_R7/check_lcopt.py` (8 graphs at one and two loops, agreement $\le10^{-30}$; a directed 3-cycle with ${\rm Ord}=\varnothing$ gives 0). Error type: exact.
- **引理 `lem:appO-numerators` — 分子、瞬时项与瞬时圈** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$ …）（本引理只涉及部分子态。）Feynman gauge. (i) Fermions: with $l_{\rm on}=(l^+,E(l),\mathbf l_T)$, $\frac{i(\slashed l+m)}{l^2-m^2+i0}=\frac{i(\slashed l_{\rm on}+m)}{2l^+(l^--E(l))}+\frac{i\gamma^+}{2l^+}$; the second term (instantaneous fermion) places both endpoints at equal light-cone time. (ii) Gluon and ghost numerators are $l^-$ independent. (iii) In general $\mathcal N\prod_l\frac{i}{l^2-m_l^2+i0}=\sum_{T\subseteq L}c_T\prod_{l\notin T}\frac{i}{l^2-m_l^2+i0}$ with $c_T$ polynomial in the $q_v^-$ and in minus components of loops inside $T$. (iv) If $T$ is a forest and $J/T$ has no self-loops, the term is the LCOPT sum of `lem:appO-lcopt` for $J/T$ (contact vertices at one time). (v) Otherwise the only non-absolutely-convergent pieces are one-dimensional integrals $\int dx\,x^r$ and $\int dx\,[2l^+(x-E(l))]^{-1}$ ("instantaneous loops"), independent of all external minus energies; hence for **any** linear assignment of their values every term depends on the external minus energies as (polynomial) × (product of LCOPT denominators). `\uses{lem:appO-lcopt}`. Proof: full (substitute $l^-=E(l)+\Delta_l/(2l^+)$, cancel propagators, iterate; private loop variables for $T$-cycles and self-loops). Check: explicit Dirac matrices (`refdoc/work_R7/check_cutsum.py`). Remark to print: light-cone gauge would add gluon instantaneous terms; Feynman gauge is used throughout.
- **注 `rem:appO-contact` — 与 $V_H$ 重合的接触顶点在领头幂次不出现** (no tag). Contracting an instantaneous fermion line between a soft vertex on an active line and $V_H$ produces a contact vertex at $\tau=0$; at leading power it is absent because the leading soft/Glauber coupling to $A$-collinear fermions is $\gamma^+$ and $\gamma^+\gamma^+=0$, and a soft gluon effectively attached to $H$ costs $\lambda^2$. (E-S.) Longitudinal collinear gluons attached to $H$ ($J^+H^-$) are ordinary active lines.
- **假设 `asm:appO-lcopt` — 光锥序微扰论的适用性** [\tagA]. Full text in `ASSUMPTIONS.md`. Content: for a DY leading region and $J=J_A$ (likewise $J_B$): (i) (zero modes, endpoints, truncation) on $U_R$, in the integral norm, the regularized Feynman integral of $J$ equals up to $O(\lambda^a)$ the iterated integral "all internal minus components over $\mathbb R$ first (`lem:appO-lcopt`, `lem:appO-numerators`), then plus and transverse components over $\{l^+\ne0\}$"; distributions concentrated at $l^+=0$ and endpoint contributions of plus integrals are absent at leading power, and the minus-component restriction of `def:method-neighbourhoods` can be dropped (removed tail $O(\lambda_J/\lambda^\beta)$); (ii) (instantaneous loops) there is an assignment of the one-dimensional integrals of `lem:appO-numerators`(v) (depending only on plus and transverse components) for which the minus integral equals the expansion of `lem:appO-numerators`. Item (iii) of the earlier formulation (Hermiticity of the cut rules for ghost and three-gluon vertices) is **not** an assumption: it is `lem:appJ-cut-hermiticity` (App J). `\uses{lem:appO-lcopt, lem:appO-numerators, def:method-neighbourhoods}`.
- Figure `fig:appO-lcopt-term` (FIGURES_INDEX_v2 S15): one LCOPT term of $J_A$ (amplitude side) with $V_H$ at $\tau=0$, two soft injections, denominators $D_1,\dots,D_5$ marked initial/final.

### O.3 `sec:appO-orientation` 取向与幂次计数 (3 pp)

- **定义 `def:appO-orientation` — 初态与末态中间态、取向与标准流** (no tag). In an LCOPT term of $J_A$ whose amplitude part contains $V_H$: (a) amplitude-side intermediate states before $V_H$ ($\tau<0$) are *initial*, those after $V_H$ (before the cut) *final*; the conjugate side is mirrored. (b) $k_j$ has orientation $\eta=-1$ ($+1$) in a term if $k_j^-$ appears there in an initial (final) denominator; "$k_j$ flows only through $\eta=-1$ states" means $k_j^-$ appears only in initial denominators in every term (never in final denominators or in the cut $\delta$). Orientation is a property of intermediate states, **not of Feynman lines** (a spectator line can belong to initial and final states). (c) *Standard flow:* route the loop momenta of $J_A$ so that each soft $k_j$ attached at $v_j$ on side $\sigma_j$ enters at $v_j$, flows along a same-side path to $V_H$ ($\sigma_j=+1$) or $V_H^\dagger$ ($\sigma_j=-1$) and exits through the active lines: $l_A=\hat l_A+\sum_{j\in\mathcal A,\sigma_j=+1}k_j$, $l'_A=\hat l'_A+\sum_{j\in\mathcal A,\sigma_j=-1}k_j$, $\hat l_A=\hat l'_A=P_A-\sum_{c\in C\cap J_A}p_c$ (cut momenta soft independent); for $J_B$ use incoming connection momenta. `\uses{def:appO-lcopt, def:appO-leading-data}`.
- *Target:* **`lem:dy-poles-orientation`** (Ch12, [R, R_PT]) — 光锥序中间态的取向与 $k^-$ 极点: in the finite-$\eta_0$ LCOPT representation (`lem:appO-lcopt`) of a jet subgraph $J_A$ whose active momenta exit at one vertex $V_H$, with standard flow: (a) every amplitude-side initial denominator has the form $D=A_D+\sum_{j\in I(D)}k_j^-+i0$ and every final one $D=A'_D-\sum_{j\in F(D)}k_j^-+i0$, with $A_D$, $A'_D$ independent of all soft minus components ($I(D)$, $F(D)$: soft momenta injected before, resp. after the state but before the cut); otherwise $k_j^-$ occurs only polynomially in vertex/contact factors, never in $1/(2l^+)$, on-shell numerators or the cut $\delta$; (b) as functions of $k_j^-$, initial zeros lie at ${\rm Im}\,k_j^-<0$ and final zeros at ${\rm Im}\,k_j^->0$; for any $\kappa_j\ge0$ the simultaneous shift $k_j^-\to k_j^-+i\kappa_j$ gives every initial denominator ${\rm Im}\,D=\sum_{I(D)}\kappa_j+\eta_0>0$; conjugate side: $k_j^-\to k_j^--i\kappa_j$; both sides $k^-\to k^-+i\sigma\kappa$; (c) $J_B$: $+\leftrightarrow-$, injected momenta $-k_j$ for direct lines, compatible shift $k_j^+\to k_j^+-i\sigma_j\kappa_j$. Hence the $k^-$ poles of the lines coupling to $k$ lie on one side iff all intermediate states it traverses have one orientation; it generalizes `lem:coll-final-poles` and `prop:dy-glauber-pinch`. *Proof:* full — $Q_i^\pi$ before $V_H$ contains $P_A^-$ plus the soft energies injected so far; after $V_H$ use overall minus conservation of the amplitude part ($P_A^-+K_{\rm tot}-u^-=\mathcal E$, $u$ the active momentum, $\mathcal E=\sum_{\rm cut}E(p_c)$) to write $Q_i^\pi=\mathcal E-K_{>i}$; ${\rm Im}$ of $-E(l)$ is $+\eta_0/(2l^+)$. Orientation conventions vs `lem:method-i0-compat`: there $k_D=-k_j$ flows out of the jet and $\eta_D=-1$ ⇔ initial; the deformations coincide (print the short dictionary: $\eta=-1$ ≡ initial ≡ amplitude-side ${\rm Im}\,k_j^->0$, ${\rm Im}\,k_j^+<0$ for direct lines). Example: `ex:dy-spectator-lcopt` (two orderings = two covariant propagators). `fig:dy-pole-orientation` (owner Ch12).
- **定义 `def:appO-pinch` — Glauber 区与 Glauber 夹挤** (no tag). Fix $0<\delta\le\frac18$. (i) $G_\delta=\{k:\mathbf k_T\ne0,\ |k^+k^-|\le\delta\,\mathbf k_T^2\}$ (moduli of components for complex $k$). (ii) $k_j$ is *pinched in $J_A$* if $k_j^-$ appears in an initial denominator of some term and in a final denominator of the same term (*intra-term*) or another term (*inter-term*); likewise $J_B$ with $k_j^+$. (iii) A direct line is *Glauber-pinched* if pinched in $J_A$ and in $J_B$; for a general soft subgraph a soft loop momentum is Glauber-pinched if one connection momentum it flows through is pinched in $J_A$ and another in $J_B$. Remarks to print: (1) cut lines are never in $G_\delta$ ($2|k^+k^-|=\mathbf k_T^2+m^2>2\delta\mathbf k_T^2$), so Glauber momenta live on uncut lines and $\sigma_j$ is defined; (2) inter-term pinches are obstructions because Ward identities hold only after summing attachments, so all terms must share one contour (the K-term pole at $k^-=0$ lies between up- and down-deformed contours; its residue is the Glauber phase); (3) if $k_j$ is pinched in only one jet, deforming the other component even to $|k_j^+|\sim|\mathbf k_{Tj}|$ does **not** leave $G_\delta$ ($|k^+k^-|\sim\lambda^3\ll\mathbf k_T^2$) — it only validates the soft approximation on that side (misconception M31; `rem:method-two-component`). `\uses{def:ir-glauber, lem:dy-poles-orientation}`.
- **引理 `lem:appO-glauber-pc` — Glauber 幂次计数** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$ …）（本引理只涉及部分子态。）Scaling region of $R$: $J_A$ lines $\sim(1,\lambda^2,\lambda)$, $J_B$ lines $\sim(\lambda^2,1,\lambda)$, $H$ lines of virtuality $\sim Q^2$, soft $k_j$ soft $(\lambda^2,\lambda^2,\lambda^2)$ or Glauber $(\lambda^2,\lambda^2,\lambda)$ ($Q=1$). (a) In every denominator and numerator of $J_A$ the terms containing $k_j^+$ are relatively $O(\lambda^2)$: $(l+c)^2-m^2=(l^2-m^2)+2l^+c^-+2l^-c^+-2\mathbf l_T\cdot\mathbf c_T+2c^+c^--\mathbf c_T^2$, dropped terms $2l^-c^+$, $2c^+c^-$ ($\sim\lambda^4$ against kept $\sim\lambda^2$); (b) $J_B$ likewise with $k_j^-$; (c) (pointwise) on $G_\delta$ the connecting denominator does not vanish and $\frac{1}{2k^+k^--\mathbf k_T^2+i0}=-\frac{1}{\mathbf k_T^2}(1+r)$, $|r|\le\frac{2\delta}{1-2\delta}$; (d) $H$ and the measurement ($Q^2$, $y$; $\mathbf q_T$ for TMD) depend on $l_A^-$ and on soft minus components routed through $V_H$ only at relative $O(\lambda^2)$, on $l_B^+$ likewise, on transverse components at relative $O(\lambda)$ (TMD: only through $\delta^{(2)}(\mathbf q_T-\dots)$): $H=H(l_A^+,l_B^-;q)$ at leading power; (e) for TMD soft momenta $(\lambda,\lambda,\lambda)$ ($\lambda=q_T/Q$) the ratios of (a)(b)(d) become relative $O(\lambda)$; at the Glauber scale (a)–(d) are unchanged. `\uses{def:appO-leading-data, def:ir-glauber}`. Proof: full (expansion of the denominators; (c) $r=x/(1-x)$, $|x|=|2k^+k^-/\mathbf k_T^2|\le2\delta$). Error type: (a)(b)(d)(e) **E-S** (scaling statements, not pointwise bounds near zeros of jet denominators; integral forms by `asm:appN-envelope`, `rem:ir-scaling-vs-bound`); (c) **E-P**.
- **引理 `lem:appO-hard-locality` — 硬顶点的光锥定域性与无权变量 $\mathcal E$** [R, R_cond]. （$R_{\rm cond}$ …）前提：\cref{asm:appO-lcopt}。（本引理只涉及部分子态。）After the approximation of `lem:appO-glauber-pc`(d) ($H$ and the measurement ignore $J_A$'s active minus components), in the cut graph: (o) the amplitude part of $J_A$ together with $V_H$ is connected (plus-momentum conservation; a component without the hadron would be a closed collinear subgraph attached only to soft lines, not on a pinch surface); (i) integrating the active minus components (unweighted) fixes $V_H$ and $V_H^\dagger$ at $\tau=0$; (ii) after this integration
  $J_A^{\rm cut}=\sum_{\pi,\pi'}\mathcal F_{\pi\pi'}\prod_{i<i_H}\frac{i}{P_A^-+K^\pi_{\le i}-\sum_{S_i^\pi}E(l)}\prod_{i\ge i_H}\frac{i}{\mathcal E-K^\pi_{>i}-\sum_{S_i^\pi}E(l)}\times\big[\text{conjugate}\big]^*$,
  with $\mathcal F_{\pi\pi'}$ independent of all minus components, $\mathcal E=\sum_{p\in X}E(p)$ an unweighted variable fixed by the cut's $2\pi\delta(\mathcal E-\sum_XE(p))$; initial denominators do not depend on $\mathcal E$, final ones not on $P_A^-$; (iii) the standard flow of `def:appO-orientation` exists and is legitimate (different paths differ by exact translations of plus and transverse loop variables and by changes of $H$ and the measurement of relative order $\lambda^2$, resp. $\lambda$); (iv) in operator language the PDF fields sit at $x^+=0$; for TMD the active transverse weight $e^{i\mathbf b\cdot\mathbf l_{TA}}$ contains no minus component. `\uses{asm:appO-lcopt, lem:appO-lcopt, lem:appO-numerators, lem:appO-glauber-pc, def:appO-orientation}`. Proof: full (injections $-u^-$ at $V_H$, $P_A^-$ at the hadron, $k_j^-$ at soft vertices; relative active minus components give $\delta(\tau_r-\tau_{r'})$; $u^-$, $u'^-$ appear only through $\exp[iu^-(\tau_{V_H}-\tau'_{V_H^\dagger})]$). Error type: exact given `asm:appO-lcopt` and the E-S approximation (d). Pointer: `rem:dy-one-time-per-jet` (one light-cone time per jet: ordering the whole graph in one $x^+$ would spread the $B$ end of the hard vertex over $O(1/Q)$ in $x^+$, exactly the Glauber range).

### O.4 `sec:appO-unitarity` 割图求和的幺正性 (3 pp)

- **定义 `def:appO-chain` — 末态链、割位置与约化权重** (no tag). Fix the initial orderings on both sides. List the final-part vertices of $J_A$ in closed-time-path order (amplitude $\tau>0$ vertices increasing in $\tau$, then conjugate $\tau>0$ vertices decreasing): $v_1,\dots,v_n$ with states $s_0,\dots,s_n$ ($s_0$ right after $V_H$, $s_n$ right before $V_H^\dagger$); the cut crosses $s_j$ ($0\le j\le n$). Reduced weights: amplitude vertex $\alpha_i=(-iv_i)\cdot i$, conjugate vertex $\beta_i=(+iv_i)\cdot(-i)$, with $v_i$ the numerical vertex factor (on-shell numerator × coupling); amplitude insertions (attachment points of soft lines lying on the amplitude side) have $\beta_i=0$, conjugate insertions $\alpha_i=0$. State energies $E_i=\sum_{l\in s_i}E(l)+\sum_{\text{amp. ins. }a>i}k_a^-+\sum_{\text{conj. ins. }b\le i}k_b'^-$ (independent of $j$). The term with the cut through $s_j$: $\mathcal T_j=W_j\int\frac{d\mathcal E}{2\pi}\prod_{i<j}\frac{1}{\mathcal E-E_i+i0}\,2\pi\delta(\mathcal E-E_j)\prod_{i>j}\frac{1}{\mathcal E-E_i-i0}$, $W_j=\prod_{i\le j}\alpha_i\prod_{i>j}\beta_i$. `\uses{def:appO-orientation, def:prelim-cut}`.
- **引理 `lem:appO-cut-position` — 割位置恒等式（含插入）** (数学). $E_0,\dots,E_n$ real; $\alpha_i,\beta_i\in\mathbb C$ independent of $\mathcal E$; $\pm i0\to\pm i\eta_0$, $2\pi\delta(x)\to2\eta_0/(x^2+\eta_0^2)$. With $H_m(\mathcal E)=\prod_{i<m}\frac{1}{\mathcal E-E_i+i0}\prod_{i\ge m}\frac{1}{\mathcal E-E_i-i0}$ ($1\le m\le n$):
  $\sum_{j=0}^n\mathcal T_j=\delta_{n0}W_0-i\sum_{m=1}^n(\alpha_m-\beta_m)\prod_{i<m}\alpha_i\prod_{i>m}\beta_i\int\frac{d\mathcal E}{2\pi}H_m(\mathcal E)$,
  exactly for every $\eta_0>0$. (a) If $\alpha_i=\beta_i$ for all $i$, $\sum_j\mathcal T_j=\delta_{n0}$. (b) Only vertices with $\alpha_m\ne\beta_m$ contribute; an amplitude insertion contributes only if it is the last amplitude insertion with no conjugate insertion before it, a conjugate insertion only if it is the first conjugate insertion with no amplitude insertion after it: at most two terms. `\uses{def:appO-chain}`. Proof: full (with $a_i=(\mathcal E-E_i+i\eta_0)^{-1}$, $b_i=(\mathcal E-E_i-i\eta_0)^{-1}$: $\prod_{i<j}a_i\,i(a_j-b_j)\prod_{i>j}b_i=i(H_{j+1}-H_j)$; Abel summation; $\int H_0=\int H_{n+1}=0$ for $n\ge1$). Interpretation (print, Veltman's largest-time equation in light-cone time, `Veltman1963`): moving the cut across the latest vertex changes only its weight. Check: `refdoc/work_R7/check_cutsum.py` ($n=0..6$, random complex weights, agreement $\le10^{-39}$; insertion patterns ICA, CIA, AICIA give 0). Generalizes the $\alpha=\beta$ telescoping of c_glauber §1.5 Step 5.
- **引理 `lem:appO-unitarity` — 光锥幺正性引理（闭时路径形式）** [R, R_cond]. （$R_{\rm cond}$ …）前提：\cref{asm:appO-lcopt}。（本引理只涉及部分子态。）Under the approximations of `lem:appO-glauber-pc`(a)(d), fix everything outside $J_A$: amplitude insertions $O_a$ (the good component $J^{+a}$ of the colour current at a soft attachment, integrated over $x^-$, Fourier transformed in $\mathbf x_T$), conjugate insertions $O'_b$, and their external factors; sum over all measurement-compatible cuts of $J_A$ (keeping every insertion on its side) and integrate $\mathcal E$ unweighted. Then (a) $\mathcal J_A=\int\prod_ad\tau_ae^{-ik_a^-\tau_a}\prod_bd\tau'_be^{+ik_b'^-\tau'_b}\langle P_A|\bar{\rm T}\{V_H^\dagger(0)\prod_bO_b'^\dagger(\tau'_b)\}\,{\rm T}\{V_H(0)\prod_aO_a(\tau_a)\}|P_A\rangle$, and the terms of a given forward graph equal the sum of its side-preserving cuts; (b) in each chain, contributions of internal vertices later than all insertions cancel in the cut sum; without $\tau>0$ insertions $\mathcal J_A=\langle\Psi'|\Psi\rangle$ with $|\Psi\rangle=V_H(0){\rm T}\{\prod_aO_a(\tau_a)\}|P_A\rangle$: final-state interactions internal to $J_A$ are absent; (c) two families of cut graphs differing only in the side of one insertion that is the latest vertex sum to $(\alpha_m-\beta_m)$ times a common factor. `\uses{asm:appO-lcopt, lem:appO-hard-locality, lem:appO-cut-position, lem:appJ-cut-hermiticity}`. Proof: full — (b)(c) from `lem:appO-cut-position` with $\alpha_i=\beta_i$ for internal vertices (`lem:appJ-cut-hermiticity`); (a) apply `lem:appO-cut-position` chain by chain ($H_m$ = "$v_m$ latest"), equivalently Gell-Mann–Low with $U^\dagger U=1$ (the light-front vacuum is trivial by `asm:appO-lcopt`(i)). Error type: exact (given the E-S approximations). Gauge: the identity is algebraic and needs no positive metric.
- **注 `rem:appO-cut-sum-content` — 幺正性引理用到了什么、没有消去什么** (no tag). (1) The only physical input is that the identity operator stands between cut states ($J_A$'s final state unconstrained, $\mathcal E$ unweighted) — "compatible with the measurement"; if a final hadron is measured or a gap imposed, the projector does not commute with the evolution and (b)(c) fail (pointer `prop:break-measurement`, Ch18). (2) What does not cancel: soft lines pin insertions to a side, so $\tau>0$ insertions cannot be paired within $J_A$ alone — the source of the Glauber pinch.
- Figure `fig:appO-cutsum` (FIGURES_INDEX_v2 S16): one chain in closed-time-path order with the four cut positions; for a fixed time configuration whose latest vertex is internal the two adjacent cut positions cancel.

### O.5 `sec:appO-localization` 等光锥时刻局域化 (5 pp)

Why equal time (print, one paragraph): in $G_\delta$ the connecting propagator $\simeq-1/\mathbf k_T^2$ is $k^\pm$ independent, so in coordinate space it is $\propto\delta(x^+-y^+)\delta(x^--y^-)$; $J_A$ ignores $k^+$ (attachments at $x^-\simeq0$), $J_B$ ignores $k^-$ (at $y^+\simeq0$): the Glauber exchange happens at the hard vertex's time, when the two contracted hadrons pass through each other.

- **定义 `def:appO-windows` — 可局域端点、尺度适配窗口与软角区** (no tag). $\beta_R$ from `def:method-neighbourhoods`; fix $0<a_G<\min_{R'}\beta_{R'}$. (a) An endpoint $j$ of an uncut soft line in $J_A$ is *localizable* if it sits on a spectator line of $J_A$ or on an active line emitted by $V_H$, and the other end is not in $J_A$; a *localizable unit* is a localizable endpoint or an SS line (direct line with both ends on spectators, one unit). (b) Jet energy scale $\nu_A\equiv P_A^-+\sum_{l\in J_A}E(l)$ (all lines of $J_A$, both sides and cut, $l^+>0$; independent of soft $\pm$ components after `lem:appO-glauber-pc`(a); $\nu_A\sim\lambda^2$ in the scaling region). (c) With $\vartheta\in C^\infty(\mathbb R)$, $0\le\vartheta\le1$, $\vartheta=1$ on $(-\infty,\frac12]$, $\vartheta=0$ on $[1,\infty)$; $\mu_j\equiv\lambda^{\beta_R}$ for an endpoint in $J_A$, $\mu_j\equiv\nu_B$ for an SS line: $\Theta_j\equiv\vartheta\big(\frac{4\nu_A\mu_j}{\lambda^{a_G}\mathbf k_{Tj}^2}\big)$, $\Theta'_j\equiv\vartheta\big(\frac{8\nu_A\mu_j}{\lambda^{a_G}\mathbf k_{Tj}^2}\big)$, carrier width $w_j\equiv\lambda^{a_G}|\mathbf k_{Tj}|$; $J_B$ by $A\leftrightarrow B$; ${\rm supp}\,\Theta'_j\subset\{\Theta_j=1\}$. (d) Soft-angle region $K_R\equiv\{x\in U_R:\prod_u\Theta'_u(x)<1\}$. The windows depend only on soft $\mathbf k_T$ and jet plus/transverse components, never on soft $\pm$ components (they commute with fibre integration and with the deformation of §O.7); Glauber $|\mathbf k_T|\sim\lambda$ lies inside the windows, the genuine soft scale $|\mathbf k_T|\sim\lambda^2$ in $K_R$. Optional figure `fig:appO-windows`. `\uses{def:method-neighbourhoods, def:appO-leading-data}`.
- **引理 `lem:appO-equal-time` — 等光锥时刻插入** (数学). Let $\mathcal J:\mathbb R\to\mathbb C$, $J^{\rm in}(k)=\int_{-\infty}^0d\tau\,e^{-ik\tau}\mathcal J(\tau)$, $J^{\rm fi}(k)=\int_0^\infty d\tau\,e^{-ik\tau}\mathcal J(\tau)$, $J=J^{\rm in}+J^{\rm fi}$. Assume **(ET1)** $J^{\rm in}$ analytic in ${\rm Im}\,k>0$, $J^{\rm fi}$ in ${\rm Im}\,k<0$, both continuous up to the real axis; **(ET2)** constants $\mathcal J_\pm$, $M\ge|\mathcal J_\pm|$, $\lambda_J>0$ with $|J^{\rm in}(k)-\frac{i\mathcal J_-}{k}|\le\frac{M\lambda_J}{|k|^2}$ (${\rm Im}\,k\ge0$, $|k|\ge2\lambda_J$) and $|J^{\rm fi}(k)+\frac{i\mathcal J_+}{k}|\le\frac{M\lambda_J}{|k|^2}$ (${\rm Im}\,k\le0$, $|k|\ge2\lambda_J$); $R$ analytic in $|k|<\Lambda$ with $|R|\le C_R|R(0)|$ there, $\Lambda\ge8\lambda_J$. Then: (i) if $\mathcal J$ is piecewise $C^1$ near 0 with integrable derivative, $\mathcal J_\pm=\mathcal J(0^\pm)$; (ii) (window form) for $2\lambda_J\le K\le\Lambda/2$: $\int_{-K}^K\frac{dk}{2\pi}JR=R(0)\frac{\mathcal J_++\mathcal J_-}{2}+\frac{iKR'(0)}{\pi}(\mathcal J_--\mathcal J_+)+O\big(M|R(0)|[\frac{\lambda_J}{K}+\frac{K^2}{\Lambda^2}]\big)$; for an asymmetric window $[-K_1,K_2]$ with $2\lambda_J\le K_{1,2}\le\Lambda/2$ the same holds with error $O(M|R(0)|[\lambda_J/\min(K_1,K_2)+\max(K_1,K_2)^2/\Lambda^2])$, and if $\mathcal J_+=\mathcal J_-$ the $K^2/\Lambda^2$ term is absent; (iii) (full axis) if moreover $R=\sum_ir_i/(k-z_i)$ is a finite sum of simple poles with $|z_i|\ge2\Lambda$, $R_\downarrow$ ($R_\uparrow$) the terms with ${\rm Im}\,z_i<0$ ($>0$) and $\|R\|=\sum_i|r_i/z_i|$:
  $\int_{-\infty}^\infty\frac{dk}{2\pi}JR=\mathcal J_+R_\downarrow(0)+\mathcal J_-R_\uparrow(0)+O\big(\frac{M\lambda_J}{\Lambda}\|R\|\big)=R(0)\big[\mathcal J_-+c\,(\mathcal J_+-\mathcal J_-)\big]+O\big(\frac{M\lambda_J}{\Lambda}\|R\|\big)$, $c\equiv R_\downarrow(0)/R(0)$.
  `% uses: none`. Proof: full — (i) integration by parts; (ii) move $J^{\rm in}$ to the upper and $J^{\rm fi}$ to the lower semicircle of radius $K$ ($\int_{C^\pm}dk/k=\mp i\pi$), expand $R=R(0)+R'(0)k+\rho$ with Cauchy estimates; asymmetric windows: semicircle of radius $\min K$ plus a real segment where (ET2) holds; (iii) **corrected proof**: $\int J^{\rm in}R_\downarrow=0=\int J^{\rm fi}R_\uparrow$; write $J^{\rm fi}=-i\mathcal J_+/(k-i\lambda_J)+E(k)$ with $E$ analytic in the closed lower half-plane and $|E|\le3M\lambda_J/|k|^2$; close in the lower half-plane: $\int\frac{dk}{2\pi}ER_\downarrow=-i\sum_{{\rm Im}z_i<0}r_iE(z_i)$, $|\cdot|\le\frac{3M\lambda_J}{2\Lambda}\|R\|$ (no derivative bound on the real axis is needed). Check: `refdoc/work_R7/check_e28.py`, `check_e28_asym.py` (error $\propto\lambda_J/\Lambda$; window errors within bound; asymmetric windows $O(\lambda_J/\min K)$). Surpass: ERR-m26 (proof of (iii); window-shape condition).
- **引理 `lem:appO-insertion-values` — 光锥序中的插入：(ET1)(ET2) 与等时值** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$ …）（本引理只涉及部分子态。）For one insertion $O$ (amplitude side, injected energy $k\equiv k^-$, other variables real) into a leading-power LCOPT jet factor $J_A$ — a sum of ordering terms with $V_H$ at $\tau=0$ and denominators of the form of `lem:dy-poles-orientation`, terms polynomial in $k$ dropped as in `lem:appO-glauber-pc` (the cut-summed $J_A$ of `lem:appO-unitarity` is of this form): (a) (ET1)(ET2) of `lem:appO-equal-time` hold with $\lambda_J\le C_\Gamma(\nu_A+\sum_i|k_i^-|)$ (other not yet localized insertions), $\nu_A$ the jet energy scale of `def:appO-windows`; (b) $\mathcal J_-$ ($\mathcal J_+$) is the sum of the LCOPT terms with $O$ immediately before (after) $V_H$; in operator language ${\rm T}\{V_H(0)O(0^-)\}=V_H(0)O(0)$, ${\rm T}\{V_H(0)O(0^+)\}=O(0)V_H(0)$, so $\mathcal J_+-\mathcal J_-$ is a matrix element of $[O(0),V_H(0)]$ and equals $\mathcal J_+^{\rm act}-\mathcal J_-^{\rm act}$ (terms with $O$ on an active line adjacent to $V_H$); (c) if $O$ sits on a spectator and no other non-commuting insertion is localized at $\tau=0$ on the same side and parton, $\mathcal J_+=\mathcal J_-\equiv\mathcal J(0)$, and $J(k)=O(\lambda_J/k^2)$ for $|k|\gg\lambda_J$: eikonal-region soft gluons do not couple to spectators at leading power (LCOPT form of the decoupling in `lem:method-ward-multi`); (d) every chain term $T_c$ of `def:method-subtraction` preserves (ET1)(ET2) with the same constants (eikonalized lines have zero on-shell energy, so $\lambda_J$ only decreases). `\uses{lem:appO-equal-time, lem:dy-poles-orientation, lem:appO-glauber-pc, def:appO-windows, def:method-subtraction}`. Proof: full (by `lem:dy-poles-orientation`, $J^{\rm in}$ has only initial and $J^{\rm fi}$ only final denominators; $|(A_D+k)^{-1}-k^{-1}|\le2|A_D|/|k|^2$; zero-duration states between $O$ and $V_H$ for spectator lines). Error type: exact given the E-S approximations.
- **注 `rem:appO-constant-c` — 常数 $c$ 与正规化依赖性** (no tag). For a direct line at fixed $k^+\ne0$, $\mathbf k_T$: $R\propto(2k^+k-\mathbf k_T^2+i0)^{-1}$, one pole at $\mathbf k_T^2/(2k^+)$ (lower half-plane for $k^+>0$); with $\Lambda=\mathbf k_T^2/(4|k^+|)$, `lem:appO-equal-time`(iii) gives $c=\theta(k^+)$, whereas the $G_\delta$ fibre window $|k|\le\delta\mathbf k_T^2/|k^+|$ gives $c=\frac12-\frac{2i}{\pi}\delta\,{\rm sgn}(k^+)+O(\delta^3)$ (check: `refdoc/work_R7/check_e28.py`). The two differ by contributions from outside $G_\delta$ (connecting line near shell). Only active-line insertions ($\mathcal J_+\ne\mathcal J_-$) feel the choice: ordering convention ${\rm T}_c\{V_H(0)O(0)\}=V_H(0)O(0)+c[O(0),V_H(0)]$ (contact term). Window errors contain $O(\delta^2)$, not a power of $\lambda$; hence the rewriting uses the full-axis form (iii).
- **命题 `prop:appO-ss-localize` — 一条旁观者–旁观者线的局域化与 Glauber 算符** [R, R_cond]. （$R_{\rm cond}$ …）前提：\cref{asm:appO-lcopt}。（本命题只涉及部分子态。）$(\Gamma,R)\in\mathcal G_0$; one amplitude-side SS line $j$ with the other insertions real and fixed, $\Lambda_j\equiv\mathbf k_{Tj}^2/(4|k_j^+|)$, $G_j=(2k_j^+k_j^--\mathbf k_{Tj}^2+i0)^{-1}$ kept exact (not replaced by $-1/\mathbf k_{Tj}^2$). (i) For $\Lambda_j\ge8\lambda_J^A$: $\big|\int\frac{dk_j^-}{2\pi}J_AR-R(0)\mathcal J_A(0)\big|\le C'M\lambda_J^A|R(0)|/\Lambda_j$ (**absolute** bound; $\mathcal J_A(0)$ may be small relative to $M$); (ii) after both fibre integrals ($k_j^-$, then $k_j^+$, using $\int\frac{dk^+}{2\pi}J_B=\mathcal J_B(0)$ and the tail $J_B=O(\nu_B/(k^+)^2)$) the error is bounded by $C\,M|R(0)|\,x(1+\ln\frac1x)$, $x\equiv\nu_A\nu_B/\mathbf k_{Tj}^2$, which is $O(\lambda^{a_G}L)$ on the SS window ($4\nu_A\nu_B\le\lambda^{a_G}\mathbf k_T^2$); the logarithm is genuine; (iii) in the window the line inserts, in the closed-time-path matrix element of $J_A\otimes J_B$ at $\tau_A=\tau_B=0$, the operator $-iV_G$ (conjugate side $+iV_G$), $V_G=\int d^2\mathbf b\,d^2\mathbf b'\,\rho_A^a(\mathbf b)\chi(\mathbf b-\mathbf b')\rho_B^a(\mathbf b')$, $\chi(\mathbf b)=g^2\int\frac{d^2\mathbf k_T}{(2\pi)^2}\frac{e^{i\mathbf k_T\cdot\mathbf b}}{\mathbf k_T^2}\Theta(\mathbf k_T)$, $\rho_A^a(\mathbf b)=\int dx^-J_A^{+a}(0,x^-,\mathbf b)$ restricted to spectators, coupling $+igJ^{\mu a}A^a_\mu$ (binding convention); on the window core ($\Theta=1$ for all side assignments) $\chi$ is a real even kernel in $\mathbf k_T$ alone and $V_G$ is Hermitian and diagonal in transverse positions; $\Theta$ cuts off $\mathbf k_T\to0$, so $\chi$ is IR finite. `\uses{asm:appO-lcopt, lem:appO-unitarity, lem:appO-equal-time, lem:appO-insertion-values, lem:appO-glauber-pc, def:appO-windows}`. Proof: full for (i)(iii); (ii) full for $|k_j^+|\le X_j\equiv\mathbf k_T^2/(32C_\Gamma\nu_A)$, scaling estimate beyond (E-S). Check: `refdoc/work_R7/check_e31.py` (relative error $/[x(1+\ln(1/x))]\approx0.41$ constant over eight decades). Normalization: $(+ig)^2(-i)/(-\mathbf k_T^2)$ per exchange; agrees with $\chi=-\frac{e_Ae_B}{2\pi}K_0(m_gb)$ of `prop:dy-abelian-model` after $e_Ae_B\to g^2\mathbf T_A\cdot\mathbf T_B$ and with the Rothstein–Stewart phase ($e^{-iV_G}=e^{i\phi}$, `RothsteinStewart2016`). Error type: E-F (plus E-S beyond $X_j$). Surpass: ERR-m26 (vertex $+ig$; absolute bound).
- **引理 `lem:appO-joint-localization` — 非对易插入的联合局域化：次序权重** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$ …）（本引理只涉及部分子态。）Let $O_1,\dots,O_N$ be insertions on the same spectator parton and side, with non-commuting colour matrices, and let their external factors be single poles $R_j(k_j)=r_j/(k_j-z_j)$, $z_j=\mathbf k_{Tj}^2/(2k_j^+)\mp i0$ (direct lines with the $J_B$ dependence frozen). (a) (*weights*) If all $z_j$ lie in the lower half-plane ($k_j^+>0$), the equal-time limit of the insertions is $\sum_\pi w_\pi\,O_{\pi(N)}\cdots O_{\pi(1)}$ ($\pi(1)$ earliest) with $w_\pi=\prod_jz_j\big/\prod_{m=1}^N\sum_{l\ge m}z_{\pi(l)}$, $\sum_\pi w_\pi=1$ (for $N=2$: $w_{12}=z_1/(z_1+z_2)=k_2^+\mathbf k_{T1}^2/(k_1^+\mathbf k_{T2}^2+k_2^+\mathbf k_{T1}^2)$); poles in opposite half-planes fix the order (weights 0 or 1; insertions with $k_j^+<0$ precede those with $k_j^+>0$); in particular the weights depend on $k_j^+$ (variables of the other jet), are piecewise in $k^+$ and non-analytic at $k_j^+=0$; (b) (*localization error*) for a leading-power LCOPT jet factor $J_A$ as in `lem:appO-insertion-values`, $\int\prod_j\frac{dk_j}{2\pi}J\prod_jR_j=\prod_jR_j(0)\sum_\pi w_\pi\mathcal J_\pi+O\big(C_NM\sum_j\frac{\lambda_J}{|z_j|}\prod_j|R_j(0)|\big)$, $\mathcal J_\pi$ the matrix element with the insertions at $\tau=0$ in the order $\pi$ (relative to $V_H$ as in `lem:appO-insertion-values`(b)); (c) with the strict approximation $G_j\to-1/\mathbf k_{Tj}^2$ and independent symmetric truncations in $k^-$ and $k^+$, each jet's order appears with weight $\frac12$ independently (for $N=2$: $w=\frac14$ for each of the four ordering pairs). `\uses{lem:appO-equal-time, lem:appO-insertion-values}`. Proof: (a) full (integrate $\prod_jiz_je^{-iz_j\tau_j}\theta(\tau_j)$ over the ordering sectors; induction on $N$); (b) full, **new**: time representation sector by sector — in each ordering sector $J$ is a finite sum of exponentials with frequencies of modulus $\le\lambda_J$ (`lem:appO-lcopt`); integrate by parts in each $\tau_j$ inside the sector; boundary terms at the sector walls reproduce (a), the remainders are bounded as in the corrected proof of `lem:appO-equal-time`(iii) (closing in the half-plane of each $z_j$); the App O writer writes it out and checks it numerically by extending `refdoc/work_R7/check_weights_colour.py` to finite $\lambda_J/\Lambda$ (if a gap appears, (b) is stated for $N=2$ and the general case goes into `asm:appO-mixing`(c) — report); (c) full (symmetric window: principal value vanishes, each $\delta$ part $\frac12$). VF-REF-12. Check: `refdoc/work_R7/check_weights_colour.py`, `refdoc/work_R8` K12 (weights sum to 1; dependence on $\mathbf k_{T1}^2/\mathbf k_{T2}^2$). Error type: (a)(c) exact, (b) E-F. **Replaces** the fixed-order hypothesis (c1) of the earlier formulation, which is false per graph (a fixed order differs from the true value by $w_{21}[O_1,O_2]=O(1)$; ERR-S3). Consequence (print): the rewritten $J_A$ now depends on $k_j^+$ through the weights, non-analytically at $k_j^+=0$ — the same obstruction as for H-emission endpoints; coincident SA/AS insertions therefore join `asm:appO-deformation`(d). Sharpened result, not marked new (verify claim N13).
- **引理 `lem:appO-glauber-operator` — Glauber 插入的非阿贝尔算符结构** [R, R_def]. （$R_{\rm def}$ …）Let $V_G=\sum_{i,j}\chi_{ij}\mathbf T_i\cdot\mathbf T'_j$ with real $\chi_{ij}$, $\mathbf T_i$ ($\mathbf T'_j$) the colour generators of spectator $i$ of $A$ ($j$ of $B$) (the form produced by `prop:appO-ss-localize`(iii); pointer). An ordering convention assigns weights $w(\pi_A,\pi_B)\in\mathbb C$, $\sum w=1$, and $\mathcal A_N=\frac{(-i)^N}{N!}\sum_{(i_e,j_e)}\prod_e\chi_{i_ej_e}\sum_{\pi_A,\pi_B}w(\pi_A,\pi_B)[\prod^{\pi_A}_eT^{a_e}_{i_e}][\prod^{\pi_B}_eT'^{a_e}_{j_e}]$, $\mathcal A_1=-iV_G$. (a) $V_G$ is Hermitian and $\mathcal U_G=e^{-iV_G}$ is unitary (formal power series); (b) abelian: $\mathcal A_N=(-iV_G)^N/N!$ for any weights; (c) common ordering ($w$ supported on $\pi_A=\pi_B$, generated by a common non-negative time distribution): $\sum_N\mathcal A_N={\rm T}\exp(-i\int dt\,V(t))$ with Hermitian $V(t)$, unitary; (d) for one spectator per hadron, $X\equiv\mathbf T_A\cdot\mathbf T_B$, and $p_{\rm opp}$ the total weight of opposite orderings: $\mathcal A_2=\frac{(-i\chi)^2}{2}\big[X^2+p_{\rm opp}\frac{C_A}{2}X\big]$ and $\mathcal A_2+\mathcal A_2^\dagger+\mathcal A_1^\dagger\mathcal A_1=-\frac12C_A\chi^2({\rm Re}\,p_{\rm opp})X$ (crossed $-$ ladder $=\frac{C_A}{2}X$; $\sum_{a,b}\{T^a,T^b\}\otimes\{T'^a,T'^b\}=4X^2+C_AX$); (e) with equal weights $w=\frac14$ for the four ordering pairs ($p_{\rm opp}=\frac12$; the strict-window weights of `lem:appO-joint-localization`(c), pointer): $(1+\mathcal A_1+\mathcal A_2)^\dagger(1+\mathcal A_1+\mathcal A_2)=1-\frac14C_A\chi^2X+O(\chi^3)$; on a colour-singlet $q\bar q$ pair ($X=-C_F$) the "probability" is $1+\frac14C_AC_F\chi^2>1$. `% uses: none` (self-contained colour algebra). Proof: full (colour algebra; Dyson series). VF-REF-10. Checks: `refdoc/work_R8/check_colour.py` (SU(2,3,4); F×F, F×F̄, F×A, A×A; common ordering with 2+2 spectators unitary to $4\times10^{-19}$, independent orderings not), `refdoc/work_R7/check_weights_colour.py`. Meaning (print): the residual is controlled by ${\rm Re}\,p_{\rm opp}$ alone; the exact two-gluon exchange between eikonal lines gives $p_{\rm opp}=-iY/\pi$ (`prop:dy-eikonal-twogluon`, proof in §O.8), so the strict-window residual of (e) is a **truncation artefact** (ERR-S1).
- **定义 `def:appO-family` — 旁观者交换族与积分映射** (no tag). $(\Gamma_0,R_0)\in\mathcal G_0$ without SS lines (the *core*). The *family* $\mathfrak F(\Gamma_0)$ of a given order: all forward graphs obtained by adding any number of SS lines to the spectator partons of $\Gamma_0$ (each on the amplitude or conjugate side), up to that order; $R_\Gamma$ = $R_0$ plus all SS lines soft. A *connection* $p$ is a multiset of SS lines with their partons $(i_l,j_l)$; a *side assignment* gives each line a side; $\Theta^{\rm SS}_\Gamma=\prod_{l\in{\rm SS}}\Theta_l$; $\mathfrak P_\Gamma$ integrates all four components of all SS momenta in $\Theta^{\rm SS}_\Gamma\tilde F_{\Gamma,R_\Gamma}$, giving a function on the space of $\Gamma_0$. $p$ is *coincident* if two localized insertions (SS, or SS with SA/AS) sit on the same spectator parton (any sides). Parts with some SS momentum outside its window belong to $K_{R_\Gamma}$ or to other regions of $\Gamma$ (SS line collinear or hard) and are handled by App N's subtractions. `\uses{def:appO-leading-data, def:appO-windows}`.
- **命题 `prop:appO-ss-cancel` — 旁观者–旁观者 Glauber 交换在族求和后相消** [R, R_cond]. （$R_{\rm cond}$ …）前提：\cref{asm:appO-lcopt}。（本命题只涉及部分子态。）For a family $\mathfrak F(\Gamma_0)$ with $J_A$, $J_B$ cut-summed (`lem:appO-unitarity`) and the non-SS soft endpoints localized or initial-only: (i) for non-coincident connections, on ${\rm supp}\,\Theta^{\rm SS}$, each SS line becomes a $\mp iV_G$ insertion at $\tau=0$ with the fibre-integrated error of `prop:appO-ss-localize`; (ii) $\rho_A$, $\rho_B$ act only on spectators and commute with $V_H$ and with $J_B$'s hard vertex (SS insertions before or after $V_H$ agree); (iii) for each non-coincident connection $p$ ($|p|\ge1$), summing over side assignments and cuts and applying $\mathfrak P$ gives **zero on the window core** up to the error of (i): $\sum_s\prod_{l:s_l={\rm conj}}(+iG_lX_l)\prod_{l:s_l={\rm amp}}(-iG_lX_l)=\prod_{l=1}^N(-iG_lX_l+iG_lX_l)=0$ ($X_l=t^a_{i_l}\otimes t^a_{j_l}$; the identity on $\mathcal H_A\otimes\mathcal H_B$ stands between the two sides because the cuts of $J_A$ and $J_B$ move independently and window Glauber lines are never cut); in the abelian theory all connections are non-coincident and $\sum_{\Gamma\in\mathfrak F}\mathfrak P_\Gamma[\Theta^{\rm SS}_\Gamma\tilde F_{\Gamma,R_\Gamma}]=\tilde F_{\Gamma_0,R_0}+(\text{fibre-integrated error})+(\text{window-edge terms})$; (iv) window-edge terms (the windows depend on the side assignment through $\nu_A$, $\nu_B$) lie in the soft-angle regions and are **not** estimated here (they are covered by `asm:appO-deformation`(c), §O.7 — pointer, not a dependency; slot X1c); (v) coincident connections (non-abelian) are not covered: with the joint weights of `lem:appO-joint-localization` the localized expression is defined, but the side sum need not vanish; its $O(\chi^2)$ structure is `lem:appO-glauber-operator`. `\uses{asm:appO-lcopt, lem:appO-unitarity, prop:appO-ss-localize, lem:appO-joint-localization, lem:appO-glauber-operator, def:appO-family}`. Proof: full on window cores (commutativity and completeness only). The integrated form (over the core variables) needs `asm:appO-envelope` and is part of `prop:appO-one-sided`/`thm:appO-main`. Error type: E-F. Generalizes `prop:dy-abelian-model` (arbitrary $J_A$, $J_B$, any number of spectators).
- **例 `ex:appO-single-exchange` — 单条 Glauber 交换** . Spectator model of `ex:dy-spectator-lcopt`: $J_A(k^-)=\frac{i}{D_1}\big[\frac{i}{k^--\alpha+i0}+\frac{i}{\beta-k^-+i0}\big]$, $\beta-\alpha=D_1$; $\mathcal J_-=\mathcal J_+=i/D_1$; $\int\frac{dk^-}{2\pi}J_AR=R(0)\,i/D_1$ (no extra denominator; the exchange happens at $\tau=0$ and transfers only $\mathbf k_T$); amplitude $\to-i\chi(b)\mathbf T_A\cdot\mathbf T_B\mathcal M^{(0)}(b)$, conjugate $+i\chi$, cut sum $0$ (Parseval: same $b$ on both sides); single orderings are cutoff dependent ($\frac12$ each under symmetric truncation, $\theta(\pm k^+)$ with the full propagator), their sum is not. SA: the $J_A$ end localized, the $J_B$ end initial-only (deformable along $k^+\to k^+-i\kappa$); AS: $J_A=J^{\rm in}$, $\mathcal J_+=0\ne\mathcal J_-$, localizing would give $\theta(-k^+)R(0)\mathcal J_-$ — the contact term; therefore the rewriting does not localize endpoints on absorbed active lines. Figure: redraw of the four cut–ordering configurations (in `fig:appO-cutsum` panel b, or a separate panel). `\uses{lem:appO-equal-time, lem:appO-insertion-values, prop:appO-ss-localize}`. Checks: `refdoc/work_R8/check_kinematics.py` K2–K4.
- **例 `ex:appO-two-exchange` — 两条 Glauber 交换与 $p_{\rm opp}$** . One spectator per hadron; ladder colour $X^2$, crossed $X^2+\frac12C_AX$, one exchange on each side $X^2$ (no ordering ambiguity); $\mathcal A_2$ and the unitarity defect of `lem:appO-glauber-operator`(d). Three cases: abelian (zero for any $p_{\rm opp}$); common ordering $p_{\rm opp}=0$ (realized by the Rothstein–Stewart regulator $|2k^z|^{-\eta}$: crossed Glauber boxes vanish, ladders exponentiate to a Hermitian colour phase, `RothsteinStewart2016`); strict window $p_{\rm opp}=\frac12$, residual $-\frac14C_A\chi^2X$. **Exact eikonal value** (§O.8): $p_{\rm opp}=-iY/\pi$, ${\rm Re}\,p_{\rm opp}=0$: the whole $s$-channel weight sits in the uncrossed graph, the crossed graph contributes only the reggeization phase $\propto C_AY$; the "missing" $+\frac14C_A\chi^2X$ of the strict truncation is the principal-value part of the same graphs outside the window (ERR-S1; corrected sign ERR-m27). The genuine open problem is elsewhere: non-eikonal (composite, interacting) spectators and Glauber–soft mixing through Lipatov vertices (`asm:appO-mixing`(c), §O.6 — pointer; slot X1e). `\uses{lem:appO-glauber-operator, prop:dy-eikonal-twogluon}`. Figure: pointer to `fig:dy-two-gluon-exchange` (owner Ch12).
- **[PENDING-VERIFY X1c] 引理 `lem:appO-side-independent-windows` — 与侧分配无关的窗口** — planned [R, R_PT]. *Planned statement:* windows built from side-independent quantities ($P_A^-$, on-shell energies of the cut lines and core-graph lines only) satisfy all properties used in §O.5–§O.7 (localization errors, commutation with fibre integrals and deformations), and with them the window-edge terms of `prop:appO-ss-cancel`(iv) vanish identically. *If PASS:* `def:appO-windows` uses the new windows, `prop:appO-ss-cancel`(iv) is deleted, `asm:appO-deformation`(c) loses the window-edge clause. *Fallback:* window-edge terms stay in `asm:appO-deformation`(c).
- **[PENDING-VERIFY X1e] 命题 `prop:appO-c2-twoloop` — $O(\alpha_s^2)$ 时 `asm:appO-mixing`(c) 的显式检验** — planned [R, R_PT] (that order, that model; first sentence states it). *Planned statement:* in a colour-singlet spectator model with a common regulator, at $O(\alpha_s^2)$, the sum of two-Glauber exchanges, Lipatov-vertex real emission and its virtual corrections satisfies `asm:appO-mixing`(c). *Fallback:* `ex:appO-two-exchange` only; the status of (c) beyond the eikonal $O(g^4)$ case stays open.
- **[PENDING-VERIFY X1f] 命题 `prop:appO-twoexch-spectator` — 标量旁观者模型中的两条 Glauber 交换（精确连接传播子）** — planned [R, R_PT] (that order, that model; first sentence states it). *Planned statement:* two spectator–spectator Glauber exchanges between one spectator of $A$ and one of $B$ on the amplitude side, scalar spectator model of `ex:dy-spectator-lcopt`, exact connecting propagators $1/(2k_e^+k_e^--\mathbf k_{Te}^2+i0)$: the joint ordering weights $W(\pi_A,\pi_B)$ (normalized integrals of the four colour orderings) and $p_{\rm opp}=W(12,21)+W(21,12)$ are computed exactly ($k^-$ integrals by residues, the inner $k^+$ integral by the logarithmic-residue formula, the outer numerically, $\eta\to0$); planned conclusion ${\rm Re}\,p_{\rm opp}=O(\lambda^a)$ (the non-eikonal analogue of `prop:dy-eikonal-twogluon`) or its explicit value. *Evidence:* X1 report; scripts `verify/extend/X1-glauber/scripts/twoexch_model.py`, `test_twoexch_parts*.py`, `run_twoexch_quick.py`. *If PASS:* new item (d) of `ex:appO-two-exchange` and a sentence in the 「地位」 of `asm:appO-mixing` (non-eikonal spectators at $O(\chi^2)$ in this model). *Fallback:* `lem:appO-joint-localization`(c) and `ex:appO-two-exchange` only.

### O.6 `sec:appO-rewriting` 单侧改写 (3.5 pp)

- **引理 `lem:appO-carrier` — 整函数载子** (数学). $\phi(x)=2\sqrt\pi\,e^{-x^2}$ is entire, $\int\frac{dx}{2\pi}\phi(x)=1$, $|\phi(x+iy)|\le2\sqrt\pi\,e^{y^2-x^2}$; hence for $|{\rm Im}\,k|\le\frac12w$: $|\phi(k/w)|\le2\sqrt\pi e^{1/4}$, the integral of $\phi(k/w)/w$ along any such contour equals $2\pi$, and the mass of $\phi(\cdot/w)/w$ beyond $|k|\ge b$ is $O(e^{-b^2/w^2})$. `% uses: none`. Proof: full. Check: R8 K7.
- **定义 `def:appO-weak-equivalence` — 测量相容的割图求和与弱等价** (no tag). (a) *Collinear DY:* $\mathcal C(\Gamma)$ = all final cuts through $\gamma^*$ with given $Q^2$, $y$ (spectators, unobserved jets, soft radiation and $\mathbf q_T$ integrated); $F_\Gamma\equiv\sum_{C\in\mathcal C(\Gamma)}\Gamma_C$ on the LCOPT domain (minus components of $J_A$'s internal loops and $\mathcal E$, plus components of $J_B$'s, integrated over $\mathbb R$; jet plus/transverse components and all components of uncut soft lines kept); $U_R$, $N_{R'}$ are their projections. (b) *TMD:* add $\delta^{(2)}(\mathbf q_T-\mathbf q_T(\gamma^*))$ (spectator transverse momenta stay integrated). (c) $\mathfrak B$ is the set algebra generated by the $N_{R'}(\lambda)$ (so $U_{R'}\in\mathfrak B$). $F\doteq_RF'$ iff there are $C,a>0,K$ such that for all $V\in\mathfrak B$, $V\subset U_R$, and all $\Phi\in\{1\}\cup\{C_{R'}:R'\in\mathcal R\}$ the integrals exist (each denominator with its own $i\eta_0\to0^+$, real space) and $|\int_V\Phi(F-F')\,d\mu|\le C\lambda^aL^K$ uniformly for $\lambda\in(0,\lambda_0]$, $\varepsilon\in(0,\varepsilon_0]$ (error type E-W). Weak equivalence is the only possible form: $F_\Gamma$ carries pinched $i0$ distributions and is not $L^1$, so the integral norm of `def:method-power-suppressed` is undefined for it. `\uses{def:appO-leading-data, def:method-neighbourhoods, def:method-power-suppressed, def:method-subtraction}`.
- **定义 `def:appO-rewriting` — 改写映射 $\mathfrak r_R$** (no tag). Acting on the cut-summed integrand and on every chain term: **(R1)** $J_A$, $J_B$ in the closed-time-path form of `lem:appO-unitarity`; **(R2)** for each localizable unit $u$ a unit localization operator $\mathfrak l_u$: for a $J_A$ endpoint $j$ replace the jet factor $\mathcal J(k_j^-)$ by $\bar{\mathcal J}_j\,\phi(k_j^-/w_j)/w_j$ and set $k_j^-=0$ in the external factor, with $\bar{\mathcal J}_j=\mathcal J(0)$ (spectators), $\bar{\mathcal J}_j=c_j\mathcal J_+$, $c_j=R_{j\downarrow}(0)/R_j(0)$ (active lines emitted by $V_H$; $c_j=\theta(k_j^+)$ in $\mathcal G_0$), and, for coincident insertions on one parton and side, the jointly localized value $\sum_\pi w_\pi\mathcal J_\pi$ of `lem:appO-joint-localization`; $J_B$ likewise; SS lines: both ends; endpoints on active lines **absorbed** by $V_H$ unchanged. Then $\mathfrak r_RG\equiv\prod_u[\Theta_u\mathfrak l_u+(1-\Theta_u)]G$; $\tilde F_{\Gamma,R}\equiv\mathfrak r_RF_\Gamma$; subtraction and chain terms of rewritten forms are **defined** by $C_{R'}\tilde F\equiv\mathfrak r_RC_{R'}F_\Gamma$, $T_{R_0}X_{R_0}[\tilde F]\equiv\mathfrak r_RT_{R_0}X_{R_0}[F_\Gamma]$ ("first $T_c$, then rewrite"; $T_R$ is defined only on rational functions times measurement functions). `\uses{lem:appO-carrier, def:appO-windows, lem:appO-unitarity, lem:appO-insertion-values, lem:appO-joint-localization, def:method-subtraction}`.
- **假设 `asm:appO-envelope` — 纤维积分误差的可积包络** [\tagA]. Full text in `ASSUMPTIONS.md`. Content: for each leading region $R$ there is $\hat\Phi_R\ge0$ on $U_R$, independent of the localized soft components, with $\int_{U_R}\hat\Phi_R\,d\hat\mu\le CL^K$ uniformly in $\varepsilon\in(0,\varepsilon_0]$ and in the $i0$ regulator $\eta_0$, such that for $\Gamma$ and every chain term $T_c\Gamma$: (i) the fibre-integration error of each unit localization (`prop:appO-ss-localize`, the remainder of `lem:appO-equal-time`(iii), the remainder of `lem:appO-joint-localization`(b), truncation terms at fibre endpoints), with the other $i0$ replaced by $i\eta_0$, is bounded pointwise by $C\lambda^a\hat\Phi_R$; (ii) on boundary layers of relative width $\lambda^a$ (including fibre endpoints set by neighbourhoods $N_{R'}$ with $\beta_{R'}>\beta_R$), $\int\hat\Phi_R\le C\lambda^aL^K$. `\uses{def:appO-rewriting, def:appO-windows}`.
- **假设 `asm:appO-mixing` — Glauber–软混合与重合连接的完整图集相消** [\tagA]. Full text in `ASSUMPTIONS.md`. Content: $\Gamma$ a DY forward graph, $R$ a leading region, windows as in `def:appO-windows`, $\hat\Phi_R$ as in `asm:appO-envelope`. (a) (*smooth external factors*) for every localizable endpoint $j$ (also when attached to an internal vertex of $S$), at $\Theta_j>0$ the factor outside $J_A$ (after integrating $S$'s internal loops) splits as $R_j=R_{j\downarrow}+R_{j\uparrow}$ with the half-plane analyticity and bounds used in `lem:appO-equal-time`(iii), and $\nu_A/\Lambda_j\le\lambda^{a'}$, except on a set $E$ with $\int_E\hat\Phi_R\le C\lambda^aL^K$; same for $J_B$; (b) (*cut compatibility of $S$*) after the localization of (a) the cut sum of $J_A\otimes J_B\otimes S$ takes the closed-time-path form of `lem:appO-unitarity` with $S$'s fields as $\langle0|\bar{\rm T}\{\cdots\}{\rm T}\{\cdots\}|0\rangle$, and the $\tau=0$ insertions with both ends on spectators (also through $S$) assemble into operators on $\mathcal H_A\otimes\mathcal H_B\otimes\mathcal H_S$; (c) (*complete graph set*) for the set $\mathfrak G$ of all forward graphs of the given order with the given $J_A$, $J_B$ (closed under adding soft three- and four-gluon vertices between Glauber and soft lines, including real soft emission through Lipatov-type vertices), summed over all coincident connections and side assignments, combining the window with the adjacent near-on-shell soft region of $U_R$ (including real emission), the contribution after $\mathfrak P$ is $O(\lambda^aL^K)$ in the weak sense; equivalently $\mathcal W'^\dagger\mathcal W=1+O(\lambda^a)$ on spectator states at $\tau=0$. The per-graph fixed-order clause of the earlier formulation is deleted (replaced by `lem:appO-joint-localization`). `\uses{def:appO-windows, def:appO-family, def:appO-weak-equivalence, asm:appO-envelope, lem:appO-unitarity, lem:appO-equal-time}`.
- **命题 `prop:appO-one-sided` — 割图求和后的单侧性（弱等价）** [R, R_cond]. （$R_{\rm cond}$ …）前提：\cref{asm:appO-lcopt}、\cref{asm:appO-envelope}、\cref{asm:appN-envelope}、\cref{asm:appN-separation}（一般 $\Gamma$ 另需 \cref{asm:appO-mixing}(a)(b)）。（本命题只涉及部分子态。）Let $R$ have no H-emission endpoints. For $(\Gamma,R)\in\mathcal G_0$ (general $\Gamma$ under `asm:appO-mixing`(a)(b)), for all $V\in\mathfrak B$, $V\subset U_R$, and all chain terms $T_c$ (including $T_\varnothing=1$): $\int_V(T_cF_\Gamma-\mathfrak r_RT_cF_\Gamma)\,d\mu=O(\lambda^aL^K)$; in particular $F_\Gamma\doteq_R\tilde F_{\Gamma,R}$. In $\tilde F_{\Gamma,R}$ and every $\mathfrak r_RT_cF_\Gamma$: (a) where $\Theta_u=1$, each uncut soft $k_j^-$ appears in $J_A$ either only in initial denominators (endpoint on an active line absorbed by $V_H$) or in no denominator (only in $\phi$), never in cut $\delta$'s; likewise $k_j^+$ in $J_B$; in particular on $U_R\setminus K_R$; (b) on $U_R\setminus K_R$ no momentum is Glauber-pinched, and for any deformation with $|{\rm Im}\,k_j^-|\le\frac12w_j$ the carriers stay bounded and their integrals unchanged. `\uses{asm:appO-lcopt, asm:appO-envelope, asm:appN-envelope, asm:appN-separation, asm:appO-mixing, def:appO-rewriting, def:appO-weak-equivalence, lem:appO-unitarity, lem:appO-insertion-values, lem:appO-joint-localization, prop:appO-ss-localize, lem:appO-carrier, lem:dy-poles-orientation}`. Proof: full given the premises — (R1) exact; (R2) telescoping one unit at a time; whole real axis: spectator endpoints in $\mathcal G_0$ have one external pole at distance $2\Lambda_j$ with $\nu_A/\Lambda_j\le\lambda^{a_G}$ at $\Theta_u>0$; emitted active lines give $c_jR(0)\mathcal J_+$; SS lines by `prop:appO-ss-localize`; coincident insertions by `lem:appO-joint-localization`(b); fibre vs real axis: tails $O(\nu_A/|b|)$ and carrier mass $O(e^{-b^2/w^2})$, boundary layers by `asm:appO-envelope`(ii); chain terms (step (O-4)): $T_c$ acts on $H$ (projections; merged with $V_H$), on small soft components ($k\to\pi_\mp(k)$, orientation of $p^2-M^2+2p^+k^-+i0$ kept because $p^+$ keeps its sign on $U_R$ by `asm:appN-separation`(ii)), on $S$ (kept or eikonalized) — none alters the ordering structure, (ET1)(ET2) are preserved with $\lambda_J$ not increased, windows are fixed functions; integrate with `asm:appO-envelope`(i); integral forms of the E-S approximations by `asm:appN-envelope`. Error type: **E-W**. Surpass: per-graph statement now also for coincident insertions (ERR-S3).
- **注 `rem:appO-weak` — 为何只有弱等价；SA/AS 线；窗口外** (no tag). (1) An equal-time insertion cannot be represented by a $k^-$ function with only initial denominators (upper-half-plane analytic with $k^{-2}$ decay integrates to 0; with $k^{-1}$ decay it is cutoff dependent) — hence the entire carrier. (2) SA lines: localized at the $A$ end ($k_j^-$ only in $\phi$), $k_j^+$ only in $J_B$'s initial denominators; §O.7 moves $k_j^-$ by $\le\frac12w_j$ and $k_j^+$ by $\sim\lambda^{-a_G}|\mathbf k_{Tj}|$; their allocation among subtraction terms (the $A$-end Glauber recoil is not reproduced by $T_R$) is part of `asm:appO-rewritten-cs`. (3) On $K_R$ (including the genuine soft scale $k\sim\lambda^2(1,1,1)$, where $\Lambda_j\sim\nu_A$ and the localization error is $O(1)$) nothing is rewritten and no contour is provided: `asm:appO-deformation`(c). (4) The final result is independent of the auxiliary choices $a_G,\delta,\kappa,\rho,\vartheta$ (the integral does not depend on them); only the error constants do.
- **注 `rem:appO-h-emission` — H-发射端点的障碍** (no tag). For localizable endpoints on active lines emitted by $V_H$ ($\tau>0$ only: $J=J^{\rm fi}$, $\mathcal J_-=0$), the localized value $c_j\mathcal J_+R_j(0)=\mathcal J_+R_{j\downarrow}(0)$ depends on the half-plane of the external pole; in $\mathcal G_0$, $c_j=\theta(k_j^+)$, non-analytic in the component seen by the other jet. Localizing the other end then meets a non-analytic external factor, and deforming $k_j^+$ is blocked; not localizing leaves $k_j^-$ final-state one-sided with a **future-pointing** eikonal factor whose combination with the Ward identity is not analysed. Such regions (they occur only if $H$ emits collinear lines into $J_A$ with uncut soft lines attached) are outside the proven scope: `asm:appO-deformation`(d). The same non-analyticity arises for coincident SA/AS insertions through the ordering weights of `lem:appO-joint-localization`(a) (ERR-S3 consequence).

### O.7 `sec:appO-deformation` 同时形变 (4.5 pp)

Glauber direction (recap, no environment; VF-REF-7, row 38): $e_G=n_B-n_A$, $(e_G^+,e_G^-,\mathbf e_{G,T})=(-1,+1,\mathbf 0_T)$, $e_G^2=-2$, $n_A\cdot e_G=+1$, $n_B\cdot e_G=-1$; $(l+it\sigma fe_G)^2-m^2=(l^2-m^2+2t^2f^2)+2it\sigma f(l^+-l^-)$: the deformation only pushes real parts towards time-like, so only space-like lines with $0<m^2-l^2\le2f^2$ are dangerous. Contrast (print): deforming only $k^-$ ($v^2=0$) gives ${\rm Im}\,k^2=2\kappa k^+$ at the on-shell point and forces $\kappa\sim\mathbf k_T^2/|k^+|$ (`lem:method-i0-compat`); along $e_G$ inside the window ($k^2\le-(1-2\delta)\mathbf k_T^2$) the amplitude $\kappa|\mathbf k_T|$ is allowed. Pointer: `fig:method-glauber-direction`, `rem:method-two-component` (Ch9).

- **定义 `def:appO-deformation-map` — 形变映射与 Glauber 流** (no tag). $0<\kappa\le\frac12$, $\rho$ = twice the soft-neighbourhood radius; switch $\psi(q)=\vartheta\big(\frac{|q^+q^-|}{\delta q_T^2}\big)\vartheta\big(\frac{\|q\|}{\rho}\big)$ ($\psi=0$ at $q_T=0$; $\psi=1$ on the core $\{|q^+q^-|\le\frac12\delta q_T^2,\ \|q\|\le\frac12\rho\}$; $q\mapsto\psi(q)|q_T|$ continuous, locally Lipschitz on $\{q_T\ne0\}$). (i) *Direct lines* ($k_j$ into $J_A$, side $\sigma_j$): $k_j^-\mapsto k_j^-+i\sigma_j\kappa\psi_As_A|\mathbf k_{Tj}|$, $k_j^+\mapsto k_j^+-i\sigma_j\kappa\psi_Bs_B|\mathbf k_{Tj}|$, $\mathbf k_{Tj}$ fixed; AA: $\psi_A=\psi_B=\psi(k_j)$, $s_A=s_B=1$ (i.e. $k_j\mapsto k_j+i\sigma_j\kappa\psi|\mathbf k_{Tj}|e_G$); SA (A end localizable): $\psi_A=\psi\Theta'_j$, $s_A=\lambda^{a_G}$, $\psi_B=\psi$, $s_B=1+(\lambda^{-a_G}-1)\Theta'_j$ — at $\Theta'_j=1$, $k_j^-$ moves by $\kappa w_j\le\frac12w_j$ and $k_j^+$ by $\kappa\lambda^{-a_G}|\mathbf k_{Tj}|$; for $0<\Theta'_j<1$, $k_j^-$ moves by $\kappa\psi\Theta'_jw_j$ (corrected statement; fixed only where $\Theta'_j=0$); AS by $A\leftrightarrow B$; SS: not deformed; H-emission endpoints: not defined (`rem:appO-h-emission`). (ii) *Glauber flow* on a $\sigma$-side uncut soft component $C$ with both $A$- and $B$-legs and no localized legs: real $f_l$ on every line (incl. legs) with conservation at internal vertices of $C$, $f=0$ on cut lines, $f_a\ge\kappa\psi(q_a)|q_{Ta}|$ on $A$-legs, $-f_b\ge\kappa\psi(q_b)|q_{Tb}|$ on $B$-legs; deformation $l\mapsto l+it\sigma f_le_G$, $t\in[0,1]$, realized by loop-momentum deformations closed through $J_A\to H\to J_B$; $\Omega_t$, $\Omega\equiv\Omega_1$. Not covered: components with localized legs and internal vertices; components with only one kind of legs (`asm:appO-deformation`(a)). Optional figure `fig:appO-glauber-flow`. `\uses{def:appO-windows, def:appO-leading-data, def:method-neighbourhoods}`.
- **引理 `lem:appO-no-crossing` — 形变不跨越奇点** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$ …）（本引理只涉及部分子态。）$0<\kappa\le\frac12$, $0<\delta\le\frac18$. Let $F$ be any integrand with the structure of the rewritten forms: (F1) $J_A$ depends on soft momenta only through initial denominators $D=\mathcal A_D+\sum_{a\in I(D)}q_a^-+i\sigma0$ and polynomial numerators, localized $A$-legs only through $\phi(q_a^-/w_a)$ ($J_B$ alike); (F2) $H$, $H^\dagger$ depend only on $l_A^+,l_B^-,q$ and the measurement only on $q$ (TMD: transverse momenta); (F3) uncut soft lines carry exact propagators, localized units as in `def:appO-rewriting`. Then for all $t\in[0,1]$ on $\Omega_t$: (a) every $J_A$, $J_B$ energy denominator obeys $\sigma{\rm Im}\,D\ge\sigma{\rm Im}\,D|_{t=0}>0$; (b) every deformed direct line with $\psi>0$ has ${\rm Re}(k_t^2)\le-\frac14\mathbf k_T^2$; carriers on localized legs have $|{\rm Im}\,k_j^\mp|\le\frac12w_j$, hence $|\phi|\le2\sqrt\pi e^{1/4}$; (c) every other uncut soft line $l$ with non-zero flow is non-singular provided at the real point $(N_l)$: $l^2-m_l^2\ge0$ or $m_l^2-l^2>2f_l^2$; (d) $H$, collinear lines without soft momentum, cut $\delta$'s and the measurement are unaffected; (e) approximator-generated denominators of $C_{R'}F$ are non-zero **provided the eikonal denominators take the past-pointing prescription**: soft-approximated jet denominators keep the form of (F1), and eikonal denominators $n_A\cdot K+i\sigma0$, $n_B\cdot K'+i\sigma0$ (or with $n_A(y_A)$, $n_B(y_B)$ of `def:appN-tmd-approx`) satisfy $\sigma{\rm Im}\ge0$, since ${\rm Im}(n_A\cdot K)=t\sigma\sum f_a$, ${\rm Im}(n_B\cdot K')=-t\sigma\sum f_b$, $n_A(y_A)\cdot e_G=1+e^{-2y_A}>0$, $n_B(y_B)\cdot e_G=-(1+e^{2y_B})<0$. Hence if every flow-carrying soft internal line satisfies $(N_l)$ (vacuous in $\mathcal G_0$), $\int_{\mathbb R}F=\int_\Omega F$ (deform at $i\eta_0$, then $\eta_0\to0$). `\uses{def:appO-deformation-map, def:appO-rewriting, lem:appO-carrier, lem:dy-poles-orientation, def:appN-tmd-approx}`. Proof: full — (a) ${\rm Im}\,q_a^-=t\sigma f_a$; a final denominator would give $\eta_0-t\sum f_a$ and cross zero (the single-cut-graph pinch); (b) ${\rm Re}(k_t^2)=k^2+2t^2\kappa^2\psi_A\psi_Bs_As_B\mathbf k_T^2\le(2\delta-1+2\kappa^2)\mathbf k_T^2\le-\frac14\mathbf k_T^2$ (scan: maximum $-0.388\,\mathbf k_T^2$, R8 K9); (c) from the deformed-denominator formula; (e) σ restored (ERR-m27); Stokes with boundary faces $|\mathbf k_{Tj}|=\gamma\to0$. Error type: exact.
- **定义 `def:appO-graded` — 分级构型** (no tag). $C$ a two-leg-type component with $N_C$ legs, $E$ an open set of real soft momenta. $C$ is *graded on $E$* (constant $K\ge1$) if there are positive functions $s_1\ge\dots\ge s_m$ on $E$ and levels $i(l)$ with **(GC1)** $\|l\|\le s_{i(l)}$; **(GC2)** $l$ space-like ⇒ $m_l^2-l^2\ge s_{i(l)}^2/K$; **(GC3)** each leg $e$ with $\psi(q_e)>0$ has a path $\pi_e$ in $C$ to a leg of the other jet through lines with $i(l)\le i(e)$. ($m=1$: single soft scale; $s_{i+1}\le\theta s_i$: strong ordering.) Renamed items: the reference's (S1)–(S3) are (GC1)–(GC3). `\uses{def:appO-deformation-map}`.
- **定义 `def:appO-target` — 形变目标与相容性** (no tag). $G$ an integrand (e.g. $\tilde F_{\Gamma,R}$); $h:[0,1]\times\mathbb R^N\to\mathbb C^N$ continuous, $h_0={\rm id}$, moving only the $\pm$ components of $R$'s uncut soft loop momenta; $\Omega=h_1(\mathbb R^N)$ is a *deformation target* for $G$ if (a) $h_t(x)=x$ for large $\|k\|$ or when every soft momentum of $R$ is outside a fixed neighbourhood of $G_\delta$; (b) on $h_1(U_R\setminus K_R)$ every uncut soft momentum appearing in a denominator of $G$ has $|k_j^+||k_j^-|\ge c\,\mathbf k_{Tj}^2$ ($c$ independent of $\lambda$); (c) for all $t$, $h_t(\mathbb R^N)$ contains no singularity of $G$ or of $C_{R'}G$. It is *compatible with $\mathfrak B$* if moreover (d) $\int_VG'd\mu=\int_{h_1(V)}G'd\mu_\Omega$ for all $V\in\mathfrak B$, $V\subset U_R$, $G'\in\{G\}\cup\{C_{R'}G\}$. (a)–(c) are the Glauber part of (GA1)(a) and of the first part of (GA4)(iii) (`asm:appN-contour`, `asm:appN-separation`); a sufficient condition for (d): each moved component's fibre of $V$ is a finite union of intervals at whose endpoints $h_t$ is the identity; general $V$ fail it. `\uses{def:appO-windows, def:appO-weak-equivalence, def:appO-pinch, def:method-subtraction}`.
- **命题 `prop:appO-target` — $\mathcal G_0$ 与分级构型的形变目标** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$ …）（本命题只涉及部分子态。）$0<\delta\le\frac18$, $0<\kappa\le\frac12$. (i) For $\Gamma\in\mathcal G_0$ without H-emission endpoints, $\Omega$ of `def:appO-deformation-map`(i) is a deformation target ((a)–(c) of `def:appO-target`); on $U_R\setminus K_R$ at $\|k\|\le\rho/2$ every deformed soft momentum satisfies $|k^+||k^-|\ge c\,\mathbf k_T^2$ with $c=\min(\kappa^2,\delta/2)$; (ii) for general $\Gamma$: if every two-leg-type component without localized legs is graded on $E_C$ and $\kappa^2<1/(2KN_C^2)$, there is a Glauber flow such that every line satisfies $(N_l)$ on $E_C$ and every leg $|q_e^+||q_e^-|\ge cq_{Te}^2$; (iii) gluing over a finite cover $\{E_\alpha\}$ by a partition of unity for $\kappa^2<\min_\alpha1/(2K_\alpha N_C^2)$, provided components with localized legs and internal vertices and single-leg-type components are absent. `\uses{lem:appO-no-crossing, def:appO-graded, def:appO-target, def:appO-deformation-map}`. Proof: full — (i) independent loop momenta for direct lines; if $|k^+k^-|\le\frac12\delta\mathbf k_T^2$ then $\psi=1$ and $|k^+||k^-|\ge|{\rm Im}\,k^+||{\rm Im}\,k^-|=\kappa^2\mathbf k_T^2$, else $\ge|{\rm Re}\,k^+||{\rm Re}\,k^-|>\frac12\delta\mathbf k_T^2$ (scan: minimum ratio 1.0006, R8 K10); (ii) routing $f_l=\sum_{e:l\in\pi_e}\pm\kappa\psi(q_e)|q_{Te}|$, $|f_l|\le\kappa N_Cs_{i(l)}$, space-like $l$: $m^2-l^2\ge s^2/K>2\kappa^2N_C^2s^2\ge2f_l^2$; (iii) the conditions are convex in $f$ at fixed real points. Error type: exact (geometry). Content new relative to the literature at this level of precision (not marked; constants explicit).
- **假设 `asm:appO-deformation` — 一般构型、相容性、软角区与 H-发射端点** [\tagA]. Full text in `ASSUMPTIONS.md`. Content: for any $\Gamma$, $R$, $F=\tilde F_{\Gamma,R}$: (a) a deformation target exists (Glauber flows; two separately conserved flows on localized legs with imaginary parts $\le$ half the carrier width; internal deformations of $S$; single-leg-type components moved in the component their jet does not see) with the sign condition of `lem:appO-no-crossing`(a), $|q_e^+||q_e^-|\ge cq_{Te}^2$, and no denominator of $S$ crossed; (b) the target is compatible with $\mathfrak B$ (`def:appO-target`(d)); (c) on the soft-angle region and for the window-edge terms of `prop:appO-ss-cancel`(iv), (CS1)$_R$ and (CS2)$_R$ hold for the rewritten forms: $\int_{h_1(U_R)}(1-\prod_u\Theta'_u)\big(|\mathfrak r_R(1-T_R)X_R[F_\Gamma]|+\sum_{R'\not\le R}|\mathfrak r_RC_{R'}F_\Gamma|\big)d\mu_\Omega\le C\lambda^aL^K$; (d) for regions with H-emission endpoints **and for coincident SA/AS insertions** (non-analytic ordering weights), a weakly equivalent rewriting exists in which each uncut soft momentum appears in each jet only in denominators of one orientation or only in carriers, with a deformation target such that `prop:appO-eikonal` and `prop:appO-past-wilson` hold. (a) is proved for $\mathcal G_0$ and graded configurations (`prop:appO-target`); **(b)(c)(d) are needed even for abelian $\mathcal G_0$**. `\uses{def:appO-target, def:appO-rewriting, def:appO-graded, prop:appO-ss-cancel}`.
- **[PENDING-VERIFY X1d] 命题 `prop:appO-compatibility-g0` — $\mathcal G_0$ 的相容性** — planned [R, R_PT]. *Planned statement:* for $\mathcal G_0$, with a set algebra adapted to the windows (sets whose boundaries avoid ${\rm supp}\,\psi$ in the moved directions), the target of `prop:appO-target`(i) is compatible (`def:appO-target`(d)), and the adapted algebra still covers the domain and carries the App N subtraction structure. *If PASS:* `asm:appO-deformation`(b) is restricted to configurations outside $\mathcal G_0$. *Fallback:* (b) stays for $\mathcal G_0$.
- **命题 `prop:appO-eikonal` — 变形围道上的程函近似** [R, R_PT]. （$R_{\rm PT}$ …）（本命题只涉及部分子态。）Let $p$ be the $A$-collinear part of an initial-state $J_A$ line ($p^+>0$), $k=\sum_{a\in I}q_a$ the legs injected before it, all in the *Glauber core* ($\psi(q_a)=1$, $|{\rm Re}\,q_a^\pm|\le|q_{Ta}|$), $f_I=\sum_If_a\le\kappa N_Cs$. On the amplitude side of $\Omega$: (a) $|k^-|\ge{\rm Im}\,k^-=f_I\ge\kappa\sum_I|q_{Ta}|\ge\kappa|\mathbf k_T|$, so $|2p^+k^-|\ge2\kappa p^+|\mathbf k_T|$; (b) $\tilde D=p^2-m^2+2p^+k^-+i0$ has $|\tilde D|\ge2p^+f_I$ and $D=(p+k)^2-m^2+i0$ satisfies $\frac{|D-\tilde D|}{|\tilde D|}\le\frac{1}{\kappa p^+}\big(2|p^-|+|\mathbf p_T|+\frac92N_Cs\big)$; (c) with $\varepsilon_3\equiv(|k^+||p^-|+|\mathbf k_T||\mathbf p_T|)/(|k^-|p^+)\le\frac12$, the Grammer–Yennie ratio $G/K$ (`GrammerYennie1973`) is at most twice $r_+\frac{|p^-|}{p^+}+r_T\frac{|\mathbf p_T|}{p^+}+\frac{2|p^-|+|\mathbf p_T|}{\kappa p^+}$, $r_+=|S^+|/|S^-|$, $r_T=|\mathbf S_T|/|S^-|$ ($O(1)$); on the real contour in the Glauber region the last term is $O(1)$ (failure of the soft approximation), on $\Omega$ it is $O(\lambda/\kappa)$; (d) the eikonal denominators $n_A\cdot K+i\sigma0$ satisfy $\sigma{\rm Im}(n_A\cdot K)\ge0$ on $\Omega$; the unique compatible prescription is $+i\sigma0$, the past-pointing Wilson line $W[0,-\infty n_A]$ (VF-WL-1; = `lem:method-i0-compat` with $\eta=-1$). On $U_R$ the bounds are $O(\lambda^a/\kappa)$. Both components must be deformed (moving only $k^+$ leaves $k$ in $G_\delta$). `\uses{def:appO-deformation-map, prop:appO-target, lem:appO-no-crossing, lem:method-i0-compat}`. Proof: full ($|{\rm Re}\,k^\pm|\le f_I/\kappa$, $|k^\pm|\le2f_I/\kappa$, $|\mathbf k_T|\le f_I/\kappa$; $|D-\tilde D|=|2p^-k^+-2\mathbf p_T\cdot\mathbf k_T+2k^+k^--\mathbf k_T^2|$; $K^{\mu\nu}=n_A^\mu k^\nu/n_A\cdot k$). Check: random sampling (R8 K11; maximal ratios 0.77, 0.51, 0.89). Error type: E-P (on the deformed contour); off-core momenta controlled by the second part of (GA4)(iii) as in $e^+e^-$.
- Figure `fig:appO-deformation` (FIGURES_INDEX_v2 N22): three panels — $k^-$ plane ($J_A$: initial poles below, contour raised to ${\rm Im}\,k^-=\kappa|\mathbf k_T|$ over the window, the soft line's own pole outside the window), $k^+$ plane ($J_B$: lowered), real $(k^+,k^-)$ plane with the cross-shaped window bounded by $|k^+k^-|=\delta\mathbf k_T^2$ and the mass shell outside.

### O.8 `sec:appO-wilson` Wilson 线 (2.5 pp)

- **假设 `asm:appO-rewritten-cs` — 改写形式与族求和形式的 Collins 条件** [\tagA]. Full text in `ASSUMPTIONS.md`. Content: (CS1)$_R$ and (CS2)$_R$ of `def:method-collins-conditions` hold for the rewritten forms $\mathfrak r_R(\cdot)$ and for the family-summed forms $\sum_{\mathfrak F}\mathfrak P[\cdot]$ on $h_1^{(R)}(U_R)$, including (i) the term-by-term matching of the localized spectator terms (plus the $(1-T_R)$ errors) with the external-endpoint distributions at $n_A\cdot K_i=0$ that fixes the Wilson-line prescription, and (ii) the allocation of localized SA/AS endpoints between the region $R$ and the $A$-collinear region $R'$ (the $A$-end Glauber recoil is not reproduced by $T_R$; overlap = zero-bin). Glauber specific (absent in DIS and $e^+e^-$); harmless for collinear PDFs (direction irrelevant), substantive for TMD. `\uses{def:method-collins-conditions, def:appO-rewriting, def:appO-family}`.
- **命题 `prop:appO-past-wilson` — Ward 恒等式与指向过去的 Wilson 线** [A]. Under `prop:appO-eikonal`, on $\Omega=\Omega_R$, summing all graphs and all attachments of soft gluons and longitudinal collinear gluons: (a) for the soft-approximated cut-summed integrand (the soft-factorized part of subtraction terms), the sum of all soft attachments to $J_A$ (including final-state spectators and lines emitted by $V_H$) equals, where the eikonal denominators are non-zero, $J_A$ without soft attachments times a Wilson line along $n_A$ ending at $V_H$; with the prescription fixed by the deformation it is the past-pointing $W[0,-\infty n_A]$, eikonal factors $\prod_i\frac{i}{n_A\cdot K_i+i0}$ (conjugate: Hermitian conjugate at $V_H^\dagger$); soft attachments to $J_B$ give $W[0,-\infty n_B]$; $A$-collinear longitudinal gluons attached to $H$ or $J_B$ give the past-pointing line $W[\cdot,-\infty n_B]$ in $A$'s collinear factor (direction irrelevant for collinear PDFs, `prop:method-collinear-wilson`); (b) the soft factor is $S^{(-)}(k_{\rm tot})=\sum_{X_s}\langle0|\bar{\rm T}[\mathcal U^{(-)\dagger}]|X_s\rangle\langle X_s|{\rm T}[\mathcal U^{(-)}]|0\rangle\delta^4(k_{\rm tot}-p_{X_s})$, $\mathcal U^{(-)}=W[0,-\infty n_A]\,W[0,-\infty n_B]$ (colour charges in the representations of the active partons); (c) localized SS endpoints cancel in the family sum (`prop:appO-ss-cancel`); SA endpoints belong, in the $A$-collinear region, to $J_A$ and become part of $A$'s past-pointing line (the $i0$ comes from $J_B$'s initial denominator); the term-by-term agreement on $U_R$ is `asm:appO-rewritten-cs`. `\uses{prop:appO-eikonal, prop:appO-ss-cancel, asm:appO-rewritten-cs, asm:appN-ward-product, lem:method-ward-multi, prop:method-soft-wilson, prop:method-collinear-wilson, prop:prelim-wilson-rules, ex:dy-future-vs-past, lem:appO-no-crossing}`. Proof: sketch — (1) the soft approximation inserts $n_A^\mu n_{B\nu}=n_A^\mu\pi_-(k)_\nu/n_A\cdot k$ (the Grammer–Yennie K term); the Ward identities (`lem:method-ward-multi`, (WI1)) give endpoint terms; external endpoints vanish by LSZ; the $V_H$ endpoint term is the expansion of the Wilson line (`prop:prelim-wilson-rules`); (2) prescription: in a single cut graph the attachment sum equals the **future**-pointing line (`ex:dy-future-vs-past`: $\frac{iQ_a}{k^-+i0}+iQ_s[\frac1{k^-+i0}-\frac1{k^--i0}]=\frac{iQ_a}{k^--i0}$ for $Q_s=-Q_a$); after the rewriting, spectator attachments are localized at $\tau=0$ and attachments on absorbed active lines are initial-only, and on $\Omega$, $\sigma{\rm Im}(n_A\cdot K_i)>0$: moving back to the real contour without crossing poles needs $+i\sigma0$ (`lem:appO-no-crossing`(e)); (3) the Ward identity acts on unrewritten integrands and does not commute with $\mathfrak r_R$; the reconciliation is `asm:appO-rewritten-cs`(i). 「地位与缺口」: [A] because of step (3); harmless for collinear factorization (soft-factor unitarity holds for past, future or mixed directions), substantive for TMD (staple direction; Sivers sign). VF-REF-11 (single cut graph = future-pointing line, step 2); VF-WL-1 (eikonal rules); directions as in §00.5 of this file. Literature: `Collins2002`, `Collins2011` §14.4, `CollinsSoperSterman1988`.
