- **引理 `lem:appO-past-unitarity` — 指向过去的软因子的幺正性** [R, R_def]. （$R_{\rm def}$ …）With $v^0>0$ for $v\in\{n_A,n_B\}$, the path ordering of $W[0,-\infty v]$ is time ordering: ${\rm T}[\mathcal U^{(-)}]={\rm T}\exp\big(-i\int_{-\infty}^0dt\,h(t)\big)$, $h(t)=-g\sum_vv\cdot A^a(tv/v^0)\mathbf T_v^a/v^0$ Hermitian, so ${\rm T}[\mathcal U^{(-)}]=U_I(0,-\infty)$ is unitary as a formal power series and $\int d^4k_{\rm tot}\,S^{(-)}(k_{\rm tot})=\langle0|\bar{\rm T}[\mathcal U^{(-)\dagger}]{\rm T}[\mathcal U^{(-)}]|0\rangle=\mathbb 1_{\rm colour}$ (in DR all non-trivial orders are scaleless). `\uses{def:prelim-wilson}`. Proof: full (as in `lem:method-soft-unitarity`, pointer, with $U_I(\infty,0)\to U_I(0,-\infty)$; if Ch9's lemma already states the past version, this lemma is replaced by a pointer — report). Collinear DY: $Q^2$, $y$ depend on $k_{\rm tot}$ only at relative $O(\lambda^2)$, so the $k_{\rm tot}$ integral is unconstrained.
- **注 `rem:appO-timelike` — 类时与类空的非光锥 Wilson 线** (no tag). The identification of path order with time order needs $v^0>0$ along the line towards $-\infty$; for time-like non-light-like past-pointing lines used as rapidity regulators path order is anti-time order and the analogue of `lem:appO-past-unitarity` fails at finite rapidity (c_tmd §3.4); App O and App N use Collins' space-like lines (`def:appN-tmd-approx`), for which the TMD statements below hold. Active–active Glauber exchange is the Coulomb phase of the soft factor (it makes the DY and SIDIS soft factors differ only by the direction of the lines, `RothsteinStewart2016` §10.1).
- *Target:* **`prop:dy-eikonal-twogluon`** (Ch12, [R, R_PT]) — 两条程函线之间的双胶子交换：$O(g^4)$ 幺正性与 Regge 相位. Setting: two infinite time-like Wilson lines $v_A$, $v_B$ (rapidity difference $Y>0$, colour generators $\mathbf T_A$, $\mathbf T_B$) exchange two gluons of mass $m$ (IR regulator) with exact propagators ($k^+k^-$ kept), at fixed transverse momenta; $a=\mathbf k_T^2+m^2$, $b=(\mathbf K-\mathbf k)_T^2+m^2$. (a) After the $\delta(v_A\cdot K)\delta(v_B\cdot K)$ of the infinite lines, with $x_A=v_A\cdot k$, $x_B=v_B\cdot k$, measure $dk^0dk^3=dx_Adx_B/\sinh Y$, $Q\equiv k_\parallel^2=(-(x_A^2+x_B^2)+2\cosh Y\,x_Ax_B)/\sinh^2Y$, $g=1/((Q-a+i0)(Q-b+i0))$: $J_{\rm box}=\int dx_Adx_B\frac{i}{x_A+i0}\frac{i}{-x_B+i0}g$, $J_{\rm cross}=\int dx_Adx_B\frac{i}{x_A+i0}\frac{i}{x_B+i0}g$ equal $J_{\rm box}=\frac{2\pi}{ab}(\pi+iY)$, $J_{\rm cross}=-\frac{2\pi iY}{ab}$; with $c_{\rm box}=X^2$, $c_{\rm cross}=X^2+\frac{C_A}{2}X$, $J_{\rm box}c_{\rm box}+J_{\rm cross}c_{\rm cross}=\frac{2\pi}{ab}[\pi X^2-iY\frac{C_A}{2}X]$; (b) after the transverse integrals the first term is the exponentiation term $\frac12(-i\chi)^2X^2$, the second is $i\times$real$\times$Hermitian — the one-loop reggeization phase $\propto C_AY$; (c) in the parametrization of `lem:appO-glauber-operator`, $p_{\rm opp}=-iY/\pi$, ${\rm Re}\,p_{\rm opp}=0$, so the strict-window residual vanishes and the two-gluon exchange graphs give $\mathcal V^\dagger\mathcal V=1+O(\chi^3)$ with no real emission (for straight infinite lines real cuts start at $O(g^6)$); not shown: the other $O(g^4)$ graphs (slot X1b), non-eikonal spectators, all orders (slot X1a). *Proof:* full — $x_B$ integral by residues: for $J_{\rm cross}$ close in the upper half-plane (poles $x_+^{a}$, $x_+^{b}$), ${\rm inner}_{\rm cross}(x_A)=-\frac{i\pi\sinh Y}{a-b}\big[\frac{1}{R_ax_+^a}-\frac{1}{R_bx_+^b}\big]$; for $J_{\rm box}$ close in the lower half-plane (poles $x_-^a$, $x_-^b$), ${\rm inner}_{\rm box}=\frac{i\pi\sinh Y}{a-b}\big[\frac{1}{R_ax_-^a}-\frac{1}{R_bx_-^b}\big]$, with $R_c=\sqrt{x_A^2-c+i0}$ (${\rm Im}\,R_c\ge0$), $x_\pm^c=\cosh Y\,x_A\pm\sinh Y\,R_c$; then $J=-\int dx_A\frac{{\rm inner}(x_A)}{x_A+i0}=-\big[{\rm PV}\!\int\frac{{\rm inner}}{x_A}-i\pi\,{\rm inner}(0)\big]$, evaluated in closed form. Checks: `refdoc/work_R3/scripts/eikonal_box.py`, `eikonal_box_check.py` (residue formulas vs brute-force $i\varepsilon$ quadrature), `eikonal_box_closedform.py` (closed form to $<5\times10^{-16}$ at 10 points). VF-REF-9, VF-REF-10. Marker: 「本书新结果（待文献核查）」 (verify claim N01; ingredients classical: `Cheng:1969eh`, `Korchemskaya:1994qp`, `Korchemsky:1993hr`, `Caron-Huot:2013fea`; the marker is printed only after the literature check of WRITER_SPEC_v2 §6.5). Figure `fig:dy-two-gluon-exchange` (owner Ch12).
- **[PENDING-VERIFY X1a] 命题 `prop:appO-eikonal-allorders` — 程函旁观者：所有阶的幺正性** — planned [R, R_def]. *Planned statement:* for two straight infinite Wilson lines (eikonal spectators), the complete set of exchange graphs at every order gives $\mathcal V^\dagger\mathcal V=1$ as formal power series, from the unitarity of ${\rm T}\exp\{ig\int dt[v_A\cdot A(v_At)\mathbf T_A+v_B\cdot A(v_Bt+\mathbf b)\mathbf T_B]\}$ (same formal level as `lem:appO-past-unitarity`). *If PASS:* `rem:dy-common-ordering`(ii) points to it; `asm:appO-mixing`'s 「地位」 records it. *Fallback:* `rem:dy-common-ordering`(ii) stays a remark.
- **[PENDING-VERIFY X1b] 命题 `prop:appO-g4-complete` — $O(g^4)$ 的完整检验** — planned [R, R_PT] (that order; first sentence states it). *Planned statement:* all $O(g^4)$ graphs between two eikonal lines (box, crossed box, one-loop corrections of the single exchange, three-gluon graphs) contribute to $\mathcal V$ only Hermitian phases beyond the exponentiation term; unitarity is exact at $O(g^4)$. *If PASS:* the "not shown" clause of `prop:dy-eikonal-twogluon`(c) is shortened. *Fallback:* "argued, not computed".

### O.9 `sec:appO-main` 主定理 (2 pp)

- **定理 `thm:appO-main` — 色单态单举 DY 中 Glauber 区相消的条件定理** [R, R_cond]. （$R_{\rm cond}$ …）前提：\cref{asm:appO-lcopt}、\cref{asm:appO-envelope}、\cref{asm:appO-mixing}、\cref{asm:appO-deformation}、\cref{asm:appO-rewritten-cs}、\cref{asm:appN-envelope}、\cref{asm:appN-separation}、\cref{asm:appN-ward-product}（及 \cref{prop:appO-past-wilson}、\cref{prop:method-collinear-wilson}）。（本定理只涉及部分子态。）Setting: colour-singlet inclusive DY ($A$, $B$ colour-singlet partonic models; massless active partons), any order of non-abelian perturbation theory, summed over all cuts $\mathcal C(\Gamma)$ and all graphs of the order; $R$ a leading region, $U_R$ its dominated set on the LCOPT domain, $K_R$ the soft-angle region. Then: (i) (*rewrite*) $F_\Gamma\doteq_R\tilde F_{\Gamma,R}$, i.e. $\int_V(T_cF_\Gamma-\mathfrak r_RT_cF_\Gamma)=O(\lambda^aL^K)$ for all $V\in\mathfrak B$, $V\subset U_R$, and all chain terms (E-W); (ii) (*one-sidedness*) on $U_R\setminus K_R$, under standard flow, each uncut soft $k_j$ through $J_A$ ($J_B$) has $k_j^-$ ($k_j^+$) only in initial denominators or only in an entire carrier, never in a cut $\delta$; (iii) (*deformation and factorized structure*) a deformation target $\Omega_R$ exists (`prop:appO-target` for $\mathcal G_0$ and graded configurations, `asm:appO-deformation`(a) otherwise); on it the soft approximation for Glauber-core couplings to active lines holds with relative error $O(\lambda^a)$ (E-P); after summing graphs and attachments the approximated couplings become past-pointing Wilson lines — soft attachments to $J_A$ ($J_B$) give $W[0,-\infty n_A]$ ($W[0,-\infty n_B]$) in the soft factor, $A$-collinear ($B$-collinear) longitudinal gluons give $W[\cdot,-\infty n_B]$ ($W[\cdot,-\infty n_A]$) in the collinear factor of $A$ ($B$); the soft factor equals 1 and the collinear factors are the DIS PDFs; (iv) (*spectator exchange*) summed over a spectator-exchange family, the in-window SS exchanges of non-coincident connections cancel (all connections in the abelian theory); coincident connections (non-abelian) cancel only in the complete graph set (`asm:appO-mixing`(c)); (v) the subtraction terms have the same structure (all statements hold for every $\mathfrak r_RT_cF_\Gamma$). Regions with H-emission endpoints: the corresponding parts of (i)–(iii) are replaced by `asm:appO-deformation`(d). `\uses{asm:appO-lcopt, asm:appO-envelope, asm:appO-mixing, asm:appO-deformation, asm:appO-rewritten-cs, asm:appN-envelope, asm:appN-separation, asm:appN-ward-product, prop:appO-past-wilson, lem:appO-hard-locality, lem:dy-poles-orientation, lem:appO-unitarity, prop:appO-one-sided, prop:appO-ss-cancel, prop:appO-target, prop:appO-eikonal, lem:appO-past-unitarity, prop:method-collinear-wilson}`. Proof: full, eight steps (O-1)–(O-8) as in §O.1 with their error types; special cases: $\mathcal G_0$ without H-emission needs `asm:appO-mixing` only for $\ge2$ non-commuting localized insertions on one spectator parton; (O-5) needs no `asm:appO-deformation`(a) for $\mathcal G_0$/graded; (O-8) always needs (b)(c). Content grade [A]. **Remark to print:** `asm:appO-deformation`(b)(c)(d) are needed **even for abelian graphs** — no part of the DY Glauber cancellation is unconditional at the cross-section level. It supplies (CU1) of `asm:appN-cutsum` for these graphs (via `lem:appO-lcopt`, `lem:appO-hard-locality`).
- **推论 `cor:appO-interface` — 与附录 N 的接口** [R, R_cond]. （$R_{\rm cond}$ …）前提：\cref{asm:appO-lcopt}、\cref{asm:appO-envelope}、\cref{asm:appO-mixing}、\cref{asm:appO-deformation}、\cref{asm:appO-rewritten-cs}、\cref{asm:appN-envelope}、\cref{asm:appN-separation}、\cref{asm:appN-ward-product}（及 \cref{prop:appO-past-wilson}）。（本推论只涉及部分子态。）With $F=F_\Gamma$, $\tilde F_{R_0}=\mathfrak r_{R_0}F$, $\Omega_{R_0}$, $h^{(R_0)}$ from `thm:appO-main`: **(Gla-a)** $F\doteq_{R_0}\tilde F_{R_0}$ for every $R_0$ (same $\mathfrak B$, all $\Phi$, uniform error); **(Gla-b)** on $U_{R_0}\setminus K_{R_0}$, in $\tilde F_{R_0}$ and each chain term, uncut soft momenta through $J_A$ ($J_B$) occur only with initial orientation or only in carriers, so `lem:method-i0-compat` applies with $\eta=-1$ to each jet denominator; **(Gla-c)** (GA1)(a) holds for $h^{(R_0)}$ and $\tilde F_{R_0}$ with its subtraction terms, and the first part of (GA4)(iii) on $h_1^{(R_0)}(U_{R_0}\setminus K_{R_0})$ for soft momenta in denominators; (d) (*transfer*) if for every $\Phi\in\{1\}\cup\{C_R\}$, $\int\Phi F$ converges absolutely on real space for each $\eta_0>0$ with a limit as $\eta_0\to0^+$, and (CS1)$_{R_0}$, (CS2)$_{R_0}$ hold for $\tilde F_{R_0}$ on $h_1^{(R_0)}(U_{R_0})$ (`asm:appO-rewritten-cs`, with `asm:appO-deformation`(b)(c)), then $|\int F-\sum_R\int C_RF|\le C\lambda^aL^K$; (e) (*block-wise subtraction terms*) for every $R$, $V\subset U_R$ in $\mathfrak B$, and $R'$: $\int_VC_{R'}F=\int_V\mathfrak r_RC_{R'}F+O(\lambda^aL^K)$ — on each $R$-dominated block the subtraction terms may be computed in rewritten form, with the Wilson-line prescription fixed by $\Omega_R$ (past pointing). `\uses{thm:appO-main, asm:appO-lcopt, asm:appO-envelope, asm:appO-mixing, asm:appO-deformation, asm:appO-rewritten-cs, asm:appN-envelope, asm:appN-separation, asm:appN-ward-product, prop:appO-past-wilson, lem:method-i0-compat, lem:method-covering, def:appO-target}`. Proof: full (elementary; (d) disjoint blocks $V_{R^{(m)}}=U_{R^{(m)}}\setminus\bigcup_{m'<m}U_{R^{(m')}}$ cover the space, `lem:method-covering`; on each block replace $F$ by $\tilde F_{R_0}$, move to $h_1(V_{R_0})$ by compatibility, bound pointwise by (CS1)(CS2)). Note: (d) concerns the **original** $F$ (subtraction terms built from unrewritten $F$, evaluated block-wise by (e)); for collinear PDFs the direction is irrelevant, so (e) matters only for TMD. Used by `prop:appN-dy`.
- **命题 `prop:appO-tmd` — TMD 版本** [R, R_cond]. （$R_{\rm cond}$ …）前提：\cref{asm:appO-lcopt}、\cref{asm:appO-envelope}、\cref{asm:appO-mixing}、\cref{asm:appO-deformation}、\cref{asm:appO-rewritten-cs}、\cref{asm:appN-envelope}、\cref{asm:appN-separation}、\cref{asm:appN-ward-product}（及 \cref{prop:appO-past-wilson}）。（本命题只涉及部分子态。）Measurement $\delta^{(2)}(\mathbf q_T-\mathbf q_{T,\ell\ell})$, TMD modes and approximators (`def:appN-tmd-approx`), $\lambda=q_T/Q$. Then (i), (ii), (iv), (v) of `thm:appO-main` hold (soft-scale errors relative $O(\lambda)$, `lem:appO-glauber-pc`(e)), and (iii) is replaced by: (iii′) a deformation target moving no transverse component exists, on which the active-line soft approximation holds with relative error $O(\lambda^a)$; after summing graphs and attachments the Ward identities give the unsubtracted TMD correlators $\tilde f^{\rm unsub}_A(x_A,\mathbf b_T)$, $\tilde f^{\rm unsub}_B(x_B,\mathbf b_T)$ (`def:tmd-unsub`) with **past-pointing staple** Wilson lines in $b$ space and a soft factor $\tilde S(\mathbf b_T)$ of past-pointing lines (`def:tmd-soft`), rapidity-regularized as in `def:appN-tmd-approx`. The key identity: in $b$ space the measurement enters only through the momentum through $V_H$, $V_H^\dagger$; $\sum_X|X\rangle e^{i\mathbf b_T\cdot\mathbf P_{XT}}\langle X|=e^{i\mathbf b_T\cdot\hat{\mathbf P}_T}$, and $[\hat P^-,\hat{\mathbf P}_T]=0$ gives $U(\infty,0)^\dagger e^{i\mathbf b_T\cdot\hat{\mathbf P}_T}U(\infty,0)=e^{i\mathbf b_T\cdot\hat{\mathbf P}_T}$; the localized SS insertion is translation invariant and commutes with $\hat{\mathbf P}_T$, so `lem:appO-unitarity` and `prop:appO-ss-cancel` survive. `\uses{thm:appO-main, asm:appO-lcopt, asm:appO-envelope, asm:appO-mixing, asm:appO-deformation, asm:appO-rewritten-cs, asm:appN-envelope, asm:appN-separation, asm:appN-ward-product, prop:appO-past-wilson, def:appN-tmd-approx, def:tmd-unsub, def:tmd-soft, lem:appO-unitarity, prop:appO-ss-cancel}`. Proof: full given `thm:appO-main` (the deformation has $\mathbf e_{G,T}=0$; the Glauber scale is still $(\lambda^2,\lambda^2,\lambda)$; transverse links do not contribute in covariant Feynman-gauge graphs). Precise form of `lem:tmd-glauber` (Ch14); consistent with `Collins2002` (past-pointing for DY). Input of `prop:appN-tmd-window`.
- **注 `rem:appO-measurement` — 测量相容性的统一判据** (no tag). The pairing of §O.4 and the family cancellation of §O.5 use only that the measurement operator commutes with $U(\infty,0)$ and with the $\tau=0$ localized insertions — the substance of "compatible with the measurement"; $\Pi=1$ and $\Pi=e^{i\mathbf b\cdot\hat{\mathbf P}_T}$ commute, $\Pi_{h_1}$ and $\Pi_{\rm gap}$ do not (pointer `prop:break-measurement`, Ch18, which uses `lem:appO-unitarity`).

### O.10 `sec:appO-checklist` 严格性清单 (1.5 pp)

- **表 `tab:appO-checklist` — 附录 O 的严格性清单** (owner App O). Columns: 步骤 (O-1…O-8) | 陈述 (label) | 地位（完整证明／标度陈述／在假设下） | 误差类型 | 所用假设 | Glauber 特有（是／否） | 文献. Rows: regions and power counting (`lem:appO-glauber-pc`: E-S/E-P; integral form `asm:appN-envelope` — no); LCOPT (`lem:appO-lcopt`, `lem:appO-numerators`: complete; `asm:appO-lcopt`: assumption — no); hard locality and orientation (`lem:appO-hard-locality`, `lem:dy-poles-orientation`: complete under `asm:appO-lcopt` — no); cut sum (`lem:appO-cut-position`, `lem:appO-unitarity`: complete; Hermiticity proved in App J — no); equal-time localization (`lem:appO-equal-time`, `lem:appO-insertion-values`: complete — yes); joint localization (`lem:appO-joint-localization`: complete, (b) new — yes); SS localization and family cancellation (`prop:appO-ss-localize`, `prop:appO-ss-cancel`: complete on window cores; window-edge terms open — yes); Glauber operator (`lem:appO-glauber-operator`, `prop:dy-eikonal-twogluon`: complete — yes); one-sided rewriting (`prop:appO-one-sided`: under `asm:appO-envelope` (yes), `asm:appO-mixing`(a)(b) for general $S$ (yes)); deformation (`lem:appO-no-crossing`, `prop:appO-target`: complete for $\mathcal G_0$/graded; `asm:appO-deformation`(a)–(d) — yes); eikonal (`prop:appO-eikonal`: complete, E-P — yes); Wilson lines (`prop:appO-past-wilson`: [A], `asm:appO-rewritten-cs` — yes); soft factor (`lem:appO-past-unitarity`: complete — no); assembly (`thm:appO-main`, `cor:appO-interface`, `prop:appO-tmd`: complete under the listed assumptions; E-W). **Corrected bookkeeping (ERR-M29):** the Glauber-specific unproven inputs are `asm:appO-mixing`(c), `asm:appO-deformation`(a)–(d), `asm:appO-mixing`(a)(b) for general soft subgraphs, `asm:appO-envelope` **and** `asm:appO-rewritten-cs`; the App N inputs `asm:appN-envelope`, `asm:appN-separation`, `asm:appN-ward-product` are shared with DIS and $e^+e^-$.

### O.11 `sec:appO-neighbours` 相邻问题 (1 pp; cut first if pages are short)

- **注 `rem:appO-hh` — $pp\to h_1h_2X$ 在 (O-3) 失效** (no tag). Observed jets are not merged into $H$; pairing needs completeness ($U^\dagger\Pi_{h_1}U\ne\Pi_{h_1}$); the equal-time rewriting needs $k^-$-independent external factors — the relevant pinch region is the Glauber region **relative to the pair of directions** $(n_A,w_1)$ ($k\cdot n_A\sim k\cdot w_1\sim\lambda^2$, $\mathbf k_T$ nearly orthogonal to $\mathbf w_{1T}$), where $J_1$ sees $w_1\cdot k\sim\lambda^2$ and depends on $k^-$ at the jet scale (ERR-M31); the Glauber colour operator with opposite signs for initial and final partons is $\mathbf T_a+2\mathbf T_b$ in all-outgoing convention for the configuration of `prop:break-colour` (VF-REF-16; ERR-S2; pointer `CollinsQiu2007`, `RogersMulders2010`, Ch18).
- **注 `rem:appO-gaps` — 快度间隙与衍射** (no tag). A gap projector commutes neither with the evolution nor with the $\tau=0$ insertions: $\langle\cdots\mathcal W'^\dagger\Pi_{\rm gap}\mathcal W\cdots\rangle\ne\langle\cdots\Pi_{\rm gap}\cdots\rangle$ (gap survival); diffractive DIS is safe (one hadron; (O-3) not needed). Two-Glauber factorization violation for single-scale observables: contested — `Gaunt2014` vs `Schwartz:2018obd` (pure Glauber ladders cancel; the leading violation needs a soft gluon via the Lipatov vertex, i.e. Glauber–soft mixing); explicit counterexample for MPI-sensitive observables `Zeng:2015iba`. Pointer `prop:break-hh-diffraction` (Ch18).
- **注 `rem:appO-dft` — Doria–Frenkel–Taylor** (no tag). Outside the premises (coloured massive incoming partons; no uniform-in-$\varepsilon$ envelope); heuristically located in the dropped $\frac{m^2}{2(p_A^+)^2}k^+$ term of the incoming denominator, which couples the two jets' orderings (VF-REF-8); power suppressed; pointer `rem:dy-dft-premises` (Ch12) with `DoriaFrenkelTaylor1980`, `DiLietoEtAl1981`, `Frenkel:1983di`, `Catani:1985xt`, `Andrasi:1980qw`, and `Basu:1984ba` (factorization of power corrections fails at $m^4/s^2$).
- **注 `rem:appO-double-dy` — 双 Drell–Yan** (no tag). (O-2) generalizes (both hard vertices at $\tau=0$; DPD fields at $x^+=0$); (O-3) seems to generalize but is not written; new inputs (colour-correlated DPDs; $1\to2$ splitting) lie in (O-7) and in region overlaps (`Diehl:2015bca`, `Diehl:2017kgu`); pointer `thm:break-double-dy` (Ch18).
- **注 `rem:appO-rs-dictionary` — 与 Glauber SCET 的对照** (no tag). Instantaneous potential ↔ equal-time insertion ($V_G$, $\chi$); Glauber collapse ↔ contact term $c[O(0),V_H(0)]$; causality cancellation ↔ largest-time pairing + family sum; soft–Glauber overlap ↔ App N zero-bin; Glauber–soft mixing ↔ `asm:appO-mixing`; the RS regulator $|2k^z|^{-\eta}$ realizes the common ordering ($p_{\rm opp}=0$) (`RothsteinStewart2016`); pointer `rem:scet-glauber-dictionary` (Ch15).

## Appendix P — 形式化蓝图：假设登记表、依赖图与状态表 / The formal blueprint: assumption register, dependency graphs and status table

`\label{app:appP}` · `appP_blueprint.tex` · **10 pp** (≈2 pp written + ≈8 pp generated) · introduction by a writer (last), everything else generated by the integrator

**Purpose.** Make the logical structure of the book mechanically checkable in the style of a Lean blueprint (every statement is a node with a label, hypotheses, a grade and a `\uses` list), without claiming formal verification: (i) the register of all numbered assumptions with their owners, tags, statuses and **computed** users; (ii) a table of **all** canonical statements with tag, qualifier, computed content grade, direct premises (as printed numbers) and location; (iii) for the main theorems the transitive dependency closure and the bottleneck assumptions; (iv) per-part dependency graphs of the main theorems in TikZ; (v) the results of the consistency checks — in particular that no statement depends on a later statement or on a lower-graded one without its own grade being lowered accordingly.

**Depends on.** Every unit (the generator reads all unit files); Ch1 `def:intro-tags`, `def:intro-error-types`, `def:intro-MA`, `rem:intro-grade-dictionary`, `tab:intro-assumptions`; Ch25 `tab:summary-master`; `spec_v2/ASSUMPTIONS.md` (register data); FINAL_OUTLINE_v2 registry (owners, tags).

**Sections (10 pp).**

| § | label · title | content | pp |
|---|---|---|---|
| P.1 | `sec:appP-intro` 导言 | written: what the blueprint is; tags, qualifiers, assumptions; grade propagation; error types; how to read; what the checks do not guarantee; `def:appP-content-grade`, `rem:appP-limits` | 2 |
| P.2 | `sec:appP-register` 假设登记表 | generated `tab:appP-register` | 1.5 |
| P.3 | `sec:appP-status` 全部陈述的状态表 | generated `tab:appP-status` (longtable) | 3 |
| P.4 | `sec:appP-uses` 主要定理的依赖闭包与瓶颈 | generated `tab:appP-uses` | 0.5 |
| P.5 | `sec:appP-graphs` 依赖图 | generated `fig:appP-dependency` (skeleton) and `fig:appP-dependency-b` … `-h` (per part) | 2.5 |
| P.6 | `sec:appP-checks` 一致性检查 | generated `tab:appP-checks` | 0.5 |

### P.1 `sec:appP-intro` 导言 (2 pp, written)

Content (in this order; Chinese; no reference to the reference document):
1. **What the blueprint is.** Every definition, assumption, lemma, proposition, theorem, corollary and conjecture of the book carries a label, a type, a tag (or none for definitions and pure mathematics), a qualifier for [R], a premise sentence for $R_{\rm cond}$, and a `\uses` list; together they form a directed graph. The tables and figures of this appendix are generated from the LaTeX sources by a program (named and versioned in the caption of `tab:appP-checks`: generator version, date and source revision), not written by hand. Analogy (one sentence): the blueprints that accompany Lean formalization projects; the difference: our nodes are proved on paper, at the grade stated, not in a proof assistant.
2. **The tag system on one page.** A compact table: five tags [R] [A] [P] [C] [V] with their meanings (`def:intro-tags`); four qualifiers $R_{\rm PT}$, $R_{\rm def}$, $R_{\rm OPE}$, $R_{\rm cond}$; assumptions (`assumption` environments with `asm:` labels; tag of an assumption = how the literature covers the assumed claim; global non-perturbative `asm:intro-*` vs technical assumptions).
3. **Grade propagation** (print WRITER_SPEC_v2 §4.6 items 1–3 in Chinese) and the formal definition:
   - **定义 `def:appP-content-grade` — 内容等级** (no tag). Order $[R]>[A]>[P]>[C]$; $[V]$ outside the order. For a statement $S$: $c(S)=$ its tag if $S$ is [A], [P], [C] or [V]; $c(S)=[R]$ if $S$ is $R_{\rm PT}$ or $R_{\rm def}$, or untagged (definition, pure mathematics); $c(S)=[A]$ if $S$ is $R_{\rm OPE}$; $c(S)=\min_{p\in\Pi(S)}c(p)$ if $S$ is $R_{\rm cond}$, where $\Pi(S)$ is the set of labels in its premise sentence; for an assumption $c(a)=$ its tag (no tag: $c(a)=[R]$, a convention); global non-perturbative assumptions are excluded from the minimum for ordinary statements. A statement $T$ that is not $R_{\rm cond}$ is *consistent* if ${\rm tag}(T)\le c(p)$ for every premise $p\in\texttt{uses}(T)$ other than definitions, pure mathematics, global assumptions and, for $R_{\rm OPE}$ statements, the OPE premises `thm:dis-ope`, `prop:dis-ope-euclid`, `prop:dis-ope-gi`, `prop:dis-ope-forward`. `\uses{def:intro-tags}`.
4. **Error types** E-S, E-P, E-F, E-N, E-W (`def:intro-error-types`; one line each).
5. **How to read** `tab:appP-register`, `tab:appP-status`, `tab:appP-uses` (column by column) and the graphs (legend of FIGURES_INDEX_v2 §4.9 rule 5).
6. **注 `rem:appP-limits` — 蓝图检查保证什么、不保证什么** (no tag). The checks guarantee the consistency of the bookkeeping: every dependency is declared, defined, earlier (or in an appendix), acyclic, and no conclusion is printed with a grade above the weakest of its declared premises. They do not check that a proof is correct, that a `\uses` list is complete (only `\cref`'d labels inside a statement are compared, WRITER_SPEC_v2 §4.5 item 2), or that an assumption is true; the content grade of a process theorem is only as reliable as the tags of the assumptions in `tab:appP-register`. Pending items (`[PENDING-VERIFY]`) are listed in `tab:appP-checks` until the verification patch removes them.

### P.2–P.6 Generated material

- **表 `tab:appP-register` — 编号假设登记表（由 `assumption` 环境生成）** (longtable). Columns: 标签 | 所属单元 | 内容（一行） | 标签（\tagA/\tagP/\tagC/无） | 地位 | 被使用于（由 `\uses` 计算） | 解除途径. Rows in book order of the owners (Ch1, Ch22, Ch23, Ch24, App M, App N, App O). The one-line content and the upgrade path come from `ASSUMPTIONS.md` §1; the users column is computed and compared with the 「被使用于」 field (check C-P10).
- **表 `tab:appP-status` — 全部规范陈述的状态表** (longtable). Columns: 编号（印刷编号，超链接） | 标签 | 类型 | 标签与限定 | 内容等级 $c(S)$ | 前提（直接前提的印刷编号；定义不列；假设以其编号列出） | 误差类型（`% errtype:` 注释，否则 —） | 页. One row per canonical statement in book order (definitions, remarks and examples included at compact level 0, see budget control).
- **表 `tab:appP-uses` — 主要定理的依赖闭包与瓶颈** . One row per master-table statement (`tab:summary-master`, Ch25) and per $R_{\rm cond}$ theorem of App M/N/O: 标签 | 标签与限定 | 内容等级 | 闭包中的技术性假设 | 闭包中的全局假设（MA…） | 瓶颈（计算） | 瓶颈（总表） | 一致?
- **图 `fig:appP-dependency` — 主要陈述的依赖图（骨架）** and **`fig:appP-dependency-b` … `fig:appP-dependency-h`** — per part: b Part I (Ch1–Ch5), c Part II (Ch6–Ch8), d Part III (Ch9–Ch13), e Part IV (Ch14–Ch18), f Parts V–VI (Ch19–Ch21), g Part VII (Ch22–Ch24), h App M–O. Caption draft (FIGURES_INDEX_v2 §4.9 rule 6), per-part captions add the part.
- **表 `tab:appP-checks` — 一致性检查的结果** . One row per check C-P1…C-P16: check | result (PASS / number of findings) | first three findings; caption gives generator version, date, source revision.

### P.7 Generator specification (for the integrator; not printed)

**Program.** `spec_v2/checks/blueprint_gen.py` (to be written by the integrator), Python 3 standard library only, run as `python3 -I spec_v2/checks/blueprint_gen.py --root <book dir> --master master.tex --aux master.aux --outline spec_v2/FINAL_OUTLINE_v2.md --register spec_v2/ASSUMPTIONS.md --out build/appP --level <0..3> [--final]`. Deterministic output (sorted keys, fixed float formatting); exit code 0 if no check of severity E fails, 1 otherwise; `--final` turns C-P13 into an error.

**Inputs and parsing.**
1. *Unit order:* the `\include`/`\input` sequence of `master.tex` (front, ch01…ch25, appA…appQ); file → unit key by the registry headings of FINAL_OUTLINE_v2 (`### ChNN`, `### AppX`, `### front`) and WRITER_SPEC_v2 §1.1.
2. *Comments:* strip `%`-comments, except marker lines kept verbatim: `% uses: none`, `% errtype: E-S|E-P|E-F|E-N|E-W`, `% PENDING-VERIFY: <label> <claim/X-item>`.
3. *Environments:* `\begin{definition|assumption|theorem|lemma|proposition|corollary|conjecture|example|remark}` with an optional title `[...]` parsed with **balanced** brackets and braces (titles contain `$…$`, `\cref{…}`, nested brackets); the first `\label{…}` inside the environment (warning if not within the first three lines); environment type and printed type from the environment name.
4. *Tag:* exactly one of `\tagR \tagA \tagP \tagC \tagV` in the title, or none (definitions, pure mathematics, untagged remarks/examples, convention assumptions); more than one is an error.
5. *Qualifier* (for `\tagR`): the first sentence of the body compared with the four verbatim strings of WRITER_SPEC_v2 §4.3 after normalizing `–`→`--` and whitespace; the short $R_{\rm OPE}$ form is accepted after the first long form in the same unit.
6. *Premise sentence* ($R_{\rm cond}$): the sentence beginning with 「前提：」 that follows the qualifier; every label inside `\cref{…}`/`\Cref{…}` (comma lists, including the parenthetical 「（及 …）」) is collected; inner item suffixes such as `(T2)` after a `\cref` are ignored for the graph and kept as text.
7. *`\uses{…}`:* exactly one per environment of types def/asm/lem/prop/thm/cor/conj (zero allowed only with `% uses: none`); for tagged remarks and for examples: at most one. Comma-separated labels; whitespace trimmed; only the prefixes `def thm lem prop cor conj rem ex asm` are allowed (others → error C-P1).
8. *Lines:* 「非微扰地位：」 with its `\cref` labels; the partonic line 「（本…只涉及部分子态。）」 (flag).
9. *Status paragraph:* after `\end{…}` and an immediately following `proof`/`proofsketch`, the next paragraph starting with `\paragraph{地位与缺口}` or 「\textbf{地位与缺口：}」; the labels it `\cref`s are recorded.
10. *Location:* the last `\section`/`\subsection` label before the environment; source file and line.
11. *Printed numbers and pages* from `master.aux`: `\newlabel{<label>}{{<number>}{<page>}{<title>}{<anchor>}{}}` (hyperref form; the `@cref` duplicates are ignored).
12. *Registry and register:* owners and tags from the FINAL_OUTLINE_v2 registry (same parser as `check_unit_v2.py`: headings `### ChNN`/`### AppX`/`### front`/`### Assumptions`, rows whose first cell is a backticked label); the assumption data (owner, tag, one-line content, status, users, upgrade path) from the summary table of `ASSUMPTIONS.md` §1.
13. *Master table:* rows of `tab:summary-master` in `ch25_summary.tex` (label column, tag column, 瓶颈 column).

**Data model** (`blueprint.json`). Node: `{label, type, unit, unit_index, file, line, section, title, tag, qualifier, premises, uses, np_line, partonic, status_paragraph, status_labels, errtype, pending, number, page}`. Edges: $p\to S$ for $p\in$ `uses`(S) (premise-sentence labels are a subset by check C-P8). Derived: content grade $c(S)$ (`def:appP-content-grade`, computed in topological order), technical-assumption closure $A(S)$ and global-assumption closure $G(S)$ (through `uses`, and for an $R_{\rm cond}$ premise through its premise sentence), bottleneck $B(S)=\{a\in A(S): c(a)=\min_{A(S)}c\}$ (if the minimum is attained by a non-assumption statement without technical assumptions, that statement), direct users `used_by`.

**Checks** (severity E = error, W = warning, I = information).

| ID | check | severity |
|---|---|---|
| C-P1 | every label in a `\uses` list, a premise sentence or a 非微扰地位 line is defined in some unit (reserved slot labels are reported by C-P13 instead) | E |
| C-P2 | every canonical label is defined exactly in its registry owner; every registry label exists in the sources | E |
| C-P3 | **no forward dependency**: for $p\in$ `uses`(S): if $S$, $p$ are in chapters, ${\rm unit}(p)\le{\rm unit}(S)$; if in the same unit, $p$ is defined before $S$; chapter → appendix edges are allowed (counted, I); appendix → anything allowed | E |
| C-P4 | no dependency cycle (Tarjan strongly connected components; self-loops included) | E |
| C-P5 | **no dependency on a lower-graded statement without a grade drop**: every statement $T$ that is not $R_{\rm cond}$ satisfies ${\rm tag}(T)\le c(p)$ for all its premises (exceptions of `def:appP-content-grade`) | E |
| C-P6 | no positive statement uses a [V] statement (only remarks and examples may) | E |
| C-P7 | qualifier compatibility: $R_{\rm def}$ uses only $R_{\rm def}$/untagged premises; $R_{\rm PT}$ uses $R_{\rm def}$, $R_{\rm PT}$, untagged; $R_{\rm OPE}$ additionally $R_{\rm OPE}$ and the OPE premises; untagged (convention) assumptions are allowed everywhere and listed (I) | E |
| C-P8 | $R_{\rm cond}$: the premise sentence is among the first two sentences; every assumption in `uses` and every premise with $c(p)<[R]$ appears in it; every label of it appears in `uses` | E |
| C-P9 | every `asm:` label is used by at least one statement | E |
| C-P10 | the 「被使用于」 field of every assumption (tex and `ASSUMPTIONS.md`) equals the computed direct users | W |
| C-P11 | every master-table row names an existing label and prints its tag | E |
| C-P12 | no duplicate labels; no label defined in two units | E |
| C-P13 | no `% PENDING-VERIFY` comment and no reserved slot label of `OUTLINE_appMNOPQ.md` §00.4 remains | W (E with `--final`) |
| C-P14 | the 瓶颈 column of the master table contains the computed bottleneck $B(S)$ (missing and extra labels listed) | W |
| C-P15 | hadron-level statements carry the 非微扰地位 line; its labels are global assumptions and include $G(S)$; non-perturbative $R_{\rm cond}$ statements list their global assumptions in the premise sentence | E (W where the hadron/parton classification is heuristic) |
| C-P16 | every [A], [P], [C], [V] statement has a 「地位与缺口」 paragraph that names at least one label of $B(S)$ | E (W if it names none of $B(S)$) |

**Outputs** (all in `build/appP/`): `appP_register.tex`, `appP_status.tex`, `appP_uses.tex`, `appP_graph_a.tex` … `appP_graph_h.tex`, `appP_checks.tex`, `blueprint.json`, `appP_report.txt` (complete finding lists, per unit, for return to the writers), and in `--stub` mode placeholder files that only define the labels (first LaTeX pass).

**Build sequence.** (1) `blueprint_gen.py --stub`; compile the book (labels resolve). (2) Run the generator with the `.aux`; compile twice. (3) Read the pages of `\label{app:appP}` and `\label{app:appQ}` from the `.aux`; if App P exceeds 10 pp, rerun with the next compact level. The `longtable` package must be loaded by the integrator in the common preamble (the preamble files are not changed by unit writers); no other package is needed (TikZ, xcolor with `dvipsnames`, hyperref, cleveref are already loaded).

**Typesetting of the longtables.** `\footnotesize` (level ≥ 1: `\scriptsize`); `\begin{longtable}{@{}p{…}…@{}}` with repeated head (`\endhead`) and a continuation foot 「（续下页）」; printed numbers as `\hyperref[<label>]{\ref*{<label>}}`, pages as `\pageref*{<label>}`; tags with the tag macros; labels typeset with `\texttt{\detokenize{…}}` and allowed to break after hyphens (`\seqsplit`-like manual breaks inserted by the generator at `-`, no new package).

**TikZ graphs** (FIGURES_INDEX_v2 §4.9 is binding for styles; the node selection follows the task: per-part graphs of the **main theorems**).
- *Node selection.* Skeleton (`fig:appP-dependency`): the master-table statements, all `asm:` entries, the $R_{\rm cond}$ theorems of App M/N/O. Per part (`-b`…`-g`): the main theorems of the part = master-table statements of the part ∪ all `thm:` of the part; their direct premises inside the part; all assumptions in their premise sentences or `uses` (any owner); premises outside the part collapsed into one grey stub per unit (「第~$n$~章」, 「附录~X」). `-h`: the App M/N/O statements used by a chapter statement (the proof-delivery targets of `OUTLINE_appMNOPQ.md`) and their assumptions. At most **60 nodes** per figure: if exceeded, non-main premises with the smallest number of users are folded into 「+$k$」 stubs (deterministic: ties by book order). Edges between selected nodes = transitive reduction of the reachability relation restricted to the selected nodes; `asm:` → $R_{\rm cond}$ edges are never removed.
- *Layout.* Layers left→right by unit order (front, Ch1, …, Ch25, App A, …, App Q); inside a unit up to three sub-columns by longest-path depth within the unit; vertical order initialized by book order, then two barycentre sweeps (left→right with predecessors, right→left with successors), ties by book order; node separation ≥ 0.25 cm; coordinates computed in Python (3 decimals) and written as `\node[...] (n17) at (x,y) {...};`; if the drawing is wider than `\textwidth`, the whole `tikzpicture` is placed in `\rotatebox{90}{…}` inside the float.
- *Styles.* Node shapes and fills by type and tag (`OliveGreen!15`, `RoyalBlue!15`, `BurntOrange!15`, `Plum!15`, `BrickRed!15`); $R_{\rm cond}$ nodes dashed; assumptions dashed `orange!65!black` border, fill `orange!8`; [V] double border; pure-math lemmas white; node text `\tiny` short label (label without the type prefix); edges `gray!60`, `-{Stealth[length=2pt]}`, long edges (more than two layers) `opacity=0.5`; `asm:` → $R_{\rm cond}$ edges dashed `orange!65!black`; edges into the bottleneck assumptions of [A] process theorems (master-table 瓶颈 column) thick `red!60!black`; legend matrix in a corner.

**Budget control** (App P = 10 pp; measured from the `.aux`).

| level | change | expected App P size |
|---|---|---|
| 0 | full tables; status table one statement per row incl. definitions, remarks, examples | 14–16 pp |
| 1 | status table without definitions, remarks and examples (they have no grade; they remain in the registry); premises without definitions; `\scriptsize` | 11–13 pp |
| 2 | level 1 + status table two-up (two statements per row) + per-part graphs limited to 40 nodes, two per page | 9.5–11 pp |
| 3 | level 2 + `tab:appP-checks` replaced by one paragraph when all checks pass; `tab:appP-uses` limited to the master-table rows | 8.5–10 pp |

The integrator uses the lowest level that fits; the realistic outcome is level 2–3. If even level 3 exceeds 10 pp, the lead author decides between +2 pp for App P and moving the per-part graphs `-b`…`-g` to a single page of thumbnails (report).

## Appendix Q — 经核实的文献勘误 / Verified literature errata

`\label{app:appQ}` · `appQ_errata.tex` · **4 pp** · written after `verify/` completes · **[PENDING-VERIFY]** as a whole

**Purpose.** Record, in one place and in our conventions, the errors in the **published literature** that the book relies on correcting, each confirmed by the verification workflow. Literature errata are not new results (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §6.5 item 6) and are printed without emphasis. The reference document's errors are never listed (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §6.10); neither are slips in our own research files or frontier reports.

**Sections (4 pp).**

| § | label · title | content | pp |
|---|---|---|---|
| Q.1 | `sec:appQ-criteria` 收录标准 | inclusion rules (below), the verification procedure in one paragraph, the rule for author-corrected papers | 0.5 |
| Q.2 | `sec:appQ-errata` 勘误表 | `tab:appQ-errata` (class Q1); optional `tab:appQ-errata-b` (class Q2, only if the patch decides) | 2.5 |
| Q.3 | `sec:appQ-notes` 说明 | one short note per row where the table cell cannot hold the correct form or the consequence (formulas in our conventions, with VF IDs in `%` comments) | 0.75 |
| — | `rem:appQ-scope` | what App Q does not contain | 0.25 |

**Inclusion rules (binding; print in `sec:appQ-criteria` in Chinese).**
1. An item is printed only if the verification workflow recorded **two independent confirmations** (stage-1 and stage-2 records by independent verifiers) in `verify/`, and the patch cites the record paths.
2. Only class **Q1** items of WRITER_SPEC_v2 §6.13 / REFS_NOTES_v2 §3.5 (errors in published or archived sources that the book corrects). Class **Q2** items (corrected by the authors or superseded by the same group) are cited in their corrected form where used and listed in `tab:appQ-errata-b` only if the patch so decides; class **Q3** items (proof gaps, disputes between groups, convention hazards, errors reported by others) go to status paragraphs and remarks with attribution, never to App Q; E19 (withdrawn) is never printed; rule E33: for every cited paper with a published erratum the corrected version is used.
3. If the verification finds that the error was already pointed out in print, the row cites that source in the column 「后续文献中的状态」 and the book does not present the correction as its own.
4. Equation numbers refer to the version stated in the row (journal or arXiv version); where the two differ, both are given.

**Table schema** (`tab:appQ-errata`, and `tab:appQ-errata-b` without the verification column): 编号 | 来源（文献键 + 式/节） | 原文形式 | 正确形式（本书约定） | 核实（`verify/` 记录路径；独立检验） | 后续文献中的状态（作者已更正／他人已指出／未更正） | 本书依赖之处（标签）.

**Pending candidate list** (planned row IDs in order of first use in the book; the patch renumbers after removing failed items; stage-1 notes from `verify/claims/batch_E01_s1.md`, `batch_E07_s1.md`, `batch_E13_s1.md`, read 2026-10-10; stage 2 pending for all; E19–E36 stage 1 in progress).

| planned ID | claim | class | source as printed (bib key, place) | correct form / later literature | VF | where the book relies on it | stage-1 note |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Q.1 | E23 | Q1 (arXiv App. A; check the journal version) | `BecherSchwartz2008` App. A: spurious $C_F$ in $\Gamma_1$ | `KorchemskyRadyushkin1987` | VF-CONV-23, VF-SCET-16 | Ch5, Ch15, App B | pending |

## 08. Registry of the labels owned by App M–Q (format of `check_unit_v2.py`)

Only labels **owned** by App M–Q are listed (chapter-owned statements whose proofs are delivered here are in the proof-delivery tables and keep their chapter owners). Order: sections, then statements in file order (the order of this outline), then tables and figures. Tag column: [R, qualifier], [A], 数学 (pure mathematics, no tag), — (definitions, untagged remarks/examples, tables, figures, convention assumptions). Assumption tags are those of `ASSUMPTIONS.md`. The reserved [PENDING-VERIFY] slot labels are **not** registered (§09).

### AppM

| label | type | tag | 中文标题 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `sec:appM-overview` | sec | — | 概览 |
| `sec:appM-bphz` | sec | — | BPHZ 与正规乘积 |
| `sec:appM-chain` | sec | — | 链展开与 OPE 恒等式 |
| `sec:appM-remainder` | sec | — | 余项、唯一性与标度 |
| `sec:appM-massless` | sec | — | 无质量 QCD |
| `sec:appM-brst` | sec | — | BRST、规范不变算符与扭度 |
| `sec:appM-forward` | sec | — | 固定阶的全自旋求和 |
| `sec:appM-np` | sec | — | 非微扰层 |
| `sec:appM-status` | sec | — | 状态 |
| `def:appM-bphz` | def | — | 图、表观发散度、森林与 $R$ 运算 |
| `lem:appM-degree` | lem | 数学 | 表观发散度的外线公式 |
| `thm:appM-zimmermann` | thm | [R, R_PT] | Zimmermann 收敛定理（顶点可加的超减除） |
| `ex:appM-nonadditive` | ex | — | 非顶点可加的超减除会失败 |
| `thm:appM-weinberg` | thm | 数学 | Weinberg 定理（Hahn–Zimmermann 形式） |
| `cor:appM-weinberg-degree` | cor | 数学 | 渐近指数与子图发散度 |
| `rem:appM-forest-asymptotics` | rem | — | 森林公式不能逐项用 Weinberg 定理 |
| `def:appM-normal-product` | def | — | 正规乘积与冻结 $q$ 的双局域正规乘积 |
| `lem:appM-contraction` | lem | 数学 | 收缩与桥 |
| `lem:appM-chain-expansion` | lem | 数学 | 链展开恒等式 |
| `thm:appM-zimmermann-identity` | thm | [R, R_PT] | Zimmermann 恒等式 |
| `cor:appM-reduction` | cor | [R, R_PT] | 化到最小正规乘积 |
| `ex:appM-callan-symanzik` | ex | — | $\varphi^4$ 中的 Zimmermann 恒等式 |
| `lem:appM-routing` | lem | 数学 | 冻结 $q$ 时外动量分配的相容性 |
| `lem:appM-integrability` | lem | [R, R_PT] | 冻结 $q$ 正规乘积的良定义与链分解 |
| `lem:appM-contact` | lem | [R, R_PT] | 接触项 |
| `thm:appM-ope-identity` | thm | [R, R_PT] | 微扰论中的 OPE 恒等式（有质量模型） |
| `ex:appM-tree` | ex | — | 树图 OPE 就是 Taylor 展开 |
| `rem:appM-mechanism` | rem | — | 指数 $D-1-a$ 的启发式来源 |

## CHANGELOG

**2026-10-10 — v2 drafted (spec-v2 new-appendices drafter).**
1. New appendices M (OPE, 24 pp), N (precise Collins subtraction, 24 pp), O (LCOPT and the Glauber cancellation, 32 pp), P (formal blueprint, 10 pp), Q (verified errata, 4 pp) outlined at the precision of spec v1: every canonical statement with type, label, Chinese title, tag and qualifier, premise sentence, partonic/non-perturbative line, statement in formulas, `\uses`, proof plan (full/sketch) with literature, checks and VF IDs, error type.
2. Proof-delivery tables for the chapter-owned statements proved in App M (13 Ch7 statements), App N (Ch9, Ch10, Ch14, Ch19) and App O (Ch9, Ch12, Ch14, Ch18), each with the appendix lemmas that the chapter `\uses` must contain.
3. Corrections implemented (§00.5): ERR-S1 (strict-window residual is a truncation artefact; exact eikonal box and crossed box), ERR-S2 (pointer), ERR-S3 (fixed-order hypothesis replaced by `lem:appO-joint-localization`; consequence moved to `asm:appO-deformation`(d)), ERR-M24–M29, M31, M32, ERR-m23–m27; the S48 slip of our own analysis (Wilson-line directions) corrected.
4. Sharpened results (never marked new): `ex:appM-unreduced` (exact $-\kappa\pi^2/(2q^4)$), `prop:appN-triangle-exact` (exact remainder, no logarithm at next-to-leading power; Beta function for $C_S$), `lem:appO-joint-localization` (ordering weights; new localization estimate (b)), `prop:appO-ss-localize` (absolute bound).
5. New auxiliary statements: `lem:appM-routing`, `lem:appM-norm-bound`, `lem:appM-positivity` (with $Q^2$-smearing), `lem:appN-absorption`, `lem:appO-insertion-values`, `lem:appO-past-unitarity`, `def:appP-content-grade`.
6. Register decisions (with `ASSUMPTIONS.md`): (Z2) split off as `asm:appN-ir-regulator` (\tagC), used only by `thm:disall-renorm-compat` (content grade [C]); the process theorems use the DR route `asm:appN-multren`(Z3)(ii) (mass factorization); new entry `asm:appN-brst-nonlocal` (\tagA; lead-author decision flagged); `asm:appO-mixing`(c1) deleted; clash-free inner names (CU1)–(CU3), (V1)(V2), (WI1)(WI2), (GC1)–(GC3), (Ctr).
7. [PENDING-VERIFY] slots for the running extensions: X1a–X1f (App O), X2a–X2d (App N), X3a–X3e (App M), with planned statements, tags, evidence and decision rules; slot X1f and X3e added after inspecting the running scripts in `verify/extend/` (two-exchange spectator model with exact propagators; forward on-shell expansion theorem).
8. App P generator specified (parsing, data model, content grade, checks C-P1–C-P16 incl. "no forward edge" and "no dependency on a lower-graded statement without a grade drop", longtables, per-part TikZ graphs of the main theorems with ≤ 60 nodes, budget levels 0–3); App Q structure, inclusion rules and the pending candidate list keyed to WRITER_SPEC_v2 §6.13 (Q1/Q2/Q3) with the stage-1 notes of `verify/claims/`.
